# Reuse Checklist

## 首页筛选聚合与缓存

采用 bbingz 的 [PR #205](https://github.com/peach-mitao/peach/pull/205)，固定来源提交 `bbf5aac8e9562211c324c2019bff35888266df84`，许可证沿用本项目 AGPL-3.0-or-later。作品筛选集合先在独立子查询中求出，创作者、标签和演员按既有关系聚合，保留去重计数与标签可见性。没有选中标签时移除无效 `tag_match`，复用既有修订缓存、同键锁与跨连接写入失效机制，不在线改写 SQLite 统计或增加索引迁移。

顶部人物、厂牌的 `q_tops` 复用同一筛选方式：独立求出可用作品集合，再按实体聚合，保留代表作选择、种子抽样、续页和修订缓存。错误统计回归使用临时账本、冷缓存与新连接，按作品数量限制判定调用量，核对默认、JAV、已看及组合范围的输出等价。运行时不写 ledger，不改 SQLite 统计或索引。

## 混合媒体根与模型读取

馆藏采集复用 `platform.within_root` 的 SMB 根身份判断，本地路径保留字面归属比较，不逐影片访问挂载目录。STRM 是链接文件，其大小和推广名不作为远端影片的垃圾证据；垃圾队列读取时重新评分，既有人工决定与回收站记录保持原样。

YuNet 与 SFace 沿用既有模型哈希校验，Python 读取模型字节后交给 OpenCV 的 ONNX buffer 入口，避免 Windows 原生文件读取对非 ASCII 路径的限制。没有新增模型副本、依赖或路径重试。隔离验证用同一哈希的模型分别从普通路径与中文路径加载，并用合成数组执行检测和特征计算。

## 浏览器添加的本地人物资料

`/api/jav-browser/people` 复用现有专用令牌与浏览器权限检查、规范实体关联和男优排除判据。人物名只读本地账本与发现资料快照；查询单个番号时还可带上详情页的出演者名字，按既有 `resolve_entity` 先规范名后唯一别名匹配，括号里的别名一并尝试，未匹配的只返回名字。头像通过既有 `PreviewService.entity_image` 查找已安装文件，按 `avatar_focus` 的人脸记录沿用 `frontend/src/card-art/face-frame.ts` 的放大与夹持判据取景；已有 Pillow 将其在内存缩为 64px JPEG，不落新头像、不抓外站。未初始化发现资料服务时直接读取快照，避免初始化恢复外站任务。缺头像省略图片字段，缺人物信息返回空数组，接口失败与正常缺失分别反馈。用户脚本显示灰底首字母，不显示作品封面，无新增依赖。

## README Star 图

`scripts/render_star_history.py` 自行生成，只用标准库与 GitHub CLI，不新增依赖。数据来自 GitHub 原生公开 Star 历史：每周一条记录，其中 `days` 是逐日新增。脚本按日累计，横轴从第一个 Star 当天开始，到生成时刻为止；建库到第一个 Star 之间的空白期不画。曲线用单调三次插值（与 d3 `curveMonotoneX` 相同），不画出回落，也不越过下一天的累计值。画面保留蜜桃色霓虹描边、渐变填充、线性绝对值坐标和浅深双主题。脚本不补造缺失记录，也不把历史累计增加数当作完整取消 Star 的净变化账本。

此前复用 [Star Chart](https://github.com/leereilly/star-chart) v0.3（MIT，固定提交 `e7fa78bdf8f78da6c3feeb3a97b528fd97d62727`）。它只按周累计，不使用 `days` 字段，因此一周内的增速起落会被抹平：2026-10 的单周一万多个 Star 只能画成一级台阶或一段斜坡。调列数改变不了时间粒度，所以改为自行渲染。

独立 `Star History` 工作流每日生成 SVG，只在 `star-history` 分支保存产物，不触发主线测试或阻塞发版。静态 SVG 发布思路参考 SakuraMedia；未复制其受 PolyForm Noncommercial 约束的生成脚本。

## 复核字段筛选与文本差异

复核文本差异复用 [jsdiff](https://github.com/kpdecker/jsdiff) 的 `diffChars`，前端精确固定 `diff@9.0.0`（BSD-3-Clause，自带类型），按 Unicode 码点比较并仅展示当前文本中的差异片段。当前值和候选相互对照，尚无当前值时对照其他来源；来源依据只比较同名字段。Peach 保留参照选择、语义高亮与字段筛选，不自行实现通用差异算法；原始候选值、提交契约和 ledger 均不改变。

## 任务历史关联清理

采用 RAAMENN 的 [PR #151](https://github.com/peach-mitao/peach/pull/151)，固定来源提交 `eb5f6b49210ed330c767166ea204103a8193250a`，许可证沿用本项目 AGPL-3.0-or-later。复用 SQLite 的 `SET NULL` 语义和删除触发器，在外键开关两种连接下维护父任务与根任务关联。当前迁移编号为 `0049`，只清空不存在的任务引用；任务内容、有效关联、活跃后继和媒体记录保留。隔离回归覆盖旧库升级、重放、批量删除回滚及跨任务类型后继，无新增依赖。

## 馆藏任务取消、范围与问题导出

取消复用 `ProcessingControl` 的本机原子记录、安全停点与 `BackgroundJob` 结算；`stopping` 等待在途工作退出，`cancelled` 保留结果并阻止自动恢复。安装暂停与用户暂停沿用原语义。新影片范围复用 `ScanResult.new_ids`、`filelock` 和原子 JSON，接回旧扫描检查点；启动时固定为已有的 `asset_ids` 请求，恢复不重新计算范围。退场资产仅退出当前重试集合，来源离线不按文件消失处理；归档只保存提示选择，不删除问题原文或任务历史。

问题导出采用 kevin552216 的 [PR #172](https://github.com/peach-mitao/peach/pull/172) 中 `issue-export.ts` 的分页 JSON 下载思路，固定来源 head `d1803c28e0b1a2a086d9756a67b0ec313387db29`，AGPL-3.0-or-later。Peach 增加日志内容摘要、总数和页偏移校验，拒绝同任务恢复时混合两份记录；旧完整 `issues` 数组可分页读取，缺失或损坏日志不伪装成空清单。复用既有 `useAction`、AbortSignal 和 Blob 下载，不采用该 PR 的单片重抓、字段保护和来源策略改动，无新增依赖。

状态文件写入沿用 `fsutil.atomic_write_text`。补入 FriendlyPasser 的 [PR #120](https://github.com/peach-mitao/peach/pull/120) 固定提交 `b586ff907aa01b48432e51b6101b7ce4bc7f8126` 的 Windows 读句柄验证，检查实际 WinError、读者关闭、完整 JSON 发布和临时文件清理；不增加另一套保存策略。

## 发布依赖接管来源

依赖清单复用 Dependabot 的独立 PR 差集，版本与完整依赖图由当前清单和锁文件确定。相邻修改保留主线消费者并重算锁；npm vendor 与前端包使用项目入口重建，许可证和来源记录随资源分发。接管器分别识别应用与 amane 桥的清单，并以实际接管提交确认完成；普通 PR 引用不构成采用。

注册表稳定版本检查补充 FastAPI、Starlette、tzlocal、Sentry 构建依赖和 Python 镜像的更新。Pillow 的兼容与安全说明按[官方发行说明](https://pillow.readthedocs.io/en/stable/releasenotes/13.0.0.html)核对；运行依赖 amane 固定官方稳定版，并按[上游复核](reference-snapshots/upstream-review.md)适配登录失败分类。更新只替换代码、运行环境与构建资源；既有资料继续遵守原字段保护与升级修复契约。

源码与 wheel 的桥安装回执绑定完整依赖锁的 SHA-256；缺少标识或标识不符时，复用现有安装锁和 `uv sync --locked` 重建工具环境，并在离线类加载自检通过后写回执。同一 amane revision 的传递依赖升级也能生效；随包运行环境继续由构建入口按锁生成，不重置媒体、配置或资料。

主应用与独立桥环境共同固定 `curl_cffi 0.17.0`（MIT），采用[官方安全发行说明](https://github.com/lexiforest/curl_cffi/releases/tag/v0.17.0)中的内置 Brotli 1.2.1、证书库及 Windows 撤销列表修正。原生传输在测试中读取实际 libcurl 版本，核对内置 Brotli 的安全版本；Python 的独立 Brotli 包与 libcurl 自带的解码器分别验收。Peach 沿用现有代理、指纹、TLS 和来源边界，不引入 WebSocket 或账户能力。

## 全屏视频比例

采用 simon1561 的 [PR #155](https://github.com/peach-mitao/peach/pull/155)，固定来源提交 `7ee5f2d6e0da03983fe943cb8179bee69c4f5639`，许可沿用本项目 AGPL-3.0-or-later。全屏适配复用现有 CSS `object-fit: contain`，原生全屏、播放器全屏类、属性与 full-window 回退使用同一规则；页面外框、控件和媒体管线保持既有契约，无新增依赖。

社区浏览器用例核对源码及构建样式，在 4:3、16:9 与手机竖屏下检查全屏及退出状态。Windows 由现有完整浏览器入口执行，macOS 的独立入口只加载画面比例用例，不重复运行 Windows 那一份。代码及样式资源替换即可生效，无存量数据转换；视口模拟不代替折叠屏实机验收。

## 叶子证书密钥标识与存量补签

采用 peach-patcher 的 [PR #177](https://github.com/peach-mitao/peach/pull/177)，固定来源提交 `62927d4b975bdad3420ef214bc9330382a4233d6`，许可证沿用本项目 AGPL-3.0-or-later。运行期与两个 TLS 设置脚本复用 OpenSSL 的 [`subjectKeyIdentifier` 和 `authorityKeyIdentifier`](https://docs.openssl.org/3.0/man5/x509v3_config/#authority-key-identifier) 扩展，显式签入叶子证书；标准库 [`ssl.create_default_context`](https://docs.python.org/3.14/library/ssl.html#ssl.create_default_context) 的严格验证策略保持启用。现有签发工具随运行环境提供，无新增依赖。

`certs.reissue_reason` 将缺少 AKI 的叶子证书交给已有补签流程，CA 和已安装信任保持原样；合格叶子沿用 SAN 与到期判据。存量回归采用临时 CA、缺 AKI 的有效叶子、真实严格 TLS 握手和补签后重复检查，核对 CA 保留及叶子备份。社区用例按 OpenSSL 与 LibreSSL 的缺标识签发行为适配，不用跳过测试代替存量验证。未采用 mkcert 或新增 X.509 编码库，现有工具已覆盖签发能力，Peach 仅保留 CA 生命周期和补签策略。

SAN 读取使用两种工具均支持的 `x509 -text`；POSIX 设置脚本允许仅签发本机名称和回环地址，并以成功状态结束。两种签发脚本通过实际执行与严格握手回归验证，不依赖扩展配置的源码字串断言。

## 测试规划与阶段计时

`scripts/test_resume.py` 的 `ImpactPlan` 在一次恢复规划内缓存域集合、测试导入图与路径影响；调用方用当前快照创建计划，检查点输入改变时重新计算。`scripts/test_runner.py` 的 `local_batches` 统一快速检查、普通分片和内部并行容器的执行顺序，`scripts/test_run_progress.py` 的 `phase` 将阶段墙钟写入正式测试记录。沿用标准库与现有单项证明协议，不改变证明有效期、环境身份或完整批次要求。使用范围与计时口径见 [测试与 CI](TESTING.md)。

## 首次设置分步框架

首次设置、已有数据恢复与完成态共用 `frontend/src/react/pages/setup/setup-scene.tsx` 的分栏框架。媒体库卡片复用配置页的图标、来源色、处理方式及目录控件，访问范围使用 `ConfigurationChoice`。场景布局参考和来源见 [取证记录](reference-snapshots/arion-onboarding.md)，设备素材见 [素材说明](../resources/setup/README.md)。没有新增运行时依赖；提交、校验和源码文案编辑沿用现有入口。步骤标题、场景说明和操作区随步骤独立挂载，防止文案草稿映射串到其他步骤。

独立页面的编辑工具条共用 `components/selection-dock.css` 与 `batch-dock/batch-dock.css`，由 `pages/copy-editor-page.css` 将页面的 BoardUI token 对应到工具条 token，并为页面底部留出空间。编辑器核心及官网、演示站同步产物不涉及本次样式接入。

## 扫描路径冲突隔离

路径归组复用 Python 3.14 标准库的 [`PureWindowsPath`](https://docs.python.org/3.14/library/pathlib.html#general-properties) 与现有大小写口径，入库复用 SQLite 的 [UPSERT](https://www.sqlite.org/lang_upsert.html)、扫描检查点和单路径摄取入口，未新增依赖。两者均由现有运行时携带，许可与版本沿用运行时声明。路径相等不决定个人记录归属，Peach 负责将歧义组交给现有任务问题清单，保留每条身份及处置，只更新实际观察到的文件时间；不另建路径解析器或自动合并器。

隔离回归使用临时账本与目录，覆盖大小写和斜杠变体、个人记录保留、缺失文件、多根目录、断点配对和自动发现。真实 ledger 未用于修复演练，部署与报告者验收分别记录。

## 媒体库查询范围与详情类型

React 与应用层的 GET 入口共用 `frontend/src/core/library-scope.ts`：目录和筛选读取当前媒体库，调用方显式提供的 `library` 参数优先，空值表示全部媒体库。查询参数识别复用浏览器原生 `URLSearchParams`，不增加依赖或持久缓存。媒体库选择沿用整页导航，详情按资产 ID 查询并返回账本中的 `medium`，不从文件扩展名推断类型。两项契约随代码与构建资源替换生效，无需数据转换。

## 升级自动处理与更新提示

本地补修复用 `catalog_rules` 的用户识别规则、`field_owners.write_owned_fields`、r18 文字修复与复合别名拆分判据。两个维护脚本调用同一领域模块。结构迁移、SQLite 备份、文件锁和原子回执继续使用项目现有入口；后台复用 `TaskRunStore`、`FollowupRunner`、`Attempts`、来源冷却和影片处理检查点，未新增运行依赖。

派生图复用现有生成锁与原子替换，按规则、来源身份及文件代际记录签名。版本快照复用已有发行版检查与独立包安装状态；页面提示使用 BoardUI 和项目 Banner，文案编辑使用现有源码保存入口。脚本在独立维护源构建后经正式导出器同步，其元数据检查复用脚本管理器 API 和可信发布入口。运行方式与故障恢复见[升级后的自动处理](OPERATIONS.md#升级后的自动处理)，决策见 [ADR-0126](adr/0126-upgrade-effective-repairs.md)。

## 正式测试记录与中断恢复

交付预检复用已有的提交主题、共同署名与 README 影响检查，不新增规则解释器。合入前的 PR 身份复用 [GitHub CLI 的 `gh pr view`](https://cli.github.com/manual/gh_pr_view)，使用工作树仓库上下文和原生 JSON 字段；Peach 只校验开放状态、精确提交及目标分支，不引入 GitHub SDK 或另一套凭据存储。临时 Git 工作树回归覆盖未取得 PR、旧提交、其他目标、远端查询期间内容变化及成功合并回执。

测试结果继续复用 Python ≥3.14 标准库 `unittest`、`hashlib`、`json` 和项目的内容快照、影响域映射与外部测试结果。互斥复用既有 `filelock 4.2.0`，分片退出后回收其进程树复用开发依赖 `psutil 7.2.2`；版本与许可沿用锁定依赖及第三方许可清单，不新增运行时依赖。Peach 保留输入身份、逐文件覆盖、完成报告、批次来源和 `ready`／`integrate` 验收规则；串行、并行、浏览器与 Vitest 使用同一个恢复模型。

CI 跨作业恢复使用 GitLab Runner 19.5 的原生 [缓存](https://docs.gitlab.com/ci/caching/) 与 [`cache:when`](https://docs.gitlab.com/ci/yaml/#cachewhen)，保存正式证明 JSON，按作业、分支及保护状态隔离。缓存可缺失，Peach 仍负责内容、环境、时效与全量批次校验；不引入缓存 SDK 或独立证明存储。GitLab 克隆地址使用标准库 `urllib.parse` 保留仓库身份并规范化认证值，作业令牌及其密码别名按存在性参与环境身份。

候选比较包括官方 [pytest 缓存文档](https://doc.pytest.org/en/stable/how-to/cache.html) 与 [Turborepo 缓存文档](https://turborepo.dev/docs/crafting-your-repository/caching)。pytest 的失败项选择不能替代当前内容绑定的成功覆盖，不迁移测试框架；Turborepo 的任务缓存不能直接接管 Python 用例结果及集成门槛，不引入其运行时或源码。外部源码版本与许可证未作为采用依据，本次未复制这些项目的实现。

临时输入实证覆盖跨工作树运行时、安装锁 JSON、CI 运行信息、多域混合失败及多份记录搜索；行为回归覆盖失败与中断恢复、旧失败阻止成功恢复、原时间继承、外部文件完成证明、Python 容器后置检查及全量来源约束。运行时、实际依赖、共享夹具、未知输入、执行模式、过期或未来时间变化仍使相关证明失效。测试设施不读写真实 ledger。

## 媒体目录与整理路径映射

PikPak 整理复用项目已有 `PushDiscoverySettings`、`CloudPrefix` 与 `map_cloud_path`，与推送发现共用配置，不引入第二份盘符或 CloudDrive 路径表。CloudDrive RPC 继续使用现有固定版本的 gRPC／protobuf；路径归属与账号内 WebDAV 目录核验属于 Peach 的写入边界。不同盘符、子目录、云端名称、越界与冲突由临时夹具和模拟 RPC 验证，不触碰真实账号或账本。

Windows 系统卷通过系统原生 `GetWindowsDirectoryW` 获取，其他平台使用根卷。此改动没有新增第三方依赖；现有依赖版本与许可继续以 `pyproject.toml`、`uv.lock` 和下文登记为准。

## 文件名包装与补充回归

论坛 URL 包装与明确 BD Remux 尾部番号参考 [PR #123](https://github.com/peach-mitao/peach/pull/123)，固定 revision 为 `edde1fffb2c4690b7b78224dbd70dd7dfed786b3`，沿用本仓库 AGPL-3.0-or-later 许可。包装只解开一层，内部重用现有文件名与用户识别规则；Remux 的括号番号必须是唯一发行身份。普通处理后缀、日期分隔符和「番号＋片名」继续由既有解析器处理，不增加另一套处理词表或递归解析。

普通 HTTP 的浏览器和组件回归采用 [PR #119](https://github.com/peach-mitao/peach/pull/119) 的 `90d4972f9edf8b84dd866869a1a562cc7d1d24eb`；状态保存的短暂拒绝、持续拒绝和并发完整性回归采用 [PR #120](https://github.com/peach-mitao/peach/pull/120) 的 `b586ff907aa01b48432e51b6101b7ce4bc7f8126`。两者保留原作者署名，沿用同一仓库许可。正式随机标识继续复用 `core/random-id`；状态写入继续复用 `fsutil.atomic_write_text` 的同目录随机临时文件、十秒重试与失败清理，不新增依赖或真实 ledger 写入。

## SMB 媒体读写

采用 [smbprotocol 1.17.0](https://github.com/jborean93/smbprotocol/tree/v1.17.0)（MIT，Python ≥3.10，上游测试包含 Python 3.14 与 Windows、macOS、Linux）。固定传递版本见 `uv.lock`；库 wheel 约 128 kB，另有 cryptography、pyspnego 和平台认证依赖。`opensrc path smbprotocol` 未取得源码，改为核对固定 tag 的 `_pool.py`、`_io.py`、`open.py`、`connection.py`。

复用认证、协议消息与解析器、目录分页和偏移读写，以及 `SMBFileTransaction`、`set_info` 和 `FileRenameInformation`／`FileDispositionInformation`／`FileEndOfFileInformation`。Peach 保留固定根目录授权、绝对请求期限、取消、凭据隔离与媒体会话；整理和永久删除沿用既有计划、账本事务与失败恢复。目录条目的 `stat()` 会额外发请求，扫描消费枚举元数据；传输在库响应事件上施加期限，超时取消并断开独占连接。SDK 认证日志保持 WARNING，DFS 与重解析点拒绝。写入不覆盖改名目标、不跨声明根，写权限由共享服务器判定；工具用的回环 HTTP 仍只读。

未采用 fsspec，其 SMB 底层仍是 smbprotocol，无法替代媒体工具输入和取消；pysmb 的 SMB1/2 能力不覆盖同等 SMB3 需求。FFmpeg 原生 SMB 依赖二进制构建，本机工具没有列出该协议，因此使用 HTTP Range 回环桥。隔离回归覆盖认证、读取、并发偏移、配置与扫描配对；实际 FFmpeg 通过合成视频的尾部索引和 seek 抽帧。真实 NAS 吞吐和兼容性未取得，无真实 ledger 写入。

## 第三方标识的发行边界

`resources/marks/` 保存用于实体身份展示的内置标识，来源记录与文件哈希见同目录 `manifest.json`。来源可追溯不等于取得图片版权或商标授权，图片也不因与应用同仓库就自动适用 AGPL。

厂牌与事务所标识按 ADR-0026 随应用分发，包括裸文件、`icon` 和 `logo` 变体；`scripts/sync_brand_marks.py` 同步文件与来源记录。本机缓存、实体来源链接和缺图时的文字／首字回退由既有预览服务处理。

## GitLab 与 R2 发布

Windows 构建路径解析复用 PowerShell 7 的原生 [`GetUnresolvedProviderPathFromPSPath`](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/dotnet/api/system.management.automation.pathintrinsics.getunresolvedproviderpathfrompspath)；以当前 PowerShell 工作位置解析尚未创建的文件，无新增依赖或安装体积。安装更新验收使用 GitLab 官方[预定义变量](https://docs.gitlab.com/ci/variables/predefined_variables/)识别受保护版本标签及指定作业，沿用 Windows 注册表的既有安装检查。离线 PowerShell 回归覆盖进程工作目录与脚本工作位置不同的输入；主机检查覆盖受保护作业、未知作业与既有安装登记。

GitLab API、CI 认证和 Release 创建复用官方 [glab 1.124.0](https://gitlab.com/gitlab-org/cli)（MIT，维护中）；GitLab CI 的发布写入使用短期 `CI_JOB_TOKEN` 与官方 CI 自动登录。R2 的 S3 上传复用 [rclone 1.75.2](https://rclone.org/)（MIT，维护中），使用单桶对象读写凭据；版本文件不可覆盖，并由独立 HTTPS 下载校验 SHA-256。两者都是构建期 CLI，不进入应用运行时依赖。

演练产物晋级复用上述传输和 GitLab 作业 API；GitHub 通过官方 [Artifacts REST API](https://docs.github.com/en/rest/actions/artifacts)读取服务端摘要，再由标准库 `urllib.request`、`hashlib` 和 `zipfile` 下载、核验与展开。Peach 负责同一提交、版本、平台和验证任务的身份契约，以及已验收镜像的 image ID。媒体缓存沿用固定源码构建器，缓存二进制与对应源码，按实际构建输入和工具链核对，不新增缓存 SDK。协议与回收边界见 [ADR-0127](adr/0127-verified-release-artifact-promotion.md)。

GitGud 双存储副本复用同一版 glab 的原生 `packages upload` 与官方 [Packages API](https://docs.gitlab.com/api/packages/)，不另写认证或上传协议。Peach 只核对版本、文件名、大小与 `file_sha256`，复用相同内容并拒绝覆盖冲突；登记未完成最多检查三次。R2 下载表与 GitGud 备用资产链接使用不同的 Release 路径。未新增运行时依赖，离线回归覆盖首次上传、重复执行、内容冲突、未登记终止与双来源链接。

更新与轻量安装器共用 `standalone_update.download`、既有 HTTPX 和内置工具清单；GitHub 与 R2 可切换到同版本、同文件名，每个来源分别按同一份大小与 SHA-256 校验。新安装与同版本更新记录优先 GitHub，R2 保留灾备；工具下载不再另包一层来源重试。发布后空缓存验收复用 `smoke_split_update.py` 与实际冻结程序的 `--installer-tools` 入口，不新增下载器或依赖。

`fetch_release_tools.py` 从固定版本官方发行站读取 SHA-256 清单，仅提取指定二进制。Python 标准库负责制品清单、版本与提交判据，不另实现 GitLab 认证、S3 签名或多段上传协议。Windows 与 macOS 的 CI 入口复用既有测试、构建和独立制品验收脚本。下载后端及费用边界见[运维说明](OPERATIONS.md#gitlab-发布与下载后端)。

CI 大产物中转复用相同的 rclone 1.75.2 与官方 [R2 S3 适配](https://developers.cloudflare.com/r2/examples/rclone/)，覆盖 Linux amd64、macOS arm64/x86_64 与 Windows amd64；MIT 许可，仅构建期使用。传输、校验和多段上传由 rclone 负责，Peach 只维护流水线身份、输出路径白名单、SHA-256 清单与桶容量预算。macOS 只传 DMG 和构建清单，展开应用保留在构建机器。GitLab job artifacts 只传 JSON 清单；Generic Package 的单文件配额不能保证前置代理接受同等大小的请求。官方 rclone 归档名称与校验清单按实际输入核验，大文件往返使用独立临时仓库验证，不将传输测试当作正式制品验收。无新增 Python 依赖。源码获取复用共享 User-Agent 与 Peach HTTPX 客户端，针对连接中断、超时及暂时性 HTTP 错误最多尝试三次，完成 SHA-256 校验后才登记缓存；可独立构建的对应源码包使用标准库 urllib，包含共享请求标识及其许可证。

细粒度凭据缺少公开 CI 元数据的读取权限时，发版查询可在官方 API 上进行一次匿名 GET，复用 HTTPX、Peach 代理与项目共用 User-Agent；不扩大凭据权限，也不携带本机认证头。私有资源仍须通过服务端权限检查。

外部 HTTP 请求的连接策略复用 `peach_proxy.client_options`，客户端使用固定的 HTTPX 0.28.1（BSD-3-Clause）与 curl_cffi 0.16.3（MIT），没有新增依赖。共享 `HttpxTransport`、浏览器指纹传输、字幕、PikPak、更新、模型与维护脚本使用同一策略；libcurl 通过 `NOPROXY` 明确表达直连及自定义代理，避免继承相反的环境例外。Sentry 2.72.0 的事件、限流与回执仍由 SDK 维护，最终请求复用 HTTPX，保持测试禁发、无重试和无重定向。临时本机代理验证两种传输的实际请求路径；下载大小、SHA-256、重试次数和独立源码归档继续由行为回归核对。无需引入额外代理库或全局改写第三方网络函数。来源显式直连、网盘挂载、本机控制接口和独立工具的边界见[来源连接设置](SOURCING.md)。

npm 安装沿用 Node.js 24 LTS 附带的官方 [`npm ci`](https://docs.npmjs.com/cli/v11/commands/npm-ci/)，锁文件与完整性校验由 npm 维护。`npm_ci.py` 复用既有 `npm_audit.py` 的有限重试方式及 Python 3.14 标准库 `subprocess`（PSF 许可，无额外安装体积），只为明确的 `ECONNRESET`、`ETIMEDOUT`、`EAI_AGAIN` 下载错误重试，最多三次；锁文件、完整性与生命周期错误保留失败。Linux、Windows、macOS CI 和 Windows 打包入口共用此策略，不重试测试失败、不变更包版本。真实锁定安装与注入失败回归覆盖成功、重试耗尽和非网络错误，无新增应用依赖、生产入口或账本改动。

## 真实样本界面预览

用户验收预览复用项目记忆登记的真实账本小样本和已有缓存。启动入口强制调用 `scripts/check_ui_preview.py`，使用标准库 SQLite 的只读连接核对作品、实体、关联和来源清单，产出样本内容摘要；通用契约见 `docs/UI_PREVIEW.md`。核验与图像查看分别记录，隔离自动化测试夹具保留在测试范围内。没有新增依赖或生产数据写入。

## 采集磁盘预估

采集磁盘预估复用 `ProcessingTraffic`、`TrafficPlan` 的待执行与完成范围，以及 `typical_size` 的本地缓存采样。`processing_storage` 仅用 Python 标准库 `shutil.disk_usage` 和文件系统设备编号合并同盘、分别核对跨盘容量；输出读数由现有 `TrafficProgress` 同时用于启动弹窗、运行任务卡和任务通知。网盘挂载缓存保持未观测状态，不把流量数直接算成最终磁盘产物。无新增依赖。

## 状态与同步弹窗

全站滚动区通过 `observeScrollRegions` 按实际 `overflow` 接入现有 `attachOverlayScrollbar`，动态新增子树、样式启用和响应式布局复用同一入口；样式读取与轨道写入分批，行内样式变化只复核当前元素。普通内容区使用同级轨道，定位浮层与原生顶层内的轨道保持在自身层级并补偿滚动位置。组件卸载释放监听、观察器及轨道。横向手势导航保留 `wireHorizontalScroller` 的拖动和渐隐。路由冒烟统一检查所有实际溢出的滚动区，搜索回归覆盖滑块拖动、滚轮、关闭重开和轨道数量。没有新增依赖。

来源筛选与所有表格的显示列弹层共用 `SelectionPopover`，组合现有 React Aria `Popover`、`MenuDialog`、BoardUI `Checkbox` 和按钮。宽度由选项和操作组的内容决定，并受视口上限约束；长选项在限宽内折行。选项靠近触发器，批量操作根据 React Aria 的实际展开方向放在远端，DOM 顺序随之调整。`SelectionActions` 区分短操作并排与长操作铺满整行；来源筛选附带箭头的 `LinkButton` 凭据链接，列设置保留显示全部和默认显示。复核作品样本的全选、清空也复用同一操作条，保留计数及禁用条件。颜色配色器与人物说明浮层的交互含义不同，继续使用各自组件。没有新增依赖。

关注管理使用「关注列表」「添加关注」两个页签。列表复用 `FeedSources` 管理 JAV 订阅，添加页复用 `AddFeed` 的演员卡查询、用户点选与 `follow-name` 写入契约；共享 `FEEDS_KEY` 缓存，开关、移除及拉取各走原接口。来源筛选顶部统一提供凭据设置链接。JavDB 登入页判据复用 `javdb.LOGIN`，主页面与单体作品补拉共用解析入口。脚本检测在桥接文档加载后等待同源回报，加载与回报均有时限；权限指引依据 [Tampermonkey 官方 FAQ](https://www.tampermonkey.net/faq.php#Q209)。没有新增依赖。

连接状态复用 BoardUI `StatusDot` 与 `ConnectionStatus`，圆点和文字保持同一色系；成功为绿、检测中为蓝、离线或待配对为黄、失败为红、未知为灰。稳定任务结果和配置状态复用 `Chip`，由 `StatusLabel` 统一成功、进行、警告、失败和中性状态。暂停状态优先于历史错误；未经检查的来源保持未检查。用户脚本检测与功能启用分开呈现，仍由同源桥接协议核对版本、nonce 和配对回报。HTTP 状态颜色仅表示本次访问结果，删除仍以服务端确认失效的清单为准。

`formModal` 与 `confirmModal` 共用正文覆盖式滚动条，标题及底部操作固定；启动预估的来源明细跟随弹窗正文滚动，运行通知的明细维持独立限高。全部沿用已有组件、主题 token 和依赖。

## 元数据标题原文

持久元数据候选复用 `metadata.extract_peach_fields` 的原文取值和 `metadata_auto_apply` 的统一写入入口。r18 的纯拉丁字母标题需由同一作品快照的原文侧精确印证；缺少快照或只有英文译文时不进入自动结算，人工批准入口同样拒收。来源原文中的拉丁字母片名保留，不按字符集自动翻译。无新增依赖。

## 用户脚本的本机取证与进度

用户脚本的请求错误复用 Tampermonkey GM 存储与 `GM_xmlhttpRequest`，服务端复用 `error_reporting.local_event` 的脱敏与 `support_bundle` 的日志导出。独立的 `userscript` stage 仅写本机；受限字段、体积、频率和去重属于 Peach 协议策略，不接入外部遥测 SDK，不增加依赖。队列锁只保护内存与本机状态，网盘提交在锁外执行，提交幂等仍由现有 DownloadService 的共享提交锁处理。

脚本进度由 DownloadStore 的真实任务投影提供，复用 `STATE_LABELS` 与 `POSTPROCESS_LABELS`，不回传磁力、路径或网盘凭据。整体进度组合生产 `frontend/src/react/components/progress.tsx` 的 SVG、6px 高度、100 份比例和刻度，与 `library-processing/library-processing-notice.tsx` 的完成标记和当前阶段语义；BoardUI 明暗主题配色为同许可快照，固定来源为 Peach `44c347573e47cdd3b2294d35719f363ee5305246`。下载段仅填充实际百分比，其他段按里程碑推进；脚本保持零运行依赖，不嵌入 React。

## 浏览器错误的构建映射

前端复用官方 [`@sentry/vite-plugin` 11.8.0](https://github.com/getsentry/sentry-javascript-bundler-plugins)（MIT，维护中，Node ≥23.2，与项目 Node 24 和 Vite 8 兼容）生成确定性 Debug ID 与 hidden source maps；[`@sentry/cli` 3.8.0](https://github.com/getsentry/sentry-cli)（FSL-1.1-MIT）仅用于构建期本地定位和显式上传。两者精确固定在前端开发依赖，纳入现有 Dependabot；不进入 Python 运行时或独立包依赖。

`frontend/scripts/sentry-build.ts` 只保留 Peach 的公开／私有目录边界与产物匹配检查。官方插件禁用遥测、自动上传与 release 注入，普通构建不需要凭据；私有目录保存 JS/CSS/maps，公开目录只复制四个正式 JS/CSS。显式上传复用官方 CLI，先核对分发字节和 map 身份。没有实现自定义 source map 编解码器或上传协议，也没有为此引入浏览器 SDK、页面回放或性能采集。

回归使用真实 Vite 构建与官方 CLI 本地解析 source map，验证源码文件和行号；相同输入两次构建的文件字节一致，Debug ID 与 map 对应，映射源只使用相对路径。构建体积由 Vite 输出，source maps 只增加私有构建产物，不增加页面分发体积。字段与发布配置见[诊断核验](DIAGNOSTICS.md#可选远程诊断)；真实接收端符号化独立验收。
## 索引标题栏

标签、艺人、卖家、厂牌与事务所共用 `IndexPage`、现有 BoardUI Input／Button、收藏入口和版式分段器。标题与操作保留固定宽度，搜索框使用原生 Flex 的剩余空间；窄屏名册计数独占次行。首屏占位复用 `islandSearchInput`、`islandButton` 与相同布局规则，标签管理入口也预留真实尺寸。浏览器几何回归覆盖 320px 至桌面、断点两侧，以及本地／在线和管理模式。没有新增依赖、API 或 ledger 写入。

## 作品卡片与推荐理由

目录、实体作品区、竖屏条与接着看复用 `MediaCard`。多列网格统一卡片高度，`MediaGrid` 沿用现有 `ResizeObserver` 和 `gridTemplateColumns` 测量方式，单列按实际内容定高。元信息保持一行，容器查询按扣除头像后的文字区宽度决定日期、共演人数与体积的显隐。普通卡片保留最多五枚共演头像，常态按实际紧叠宽度占位，在 45px 至 109px 之间随卡宽压缩。悬停与键盘聚焦时头像浮于文字上方展开并抬起对应头像，不预留展开空间，标题列位置保持稳定；占位文字始终居中，图片沿用原有头像取景。接着看和竖屏条保留两枚头像，新作卡片复用同一几何。稍后看复用已有动作回调、撤销回执与中性填充 token，同步按钮和卡片底色，不渲染状态色块及观看次数。

接着看的推荐理由独占两行，完整内容使用已安装的 React Aria Components 1.22.1（Apache-2.0）`Popover`／`Dialog` 与现有 BoardUI 浮层样式展示，触屏、键盘与鼠标共用同一入口。没有新增依赖、API、迁移或 ledger 写入。

## 空状态与操作入口

React 页面复用 `EmptyState`，HTML 页面复用 `emptyStateHtml`。图标统一为 32px、图标框为 54px；标准区域最小高度为 320px，紧凑区域为 240px，字体、背景和边框共用。统计页上下区域采用标准尺寸。空状态按原因提供添加内容、清除筛选、重试或返回浏览等操作；正常完成状态不增加操作。

已有添加表单使用 `EmptyFocusButton` 聚焦原输入框。筛选清除保留当前页面、版式和本地或在线范围。关注检查复用 `useBackgroundJob` 与 `startCheck`；实体页仅提交该实体绑定且有效的来源 ID，不以空 ID 列表发起全量检查。没有新增依赖或自动采集任务。

同页页签切换调用页面已有状态与地址回调。站内锚点链接由 `navigationLinkPath` 按 `routeMetaOf` 的路由登记统一交给客户端导航；下载、外站、新窗口、修饰键及普通页内锚点保留浏览器行为。不按按钮文字或某条固定地址单独拦截。

## 标签身份与管理

标签浏览与管理复用 `DataTableFooter` 和既有页码算法，每页默认 100 个，可选 50、100、200 个；`useTagPagination` 统一筛选重置与页码边界。搜索输入复用 `IndexSearch`，标签正文与页脚的占位复用 `tagSkeletonHtml`。本地和在线详情的选择器通过 `useOverlayScrollbar` 接入既有覆盖轨道，搜索标题与滚动正文分层，不新增依赖。

标签复用 `entities.merge_entity`、实体别名、兼容投影、SQLite 事务及在线备份。字符宽度归一使用 Python 标准库 `unicodedata.normalize('NFKC', ...)`；同义词和用户别名属于 Peach 的身份规则。当前树、标签改名脚本与 Git 历史提供了关系迁移能力，无需引入通用模糊匹配库；语义近似不足以自动合并。隔离用例覆盖重叠关系、来源证据、隐藏偏好、旧名入库、过期计划和备份。

页面复用已安装的 React Aria 1.22.1、BoardUI Button/Input/Select、标签云和选择操作条。编辑字段沿用同步任务表单的 React portal 接法，外壳共用 `formModal` 与 `confirmModal`，按顺序打开。没有新增依赖。

在线标签复用 `profiles.read_settings` 与 `profile.settings_json` 保存本机名称、来源词成员和隐藏状态；账本版本触发器负责缓存失效，无新增表。读取投影复用 `_follow_tag_index` 的发布组计数和来源类型，标签索引、关注卡片、详情、筛选项与旧名查询共用 `OnlineTagPolicy`，不改写来源 metadata。合并限制在相同来源类型；事务、影响范围预览、过期计划和备份复用标签管理端点。归类属于 Peach 的用户决定，不引入模糊匹配或外部同义词服务。

在线标签收藏复用 `FavoriteButton`、`libraryShortcuts` 设置和既有读写端点，以 `scope=online` 与稳定来源词键区分本地实体；收藏快照经在线归类解析并去重。收藏面板沿用标签分类，以「在线标签」标明去处，链接打开 `/follow` 标签筛选；馆藏实体收藏快照继续供研究和实体标注使用。未新增依赖或存储表。

在线手动标签与内容屏蔽复用 `OnlineTagPolicy`、`profile.settings_json`、`FollowStore.group`、现有标签预览确认端点及账本版本缓存。手动标签以来源和内容编号定位，在读取副本中叠加；来源 metadata 保持独立。内容屏蔽在分页、计数及来源筛选前按完整作品组执行，任一版本命中就隐藏整组；直接详情保留标签管理入口。当前树、标签偏好与 Git 历史已有身份和事务能力；新增部分只定义 Peach 的用户选择与影响范围，无需外部规则引擎或模糊匹配依赖。界面沿用 BoardUI Input／Button、`useAction`、标签云和 `SelectionDock`，无新增依赖、存储表或迁移。

## 检查输入与源码文案

Test-diff reuse builds on Git file snapshots, the Python AST, the standard-library TOML/JSON parsers, and the existing filelock. Python comments and manifest description fields are recognized by comparing effective content; real dependencies, execution modes, and inputs that cannot be parsed keep full verification. A full batch stores results per test file, only fills in the affected scope, and does not extend how long a result stays valid.

JS/TS copy checks reuse the [Oxc parser](https://oxc.rs/docs/guide/usage/parser). The dev dependency is pinned to `oxc-parser@0.154.0` (MIT license), sourced from the [official Oxc repository](https://github.com/oxc-project/oxc). The project's Vite/Oxlint toolchain already ships Oxc; the standalone Node checker parses comments, strings, templates, and JSX, and does not enter the application runtime bundle. Frontend source parse errors are zero. A Tree-sitter trial produced errors on named tuple members and JSX query parameters and was not adopted; no new Python parser dependency is added. The Chinese and English criteria live in `scripts/copy-rules.json`; ambiguous wording only prompts a manual check.

## Standalone macOS build

macOS 发行包复用 Windows 固定的 FFmpeg 9.0.2、x264、dav1d 1.5.4 和 untrunc `9d86ec9` 源码锁、下载 SHA-256 校验与 `media_tool_bundle.validate`。untrunc 单独静态链接兼容的 FFmpeg 8.1.3；其 [上游 Makefile](https://github.com/anthwlock/untrunc/blob/9d86ec9ef2ffed1bf8131abe80742c0574db52b6/Makefile)提供 macOS 编译依据。头文件用 `-idirafter` 保留 C++ 标准头 `version` 的优先级。FFmpeg/x264 按 GPL-3.0-or-later、dav1d 按 BSD-2-Clause、untrunc 按 GPL-2.0-or-later 分发；完整对应源码归档包含全部上游源码、构建脚本和许可。Peach 仅维护目标架构、静态库组合、清单与拒收判据，不复制 Homebrew dylib。macOS 编译启用 VideoToolbox、AudioToolbox 与 Secure Transport，硬件转码选择仍服从已有产品策略。

编译工具复用 Meson 1.12.1（Apache-2.0）、Ninja 1.13.2（Apache-2.0）、pkgconf 3.0.7.post0（ISC）和 NASM 3.02（BSD-2-Clause），由 `setup_macos_media_tools.py` 安装到构建目录，应用运行无需这些工具。源码提供完整配置，两个架构分别编译；Intel 二进制可在 Rosetta 下验收，但不能视为 Intel 真机测试。对应源码与二进制的体积、架构和系统最低版本由实际产物核对。

cloudflared 采用 [Cloudflare 官方 2026.9.0 发行](https://github.com/cloudflare/cloudflared/releases/tag/2026.9.0)的双架构归档（Apache-2.0）；固定资产 SHA-256 读取官方 Release API 的 `digest`，与发布正文中的旧摘要分别核对。Peach 保留下载校验、包内查找、ad-hoc 签名与进程归属，不新增隧道协议。原生验收运行 `--version`，不因此宣称已连通公网隧道。没有新增 Python 应用运行依赖；合成媒体验收与临时目录不接触真实 ledger。

Windows、macOS 与 wheel 通过 `bundle_resources.data_files` 共用桥脚本、依赖清单和锁文件。桌面制品额外使用项目已有的 PyInstaller 6.22.3 构建独立 amane 运行环境；Docker 在构建阶段按同一锁预装独立 venv。两者随附固定 revision、版本与可执行文件清单，保持子进程边界。源码与 wheel 在首次实际需要时通过 uv 按锁自动安装到数据目录，安装锁复用 FileLock，下载代理复用 Peach 代理并通过子进程环境交给 uv 与 Git。资源定位复用 `settings_file.PROJECT_ROOT`，覆盖源码、wheel 的 `_resources` 和 PyInstaller 的资源根。打包机制沿用官方 [PyInstaller 数据文件约定](https://www.pyinstaller.org/en/stable/runtime-information.html)与 [uv 环境目录配置](https://docs.astral.sh/uv/concepts/projects/config/#project-environment-path)，不新增应用依赖。分发决策见 [ADR-0124](adr/0124-amane-runtime-preparation.md)。

独立应用复用项目已有的 PyInstaller 6.22.3（GPL-2.0-or-later，带发行例外），由 macOS 原生 bootloader 承载 Python 3.14 与 Cocoa 菜单栏，资源与 CLI 入口沿用现有桌面实现。镜像使用系统自带的 `hdiutil`，无需新增运行时依赖。构建与签名依据 [PyInstaller macOS 文档](https://www.pyinstaller.org/en/stable/usage.html)和 [Apple 分发文档](https://developer.apple.com/documentation/xcode/packaging-mac-software-for-distribution)。

Peach owns the standalone data directory, first launch, build identity, and resource manifest. arm64 and x86_64 use their own Python and native extensions and are built separately per the [PyInstaller architecture constraints](https://pyinstaller.org/en/stable/feature-notes.html#macos-multi-arch-support); frozen programs are not merged with lipo. The app installs no system components and uses drag-and-drop DMG installation; the system-level install flow of a PKG is not part of this path. The source-run LaunchAgent shell keeps its own build entry point. Launch and read/write acceptance of the standalone artifact use an isolated data directory and do not touch the real ledger or the two-machine configuration.

## Hardware transcoding backends

macOS 复用 FFmpeg 的 VideoToolbox H.264 编码及 H.264、HEVC、AV1 解码，沿用现有 `HardwareTranscoder` 的合成帧探测、命令尝试顺序和软件回退；不新增视频框架或 Python 运行依赖。`-allow_sw 0` 要求编码器实际使用硬件，AV1 硬解显式选择 FFmpeg 原生解码器，软件回退继续使用 dav1d。依据 [FFmpeg 硬件解码选项](https://ffmpeg.org/ffmpeg.html#Advanced-Video-options)和当前固定源码中的 `videotoolbox_av1.c`、`videotoolboxenc.c`；具体可用能力依赖设备与系统，不按 Intel 或 M 系列名称推断。抽帧的编码格式探测复用 `media_probe.probe_file`，按文件世代有界缓存，失败后在同一超时预算内软解。两种加速偏好复用 `media-processing.json`、`FileLock` 与原子写入，抽帧与播放各自保存；服务端新任务读取本机偏好，不调整浏览器解码器，不更改 ledger。桌面和手机自动化回归验证独立切换与保存恢复，合成片分别验收硬件可用路径及失败回退。

## HLS 请求断开清理

断开监听与子进程回归采用 [PR #122](https://github.com/peach-mitao/peach/pull/122) 的固定提交 `b49806a8685e128088a3c4825891fd425e279db1`，沿用本仓库 AGPL-3.0-or-later 许可与原作者署名。Peach 保留主线的会话校验、硬件后端和转码异常契约。

HLS 分片请求复用 Range 传输的 ASGI 断开监听、Python 3.14 的 `asyncio.wait` 与既有 `HlsSegmentService._run` 清理：请求断开时取消该请求的生成任务，终止并等待子进程退出，再移除临时分片。取消以单个请求为单位，同一播放会话的其他请求可继续生成；排队请求退出后不启动转码。

进程管理沿用标准库的 [`asyncio.subprocess`](https://docs.python.org/3.14/library/asyncio-subprocess.html)（PSF 许可，无新增安装体积），不引入进程扫描库或 FFmpeg 命令包装层。当前树、REUSE 与 Git 历史的复用检索确认，分片和整片已具备会话取消，断开监听由传输层提供。临时文件与真实 Python 子进程回归覆盖断开退出、并发隔离、排队取消及同会话重试；macOS 的临时 HTTP 服务用合成 HEVC 和 FFmpeg 8.1.1 实测，断连后子进程退出、临时分片与登记清理完成。Windows 报告环境与真实 GPU 播放验收未取得。无新增依赖、生产入口或真实 ledger 写入。

## 播放转码的硬件选择

整片 MP4 与 HLS 复用现有 FFmpeg 的 CUDA / NVENC、VAAPI、`scale_vaapi` 与 `hwupload`，不引入 Python 命令包装库。Windows 发行工具固定为 FFmpeg 9.0.2（GPL-3.0-or-later，来源清单见 `scripts/ffmpeg-sources.json`）；Linux 镜像使用 Debian trixie 的 FFmpeg、`mesa-va-drivers` 与 `vainfo`，随 Debian 安全更新取得版本，不另行复制驱动代码。Mesa 按上游各组件许可证分发，驱动增加的镜像体积由构建决定，AMD 需要宿主机 amdgpu 与固件。设备参数和硬件解码接口依据 [FFmpeg 官方文档](https://ffmpeg.org/ffmpeg.html#Advanced-Video-options)，驱动范围依据 [Debian VAAPI 说明](https://wiki.debian.org/HardwareVideoAcceleration)。

Peach keeps only device selection, the one-time synthetic-frame probe, the command attempt order, and failure fallback; the probe result is shared by the two services. The reuse search covered the current NVENC chain, the media-tool resolver, and the Git history, and found no standalone VAAPI path. FFmpeg already covers codecs and filters; wrapper layers such as ffmpeg-python provide neither device drivers nor hardware availability checks, so the project's subprocess entry point is kept and no Python dependency is added. Regression tests use synthetic input and process stand-ins to verify Linux node selection, permissions and timeouts, 10-bit to NV12 conversion, and whole-file and segment fallback. The 2026-10-19 Windows NVENC measurement used 640×360 synthetic frames for the probe, and synthetic 10-bit HEVC produced H.264 `yuv420p` through both the whole-file MP4 and HLS CUDA / NVENC paths; 128×128 does not meet the encoder's minimum size, so the probe uses 640×360 throughout. Driver initialization, decoding, and playback acceptance on the target AMD GPU were not obtained. There is no production entry point or real ledger write.

Windows 复用 FFmpeg 的 D3D11VA 解码与 `h264_amf`、`h264_qsv` 编码，沿用同一套合成帧探测与回退顺序，不新增依赖。D3D11VA 不指定 `-hwaccel_output_format`，解出的帧回到内存，能接任意编码器，只对 H.264、HEVC、VP9、AV1 开启，名单与 CUDA 相同。AMF 从 D3D11 设备派生上下文，只认 AMD 显卡；混合显卡机器的默认序号常是另一块卡，所以用 `-init_hw_device d3d11va=peach:N` 加 `hwupload` 按序号探测，派生失败时退回软件解码加 AMF。Media Foundation 的 `h264_mf` 在 NVIDIA 加 AMD 的实测机器上无论软硬解码都报 `MF_E_UNSUPPORTED_D3D_TYPE`，未接入。QSV 从 D3D11 派生的写法没有 Intel 显卡可验，只接软件解码加 QSV 编码，探测通过才使用。

随包 FFmpeg 编入 NVENC、AMF、QSV 与 D3D11VA，四条路径都不依赖 PATH 上的第三方构建。AMF 只需 GPUOpen 发布的 `AMF-headers` 头文件包，运行时由 AMD 驱动提供 `amfrt64.dll`；QSV 经 FFmpeg 的 `--enable-libvpl` 接入，libvpl 调度器用 MinGW 编成静态库，运行时由 Intel 驱动提供实现。两者都是 MIT 许可，固定版本和哈希写在 `scripts/ffmpeg-sources.json`，许可证随发行目录与对应源码包分发，整体仍按 GPL-3.0-or-later。交叉编译做法参照 [BtbN/FFmpeg-Builds](https://github.com/BtbN/FFmpeg-Builds) 的 `50-amf.sh` 与 `50-onevpl.sh`：libvpl 的 C++ 调度器静态链接时要带 `libstdc++`，Peach 在 CMake 里给它未定义的 `CXX_LIB` 赋值，让生成的 `vpl.pc` 自带这一项，不改写安装后的文件。2026-10-23 在 Ubuntu 24.04 容器里按发布流程的同一组软件包跑通 `build_ffmpeg_windows.sh`，产物列出 `h264_amf`、`hevc_amf`、`av1_amf` 与 `h264_qsv` 等编码器，`-hwaccels` 含 `cuda`、`dxva2`、`qsv`、`d3d11va`、`amf`，发行包校验通过。在 NVIDIA 独显加 AMD 核显的 Windows 机器上，随包构建的 `h264_amf` 与 `h264_nvenc` 都完成 640×360 合成帧编码，D3D11 序号 1 上传加 AMF 可用、序号 0 报 `No such device`；Peach 自动探测得到 NVENC、AMF（序号 1）与 D3D11VA。该机没有 Intel 显卡，`h264_qsv` 报 libvpl 找不到实现（`MFX_ERR_UNSUPPORTED`），探测按预期跳过；QSV 实际编码未取得。

2026-10-23 在 NVIDIA 独显加 AMD 核显的 Windows 机器上，用 gyan FFmpeg 9.0.1 对 20 秒 1080p 10 位 HEVC 合成片测进程 CPU 时间：纯软件解码 21.9 秒、D3D11VA 解码 1.6 秒；libx264 转码 62.0 秒、D3D11VA 解码加 libx264 41.9 秒；软件解码加 AMF 23.3 秒、AMD 序号上的 D3D11VA 加 AMF 3.4 秒；CUDA 加 NVENC 0.7 秒。单帧抽帧在同一片源上从 7.7 秒降到 0.7 秒。整片与 HLS 经自动、AMF、仅 D3D11VA、软件四种配置输出的都是 H.264，时长与片源一致。

## 文件名噪声清理

整理模板与 `scripts/clean_names.py` 共用 `organize_templates.clean_name_symbols` 和 `clean_filename`。字符分类复用 Python 标准库 `unicodedata`，按 Unicode 类别清除装饰图形、emoji、替代字符、控制字符和私用区噪声；Peach 保留常用标点、文字组合音标、单位、算术符号、扩展名与空名称保护。不新增依赖。

外部候选核对了 [pathvalidate 的文件名清洗](https://pathvalidate.readthedocs.io/en/latest/pages/examples/sanitize.html)与 [ftfy 的编码修复](https://ftfy.readthedocs.io/en/latest/)。前者主要处理文件系统非法字符，其通用符号替换还会删掉番号连接号和扩展名句点；后者通过启发式还原编码，超出本次删除噪声的范围，因此不引入。隔离用例覆盖装饰符号、复合 emoji、不可见字符、乱码替代字形、正常中日文与重音字母、空名称、重复执行和改名回滚；馆藏只读盘点与逐条预览保存在数据目录的复核产物中。

目录范围复用 `platform.resolve_root`、`location_mounts` 与标准库路径组件比较。界面复用 BoardUI Input、IconButton、已有 `FolderSearchIcon` 和 `/api/pick-folder`；预览、执行及后台任务恢复共用 `path` 参数，目录模板以所选目录为起点。空路径表示整个来源，目录边界按组件匹配，不按文本前缀匹配。

## 独立页面的请求反馈

Request error text reuses `core/request-error.ts`, and entity registration reuses `core/entity-links.ts`. The API references these two modules directly, and the first-launch and login pages do not load the main UI's routing and grapheme-segmentation initialization; the main UI still exports the same error text through `core/index.ts`. A cold-start import regression checks that the API does not construct the UI segmenter, and the existing feedback tests cover status codes, timeouts, and server-side Chinese reasons. No new dependencies.

## GitHub project board

User feedback belongs to the app repository `peach-mitao/peach`, and maintainer project tasks belong to `peach-mitao/peach-roadmap`; both share one public Project. Events are handled centrally in the app repository, and the planning repository is re-checked every 6 hours; credentials stay only in the app repository. Issue links use the repository name and number, and the migration mapping stays compatible with existing link rows; the delivered version is always verified against the app repository's mainline commits and release artifacts.

Board sync reuses the GitHub CLI's logged-in authentication, the GraphQL Projects API, and Git's commit-ancestor check; no new Python dependency is added. The official [Projects Actions automation](https://docs.github.com/en/issues/planning-and-tracking-with-projects/automating-your-project/automating-projects-using-actions) supports a separate project credential, and the repository default token cannot write to an organization Project. The native project status workflows have no release-label or artifact-ownership check, and generic auto-add-card Actions do not record acceptance and release separately, so `scripts/project_board.py` keeps Peach's status rules while transport and authentication are handed to the system-installed `gh`. The runtime dependencies are the officially maintained, MIT-licensed GitHub CLI and GPL-2.0 Git, using the versions built into the GitHub-hosted runner; the local machine applies changes only after read-only GraphQL and label-ancestor verification. No production service, ledger, or media writes.

## Adding JAV userscripts

下载渠道复用 `media_sources` 注册表、媒体库名称、`clouddrive_proxy.media_remote_path` 的挂载与推送路径关联，以及现有 CloudDrive2 gRPC 客户端的 `canOfflineDownload` 与 `supportOfflineDownloadManagement` 能力。`LibraryChannels` 在只读探测后生成渠道，PikPak 直连仍使用已有客户端；配置区与专属检查按已声明媒体来源显示。支持任务管理时查询远端列表，仅支持提交时按文件落地确认；取消按钮随渠道能力显示。挂载点关联复用 `CloudPrefix` 和 `map_cloud_path` 完成入库路径转换。渠道标识与媒体来源分别用于提交和落地后处理，未新增依赖、账本表或迁移。账号未实际验证时不把模拟能力视为网盘验收。版本要求与安装入口由上游发布清单传给配置页；配置页安装当前 Peach 随附的同版脚本。

用户脚本由 [peach-mitao/peach-userscript](https://github.com/peach-mitao/peach-userscript)（AGPL-3.0-or-later）独立维护，使用 [Tampermonkey 官方 GM API](https://www.tampermonkey.net/documentation.php) 的本机存储、菜单与 `GM_xmlhttpRequest`；SPA 通知依据 `window.onurlchange` 契约。上游使用 Node.js 24 LTS 内置模块确定性构建，运行时零依赖。Peach 的 `web/integrations/` 消费同一发布的完整安装脚本与站点快照，固定版本、上游提交与 SHA256 见 `jav-userscript-release.json`。安装端点直接返回制品，后端独立校验已登记域名与作品参数；上游导出工具在隔离工作树同步这两份制品和来源记录。Sleazy 热门脚本只提供显式域名与镜像发现证据，不复制其实现或执行第三方代码。宿主扩展由用户安装，兼容性以实际浏览器及脚本管理器授权为准；模拟 GM API 的真实 Chromium 页面完成回归，DMM 与 MGS 实页通过隔离只读 GM 适配核验番号、页面浮层与设置窗口。真实扩展、Peach 与网盘联合下载验收未取得。

Peach 保留页面粗提取与身份冲突提示，服务端复用 `catalog_rules` 的唯一番号规则、`feeds.scan_code`、`web_resource_search.q_resource_search` 的来源顺序与质量排序、`submit_offline_download` 的 infohash 幂等、现有云下载落地与后处理。浏览器添加在共享提交锁内按番号接管已有任务，覆盖其他入口同时提交不同磁力的情况。失败原因直接复用下载任务的 `FAILURE_KINDS` 分为磁力与网盘两类；换磁力把失败任务的 infohash 记入该番号的跳过名单，带名单的搜索不在 manko.fun 高清命中处短路，并在直连与索引器结果中一并排除；候选挑选沿用同一番号判据。专用令牌经 `CredentialStore` 仅保存 SHA-256 摘要，不声明跨机同步字段。队列用已有 `atomic_write_text` 保存本机运行状态，关闭功能会撤销令牌并停止尚未提交的添加。

DMM / MGS 的只读商品号识别复用 `sources.dmm` 的 CID 结构与 `catalog_rules.release_code_from_product`。浏览器提交净化后的详情地址及可选厂家番号；DMM 的目录数字头按来源解释，MGS 保留三位发行前缀，尾字母和日期分隔符保留为身份。`release_code_from_text` 继续处理普通文件名的字幕与画质标记，不用文件名噪声规则改写明确商品身份。资源名称的完整商品号匹配复用同一编目模式，元数据身份校验与 DMM 搜索核对尾字母。运行时不新增 HTTP 请求或依赖；已有 DMM／OpenAver 取证与来源协议足以覆盖转换，不引入第二套外部番号库。完整脚本连接临时账本的真实识别接口，联合验证 DMM／MGS 桌面与手机场景；保留基于模拟响应的跨站交互回归。

配置页继续使用 BoardUI Button / Input / Select 与共享 Note。配对信息复用当前页面 origin、签发响应和浏览器 Clipboard API，脚本在用户粘贴时填表，保存后写入 GM 存储。同源子框架桥接只回报脚本版本和当前地址的配置状态，父窗口、origin、协议版本与一次性 nonce 均须匹配，不回传令牌。未收到回报显示「未检测到」，不推断安装列表。桥接路径窄匹配与来源站顶层注入分别处理；实际脚本管理器权限和扩展联合验收未取得。无新增运行依赖、迁移或 ledger 写入。

A bookmarklet cannot run the cloud-download chain; a full browser extension adds a publishing and permission-management surface; and existing external downloaders do not take on Peach's cloud-drive targets, library import, and post-processing, so none was introduced. API regression with a temporary ledger and simulated 115 / PikPak verifies search, repeated clicks, restart recovery, failure retry, and credential isolation; the userscript and settings page pass desktop and 390×844 browser regression. No new ledger table or migration.

## Result display for long-running tasks

Metadata collection and follow checks reuse the existing field criteria, transactions, and candidate review. All local NFOs are read first and committed per movie; online metadata is committed as each source returns, and the candidate CSV is still merged and written to disk by time. Safe partial rows of a follow list and per-item details are stored separately, and the history boundary and whole-page cursor follow the connector rules. An interruption keeps committed results, and nothing is written to the database after stopping.

The display layer reuses the SQLite `ledger_revision` trigger, candidate and image-directory versions, and TanStack Query 5.104.3 (MIT) query invalidation and task polling; no push service or dependency is added. The app root refreshes the affected read caches by result version, task heartbeats do not invalidate metadata, and new versions during a slow request are queued while the current content is kept. The resource-sync scan also shows completed sources, while cleanup still requires a full scan and confirmation. An isolated ledger, a paused source call, and a manually released Promise verify that intermediate results are visible and that interruptions keep them; there is no real scraping or real ledger write.

## STRM and mounted sources

诊断输出共用 `reporting_identity` 的环境身份和 `diagnostic_exceptions` 的有限错误分类。媒体 stderr 在通用路径脱敏前归类，SQLite 使用结构化错误码与固定原因类别；本机日志、支持包和远程投影按各自字段契约消费，不引入另一套错误监控 SDK。访问日志只放行固定技术路由与协议状态，查询串整体移除；其余路径继续复用通用脱敏。

任务恢复复用固定版 APScheduler 3.11.3（MIT）和标准库 `datetime.timezone.utc`，与现有关注调度器使用同一时区选择方式。一秒间隔检查不依赖本地 IANA 时区数据库；隔离回归令系统时区读取抛错，仍能创建调度器并注册任务。未新增依赖。

日志的实际主机时区名称复用 `tzlocal` 5.4.4（MIT，纯 Python，支持 Windows、macOS、Linux）的 `get_localzone_name()`，仅读名称，不为取证构造 `ZoneInfo`。该包已由 APScheduler 安装，直接使用处在主依赖中显式固定归属，无新增安装包；本地时间和 UTC 偏移复用 `datetime.astimezone()`，名称读取失败保留可用偏移。浏览器使用标准 `Intl` 与 `Date.getTimezoneOffset()`，与服务主机时区分别保存。

响应头策略复用 Starlette 1.8.0（BSD-3-Clause，项目已有固定依赖）的 `MutableHeaders` 与官方 [ASGI 中间件模式](https://www.starlette.io/middleware/#pure-asgi-middleware)，只在 `http.response.start` 阶段设置缓存、安全及机器人策略。文件响应继续使用同一 `receive` 的断连消息，真实异常直接传播，不维护异常文本匹配或统一忽略网络错误的分支。整片转码继续复用 FFmpeg 和现有回退链，Peach 只保留有界本机证据与隐私投影，无新增依赖。

本机诊断继续复用项目的脱敏日志与 ZIP 导出，浏览器使用标准 `canPlayType()`、MSE、Resource Timing 和有界的 [UA Client Hints](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/NavigatorUAData/getHighEntropyValues)，服务端复用 Python 3.14 标准库的依赖元数据、磁盘余量、系统信息以及现有媒体探测缓存。字段白名单和本机证据汇总属于 Peach 的诊断边界；浏览器监控 SDK 会扩大依赖与采集范围，不承担这条本机导出链路。依赖沿用 Video.js 10.2.0 与现有 FFmpeg，不增加运行时依赖，不为导出执行媒体读取或硬件压力探测。能力声明与真实播放的区别按 [MDN `canPlayType()`](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/HTMLMediaElement/canPlayType) 和 [Video.js 媒体来源契约](https://videojs.org/docs/framework/html/guides/media-sources)核对，隔离回归覆盖无视频请求、编码能力缺失、错误状态、脱敏和仅本机写入。

STRM 采用文本指针、播放时解析的通用行为，参考 SmartStrm 文档、OpenList 的 `drivers/strm` 与 Jellyfin 的 STRM 探测入口；固定 revision、许可证与实际读取位置见 [取证记录](reference-snapshots/strm-source-evidence.md)。SmartStrm 核心闭源，源码未取得。Peach 独立实现受限 HTTP(S) 指针，复用 HTTPX、现有 Range 传输、`m3u8` 解析与播放器计划；无新增运行依赖，也不复制上游应用代码。

光鸭、OneDrive 与其他网盘复用声明挂载根、路径翻译、扫描、文件整理与回滚、隔离删除和空目录清理。所有挂载来源统一开放读写，底层权限或不支持的操作由挂载工具报错；网盘登录与平台 API 由飞牛 OS 原生远程挂载、CloudDrive2 或 OpenList 等挂载工具提供，不新增 SDK 或依赖。本地文件先隔离再提交账本；rclone FUSE 通过挂载表识别并直接删除，避免部分后端的远端改名触发复制，账本事务失败时保留可重试的回收站记录。飞牛 OS 原生挂载 115 的实际文件系统可报告为 `fuse.rclone`，不等于用户单独部署 rclone；其他挂载方式仍须分别验证。临时文件与账本回归覆盖三个来源的整理、回滚、删除、权限拒绝和空目录清理；真实账号兼容性见待办。挂载目录可单独设置代理流量保护，成本投影与批处理使用同一规则。STRM、ISO 跳过后台媒体读取，普通媒体保留按来源策略执行的探测；九宫格和时间轴共用本机视频预览开关。

回收站复用 `platform.translate_ledger_path`、声明来源根与 Python 标准库 `stat`，只将 `FileNotFoundError` 视为文件缺失，权限或 I/O 错误保留记录。Linux 挂载判定读取原生 `/proc/self/mountinfo`，字段、父挂载关系与文件系统类型按 [Linux man-pages 6.19](https://man7.org/linux/man-pages/man5/proc_pid_mountinfo.5.html)；采用最深挂载点，覆盖挂载按父子关系选择，不依赖文本行顺序。转义解码与路径比较属于 Peach 的删除策略，没有复制外部实现。rclone 挂载能力参考其[官方文档](https://rclone.org/commands/rclone_mount/)，不新增 rclone 运行服务；现有 psutil 7.2.2 仅是开发依赖，不为读取一份 Linux 原生挂载表增加运行依赖。未知挂载信息保留本地隔离流程，临时文件回归覆盖带空格挂载点、父目录路径、覆盖挂载、删除失败与混合批次事务失败。

Static checking of Actions workflows reuses actionlint 1.7.12 (MIT, the official Docker image `rhysd/actionlint:1.7.12`), used by a local Docker action; the standalone Docker directory is included in Dependabot and the takeover entry point. The GitHub Actions ecosystem does not update `docker://` image references. The official Windows binary completed a local POC against the published checksum; CI checks syntax, expressions, job dependencies, and scripts in a Linux container. Python and browser regression continue to use the existing unified entry point and Playwright; the six-shard browser weights come from the 69 TAP files of a [passing CI run](https://github.com/peach-mitao/peach/actions/runs/38981261189) and are recorded in `scripts/ci-browser-timings.json`; no application runtime dependency is added.

目录浏览的 `media_folders` 复用 `PureWindowsPath`、媒体库配置的目录前缀和账本路径，不访问媒体挂载。`folder_rules` 复用 profile 偏好、规范实体、字段归属、身份断言、逐件归属否定和扫描登记入口；规则以 `user:directory-<目录标识>` 保存独立来源，预览摘要绑定资产版本与关系，撤回只处理本来源。已有明确归属保持独立。目录栏复用覆盖式滚动条，按筛选条下沿、目录实际位置与可见视口计算高度；共用查询的错误由正文或手机目录弹窗呈现一次，独立请求保留各自的重试。

The UI adopts MIT-licensed BoardUI Tree View, Breadcrumb, and Direction verbatim; the pinned source SHA-256 is in `frontend/src/react/boardui/ORIGIN.md`. It reuses the existing React 19.3.0, React Aria Components 1.22.1, Motion 14.1.0, and Remix Icon 4.9.0, with no new dependencies. Peach's native link adapts the upstream `next/link` slot, and the domain logic only handles directory ownership, conflict retention, scope confirmation, and source withdrawal. The capability search covered current media-library grouping, directory negation, field ownership, and the Git history of `media_libraries.py`; physical directory organization continues to use the existing entry point. Verification uses only a temporary ledger and a synthetic library.

Linux Docker containers reuse Docker's official Python `3.14.8-slim-trixie`, uv `0.13.1` (MIT/Apache-2.0), and the "verify before publish" flow of Docker's official Buildx, login-action, and build-push-action. The Python image license stays with the upstream components, and FFmpeg uses the Debian package and its license. Peach's differences are only config reload, metadata-processing requests, and persistence paths; no new Python dependency is introduced and the real ledger is not rewritten. CloudDrive2 uses its official standalone service and is neither copied nor bundled into Peach. Deployment and evidence scope are in [Docker deployment](DOCKER.md).

The favorites window reuses the React Aria shell, Peach's settings-popover layout, and the shared close control `SettingsDialogClose`. The entry is a neutral star button; entity categories and saved filters are shown separately, and Marked and Watch later sit at the bottom of the navigation. Favorite images reuse entity references, studio marks, local representative works, and the local avatar proxy of bound online sources, trying each in turn through the site-wide image fallback chain, without writing to preferences. The original BoardUI source hashes are in `docs/BOARD_UI.md`. Rule34 video covers use the source thumbnail, images keep their original file extension, and failed images are retried when follow data refreshes. No new runtime dependency or real ledger write.

The follow cover proxy reuses the media-host allowlist registered by sources; third-party image addresses and redirects continue to go through public-network DNS validation.

The favorites popover navigation reuses the settings-page liquid glass, `useViewGlide`, `attachOverlayScrollbar`, the enter and exit keyframes, and the drop shadow, and desktop and mobile checks use the actual computed styles. The official trailer reuses the Prestige SKU→UUID protocol from amane `cc42cee6ecf290cf05af1caa347d2dc2d5aeffcd`, obtaining the address through the work identity returned by the official site and the public MP4 field; reads reuse the existing source transport, limits, and media proxy, with no new dependency.

The media indicator reuses the translucent gray capsule of the follow detail: `rgba(0,0,0,.5)` background, no outline, `--pill-radius` corners, and the existing dot spacing. Scrolling content reuses `wireHorizontalScroller`, `data-overflow-left/right`, and the left and right fade of `board.css`, keeping the backing complete. Trailers prefer the DMM / studio official declaration, then the JavDB detail for the same code, with the anonymous WebUI as a fallback. DMM reuses `DmmSource`'s CID search, identity check, GraphQL, `SourceTransport`, and the media Range proxy; the `sample2DMovie.highestMovieUrl` field was verified against the same pinned amane revision (GPL-3.0, used only as protocol evidence) and a real official response, without introducing upstream code or new dependencies. Positive and negative caches for DMM and JavDB are isolated per source and can be rechecked daily; a missing trailer and a connection failure are returned separately. `RCTD-764` confirmed 206, `video/mp4`, and an MP4 file header through a 1 KiB Range request, and identity mismatch, source priority, WebUI failure, caching, and the media-host boundary are covered by behavior tests.

## Design guideline references

Open Claude UI (MIT) is used only for the [design guidelines](DESIGN_GUIDELINES.md) and the [source trade-offs](reference-snapshots/open-claude-ui.md), and the original text is locked in `reference-sources.json`. The adopted principles are reducing duplicate frames, semantic typography, container adaptation, IME protection, keeping the reading position, recoverable gestures, anchored zoom, and real feedback; components reuse the existing BoardUI, React Aria, Motion, Swiper, and scrolling entry points. Sample-task end states, global styles, the host, voice, and proprietary protocols do not enter the runtime. The source review is complete and a browser POC was not obtained; no new dependency, production setting, or ledger write.

## Upstream app features

amane `0.20.0 / cc42cee6ecf290cf05af1caa347d2dc2d5aeffcd` (GPL-3.0) provides behavior references for entity favorites,
on-demand requests for metadata comparison images, and structured failures; Peach implements the interaction independently, reusing the existing profile,
TanStack Query, canonical entities, source settlement, and review queue, and does not bring in the upstream application database or task engine.
Entity favorites are inherited through merged identities, and named library filters store only existing catalog query conditions.

Source connections reuse `SourceTransport`, the failure-reason table, and the cooldown file, and diagnostics record the latest evidence for connection and parsing separately.
All of amane's official sources can optionally use a per-site direct connection or a public proxy, and the bridge keeps a single subprocess and a 60-second total time limit.
Comparison images are requested after a click and can be retried if not obtained within 30 seconds; no arbitrary-URL image proxy is added.

[Custom sources](CUSTOM_SOURCES.md) refer to OpenAver `502278a3e09c7520838ffb00b2ff080323a4cdcb`
(MIT) for declarative configuration, code identity checks, and the public-network target guard, using the already pinned curl_cffi, filelock,
SiteRecord, and candidate settlement; [external playback](EXTERNAL_PLAYERS.md) reuses the existing media-root check, HTTP Range,
standard M3U, and client protocols, with no new dependency. The main Python program and the standalone bridge both use 3.14, and the standalone lock still isolates the dependency tree.

## 2D playback wall

Folia (`chthollyphile/folia-major`, AGPL-3.0, revision `9a01e788ed52d25ea01b1f0d37934e5e371ae82b`) provides rectangular block templates, camera / world coordinates, viewport culling, looping / finite walls, and visual styles; the originals are saved in [rectangular block templates](reference-snapshots/upstream/folia-block-templates.ts), the [yield table](reference-snapshots/upstream/folia-block-reflows.ts), the [motion parameters](reference-snapshots/upstream/folia-wall-reflow-motion.ts), and the [wall styles](reference-snapshots/upstream/folia-wall.css). `frontend/src/react/video-wall/` uses four templates and their mirrors, 6×6 in-place expansion, and a dark right-angle collage on the standalone `/wall` page. Covers reuse `ArtSlot`, `cardArtwork`, and face / front-cover framing; full covers and front covers are chosen by tile ratio, and only videos that have a cover are accepted. Query, BoardUI, Motion, the detail entry point, the stage player, and the mini window all use existing project capabilities, with no added dependency.

Multi-part and version items inside the wall use the `queueKey`, `fetchQueue`, `chooseItem`, and `nextPart` of `item-detail`, and list rows reuse `MixQueue`; previous, next, and progress controls listen to the same video. The batch-change entry reuses the home page `Shuffle` and the original waiting animation. An expanded card's cover fills the card, the title overlays the lower middle, and during playback the cover background is blurred and the title moves to the top.

The upstream repository is still maintained, with a stack of React 19, TypeScript 7, and Vite 8. The template and coordinate algorithms have no Python or platform dependency, and the official consumer is the browser build. Three.js, Pixi, lyrics, and a music account system do not apply to a video wall and were not introduced. The explore wall reuses the existing Query pagination, assigns works by the instance key that enters the viewport, and keeps already-seen positions; when there are not enough undisplayed works it continues paging automatically, pages without covers keep being skipped, and the wall loops only after the full range is exhausted. Filtered and entity-work walls use a fixed three columns with manual continuation. Geometry tests cover 80,000 items, negative coordinates, stable continuation positions, and the exhausted state, and player tests cover same-instance handoff, cancellation, and the source threshold; see [playback wall evidence](reference-snapshots/folia-video-wall.md).

Highlighting reuses the inner stroke and glow of Folia's current-playing marker, and Peach applies it to both selection and playback. Queue wheel scrolling is handed to the existing scroll area of `MixQueue` and the wall does not take it over; manual batch change uses a separate waiting state, and once Query is ready it moves to the new area and restores the wall. The global entry stays resident like immersive mode, non-work pages keep the source and JAV context and the return address, and library and entity work pages inherit the current filters.

## Script directories and parameters

Script paths read the directory configuration of `settings_file`. The Cookie of `sync_sha1_115.py` defaults to `secrets/115-cookies.txt`, and the review CSV defaults to `review/115-sha1重复.csv` (the filename means "115 SHA-1 duplicates"); an existing file can be specified with `--cookie-file`, `--dupes-out`, or the corresponding `PEACH_115_COOKIE_FILE` and `PEACH_115_DUPES_OUT`. The directory sort preference uses the repeatable `--deprioritize-dir`. Both TLS shells share `resolve_tls_directory.py`, with an explicit `-OutDir` / `PEACH_TLS_DIR` taking precedence; the target ledger root of `rehome_unknown_jav.py` is specified explicitly by `--ledger-unknown-root`.

## 馆藏处理流量

馆藏处理的流量分账复用最长路径前缀、CloudDrive 账号数据代理配置、`ContextVar`、现有任务快照和断点恢复。`media_sources.cost_for` 只控制逐目录代理保护与工作范围。HTTP 下载复用固定版 HTTPX 0.28.1（BSD-3-Clause）的 [`num_bytes_downloaded`](https://www.python-httpx.org/advanced/clients/#monitoring-download-progress)，按压缩传输字节累计，失败的部分响应保留读数。FFmpeg 输入读取复用 verbose 级别的 AVIO Statistics，NFO 与配套图片累计实际应用读取量；挂载缓存、预读、浏览器与外部采集器使读数仅能作为估算。

`processing_traffic_plan` 是 Peach 的工作量策略，复用 `_missing_fields`、`_scrapes_as_jav`、`_RemoteSession` 的来源链、7 天缓存、`_MissCache` 与 `cover_settled`，按番号合并资料请求；只有实际待补全、未命中缓存的来源进入计划。HTTP 下载共用 `MAX_SOURCE_BYTES`，amane 计划不受该 HTTP 预算裁切。按来源连接设置拆分直连与代理；配套文件检查完毕后，用实际字段与核查范围重算，并将完成阶段和番号持久到任务快照，恢复时继续扣除已完成工作。

已有目录的文件数量与资料工作量预检访问账本和本机缓存，连接配置另向 CloudDrive 只读查询。至多采样 256 张封面与 256 份 NFO 的大小中位数；无样本时采用封面 512 KiB、NFO 32 KiB，待探测媒体 8 MiB、九帧 72 MiB，媒体项受文件大小限制。资料每站按两次、每次约 250 KiB 的采集器工作量基准估算。以上是默认计划值，不是实际下载保证。界面以 `estimated_bytes` / `metered_estimated_bytes` 显示约值，逐盘、代理与全部合计共用一份状态；已用量保留实际累计。领域工作量不引入外部估算库，通用统计、目录索引与数据库复用 Python 标准库；无新增依赖。

`processing_inventory` 复用 `scan._walk`、`walk_root_for`、`medium_of` 和番号识别规则，对尚无登记视频的根目录做只读名称预检。每次 GET 推进一批、增量规划新增文件，令牌只引用服务端已校验的配置和目录，不接受客户端路径；令牌空闲 5 分钟失效，弹窗关闭后无后台遍历任务。已有登记视频的根目录沿用账本，不重复预检。文件数按路径计、部数按可识别番号去重；没有发行证据的文件名识别仍有遗漏与误判，实际配套资料处理负责补充。

`processing_traffic_history` 复用标准库 `statistics.mean`、任务计量上下文及 `atomic_write_text`，保存每组最近 50 次正数样本、至少 3 次才替代默认值。探测／抽帧按目录分组，完整资料下载按来源集合、封面需求和连接方式分组；失败、无下载和外部流量缺测的资料处理不学习。只保存本机统计，不记录番号、URL 或文件名；预检只读历史。通用预测库不能定义 Peach 的缓存、计量覆盖与来源工作量，采用已有任务策略和标准库，不新增依赖。

明细开合复用设置页 `Disclosure` 与 `setCollapseOpen`，可由任务提示同步收起；任务正文的网格高度与同一枚箭头沿用 Collapse 的 200ms 节奏。全局减少动态效果设置关闭这些过渡。网盘标识复用 `SourceLabel`，115、PikPak 等使用已登记的站标。

## Windows 常驻托盘

The diagnostic-report completion window reuses the Windows `DialogBoxIndirectParamW` and `EndDialog` system message loop, with controls and callbacks declaring pointer width; export runs in a separate single-instance thread so the tray message callback can return. Native isolated-process verification covers "Open directory", "Open file", "Close", title-bar close, Esc, and Enter, without operating the existing tray. `TaskDialogIndirect`, which needs a common-controls v6 activation context, was not adopted, and no GUI dependency or PowerShell subprocess was introduced for a single window; no new dependency.

Standalone launch reuses Windows `CreateProcessW`, `STARTUPINFOEX`, and [`PROC_THREAD_ATTRIBUTE_PARENT_PROCESS`](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/api/processthreadsapi/nf-processthreadsapi-updateprocthreadattribute), called through the standard-library `ctypes`; the parent identity and Job inheritance come from the desktop process of the current session. The child process may keep the shared system Job, and the acceptance criterion is that it stays alive after the launcher's private Job is closed; [nested Jobs](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/procthread/nested-jobs) may allow only partial breakaway. The child is created suspended, and after ownership is recorded it runs via `ResumeThread`; when creation is refused, `CREATE_BREAKAWAY_FROM_JOB` is retried only once, and when resuming the main thread fails, the child that has not run is reclaimed. The official system API needs no extra package, pinned version, or third-party license, applies to the Windows versions the project supports, and was checked on 2026-10-15. Peach keeps parameters, the Peach data directory, and source and PyInstaller environment handling, and consumes the one-time handoff marker. The entry point first reuses the read-only lock probe of `SingleInstance` and opens the page directly when already running; the lock probe creates no file and does not block the child process from acquiring the lock.

The current tree and Git history already have `windows_restart`, `tray_lifecycle`, and `process_job`, and each continues to be reused for its restart, recovery, and child-service cleanup behavior. `DETACHED_PROCESS` only detaches from the console; `CREATE_BREAKAWAY_FROM_JOB` depends on the chosen parent's Job allowing breakaway; WMI, Shell COM, and scheduled tasks introduce extra services, environment handoff, or registration lifecycles and were not adopted; `pywin32` is not a runtime dependency and the monitor must load only the standard library, so native APIs are used, with no new dependency. The source tray calls the base interpreter directly and, following the `__PYVENV_LAUNCHER__` protocol of the [CPython 3.14.7 venv launcher](https://github.com/python/cpython/blob/v3.14.7/PC/venvlauncher.c), keeps the virtual-environment identity. Only the protocol is referenced; no PSF-licensed source was copied. Temporary-process regression covers multiple layers of shared Jobs, inner automatic breakaway, and the launcher's private kill-on-close Job: ordinary children exit with the launcher, while the desktop child stays alive in the shared Job and consumes the handoff marker. Unicode arguments, environment, working directory, virtual-environment identity, and process cleanup were also checked; the real Explorer is not added to the test Job. Diagnostics record only the process-member state that can be obtained and the raw limit values of the calling process's direct Job, with bit definitions reusing the [Microsoft docs](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/api/winnt/ns-winnt-jobobject_basic_limit_information); when the full handle chain and owner were not obtained this is stated explicitly, and nothing is inferred through a system-wide elevated scan.

## Western portraits and official covers

The fields of the Babepedia main gallery and the Vixen network's public GraphQL were checked against the Babepedia and vixenNetwork scrapers of [Stash CommunityScrapers](https://github.com/stashapp/CommunityScrapers/tree/d0f75013e09a0469b70365fef3294ca915edcf8c) (AGPL-3.0; the revision obtained on 2026-10-07). That project uses Python and can run on Windows through a plugin; Cloudflare rate limiting and upstream field changes may cause a miss. Only the public protocol and identity fields are referenced; the AGPL implementation is not copied, the closed Stash adapter is not restored, and no dependency is introduced. HTTP, HTML, image inspection, the content cache, avatar installation, and the cover sidecar reuse the existing `HttpxTransport`, BeautifulSoup 4.15.0, `avatar_provider`, `avatar_picker`, and `cover_artwork` respectively.

A real read-only trial obtained the main galleries of Angel Smalls, Christy White, Lena Anderson, Melody Marks, and Sweet Sophia; Babepedia's Blaire Ivory profile explicitly lists the alias Lena Anderson. The official covers of Tushy 2019-08-14 "Cam To Me" and Vixen 2019-04-09 "I Want It All" were both obtained. Search also returns works by Lena Paul and others; Peach filters by studio, full name, release date, and unique result, and checks the detail again. Official covers without a code are saved as `ASSET-ID-<id>`, and the asset field keeps its original value. Avatars keep the main-gallery candidate, existing avatars are not overwritten automatically, and the official full cast list takes part in co-star protection; multi-person covers need a selection box. The harvest entry point `harvest_western_artwork.py` handles at most 16 people per round, defaults to preview only, and outputs a source receipt; the cover sidecar records `auto:western-artwork@<time>` and is withdrawn by batch with `revert_auto_landing.py`.

## Entity identity and occupation

Creator references of local works get trusted browse categories and the same-source representative-work avatars of the profile page attached in bulk by `web_catalog.attach_creator_reference_details`. `avatarInner`, the queue, and the detail share the image contract of entity image first, usable representative-work fallback second; the representative work of a publishing account keeps the "not the person themself" hint. Seller is an account role, and the detail shows "Seller" according to the trusted `identity_categories` without inferring a performer identity.

Company profiles reuse `entity.metadata_json`, HTTPX, the official site's company summary table and definition lists, and the identifier harvester. `company_profiles` has only generic criteria (summary labels, the original sentence naming the party to the terms of service), stores source, original text, and batch per field, and only fills blanks; when a page has several company tables, the table is chosen by legal name against the entity name and aliases, company founding and brand launch are listed separately, and group and division-of-labor relations are not derived by code and are only candidates. `harvest_company_profiles.py` produces a review JSON by default, and `--input --apply --backup --batch auto:company-profile@…` replays the criteria against the cached pages before landing in the database; aliases, links, and third-party pages in the list are not written. `revert_auto_landing.py` can withdraw the company fields and identifiers of the same batch, with no new dependency.

Babepedia profile harvesting reuses the primary-name and alias cross-check of `babepedia_page`, the whole-row source protection of `performer_profiles`, and the occupancy check of `performer_alias_followup.land`. English stage names are registered only from explicitly allowed sources, a single romanized word is registered only when it is the page's primary name, and JAV successors keep their own name policy. Real-person accounts and performers share the profile table, alias groups, and avatar candidates; the account role and old addresses are kept, and links that disagree with the registered account only go into the receipt. A performer's main image fills an empty avatar only after face comparison with a solo-work cover through `avatar_followup.match_gallery`; account and personal-profile galleries are only candidates, recommended people are excluded, a single image failure continues with the remaining finite candidates, and the candidate table accepts only profile addresses with the verdict `命中` ("hit"). `harvest_western_profiles.py` handles at most 16 people per round, with plan, backup, receipt, and batch withdrawal all in place; existing profiles and avatars are kept. A real read-only review obtained 16 matched profiles including Ria Kurumi, with no new dependency.

Identity classification reuses `entity`, source relations, `field_owners`, SQLite migrations, and version triggers. Occupation and account role use separate multi-valued assertions, and cross-role identity uses withdrawable relations; `hasOccupation` and `sameAs` of [Schema.org Person](https://schema.org/Person) and [Role](https://schema.org/Role) provide semantic reference, and the official docs were checked on 2026-10-06. The implementation neither copies an external parser nor adds a dependency. Directory inference and search hits are stored only as candidates, and public-source facts and user review keep separate statuses.

The FC2 seller roster reuses the `label` and `seller_url` source contract of `sources/fc2.py`, `normalise_code_key`, `upsert_asset_entity`, occupation assertions, and the SQLite backup. `import_fc2_sellers.py` reads existing work snapshots and starts no collection; the account address, source work number, and library work number must agree. Same name with different accounts and work-source conflicts stay in the review output, and existing identities and profiles are not overwritten; the plan assigns collection names to existing accounts by the same criteria as ingestion, works that ingestion does not accept are recorded as skipped, and works in the trash get no seller. The write ownership is `script:fc2-seller@<batch>`, and the unified withdrawal entry point `revert_auto_landing.py` handles account links, aliases, external numbers, seller assertions, and source relations together; entities with later profiles or references are kept. Navigation reuses `BoardTabs`, and animation authors keep the `creator` identity and profile route and are browsed through the artist category. No new dependency or external parser.

Follow-author profile creation (`follow_creators`, ADR-0096) reuses the author grouping of the follow page `follow_identity.name_key` / `author_name`, the platform-name gate of `canonicalize_entity_name`, the card recognition of `follow_sources.profile_link_identity`, and the successor dispatch (ADR-0040). Links and aliases are attributed to `auto:follow-creator@<batch>`, withdrawn together by `revert_auto_landing.py`; an entity with other references is kept. The same-name confirmation on the profile page reuses `Note` and the Board `Button`, with no new dependency.

The online author profile page reuses the follow page's `FollowCard`, photo wall, and `author_facets`; local and online views share the entity identity. Online avatar candidates reuse the official-host resolution of `follow_avatar`, the credential boundary of `follow_media_resolver` and `open_upstream`, the bounded cover request of `follow_cover_service`, and the crop installation of `avatar_picker_cache`. Candidates are limited to content bound to and visible for the current entity, social-media automatic resolution covers X, Patreon, pixiv, and FANBOX, and for other sources the user supplies the image address; no new dependency.

The creator row and card avatars and bylines of the follow page reuse `FollowAuthorLink` and the sole entity binding of the source to enter the creator profile page; cards take identity from the actual publisher. Authors without a profile keep the follow filter. The creator row scrolls horizontally to reach all authors. The filter conditions and the card hover surface leave 16px; the home, follow, and profile pages share the intersection bar. `useViewGlide` reuses `ResizeObserver` to watch show/hide and adjacent control sizes, and `moveGlidePane` cancels the displacement and deformation animations when repositioning, with no new dependency.

JAV discovery cards reuse the library's `data-media-card` grid and hover surface, the `coverImage` cover framing, the `cardRatio` large/small image ratio, and the `feedIdentity` identity avatar; filtering reuses `FilterGlassRows`, `FilterPill`, `Segments`, and `SortKeys`. Performer references get their image version and framing from `web_catalog.entity_ref`; titles keep two lines and the action area sticks to the bottom. Discovery queries reuse the taste summary and the IDF/MMR of `related.rank_related`, output relevance and the actual selection score, and support sorting by taste, release date, source rating, duration, and code. Source metadata reuses `PublicAPI`, `BackgroundJob(runs=None)`, `fsutil.atomic_write_text`, and the standard-library `ContextVar`; it is fixed at no more than 100 works, with a real HTTP budget of 202 requests, 16 MiB, 900 seconds, and a 3-second serial interval. The metadata sidecar is valid for 6 hours, holds at most 2,048 entries, has a 15-minute failure cooldown, and after completion or stopping continues through an explicit refresh. `WantMagnets` shares the resource layout of the work detail and the want list through `queryUrl`, arranges actions by the width of the resource-area container, keeps caches separate per source, and reuses `fmtSize` for capacity. The static search boxes of JAV and rosters reuse `islandSearchInput`. For the metadata extension see [JAV discovery](JAV_DISCOVERY.md); no new dependency.

JAV 作品详情与馆藏视频共用 `IdentityCell` 的头像、标识和首字回退，标题折叠复用 `useFoldedTitle`。厂牌大小写唯一匹配与 `sources.javdb.maker_writing` 的日英组合解析只用于来源身份投影，标识按规范名读取。评论、资源复用 `Disclosure` 和 `setCollapseOpen`；来源与评论评分共用按小数裁切填充的星星。桌面右栏以 `contain:size` 排除正文的固有高度贡献，媒体区决定框体高度，正文在右栏内滚动。写操作取 `/healthz` 的账本只读状态，与运行配置权限分别处理。图片切换由 `MediaCarousel` 同时服务关注、库外 JAV 与馆藏详情，提供环形翻页、横向缩略图和位置圆点。缩略图与圆点复用浏览器原生 `overflow-x:auto`、`wireHorizontalScroller`，选中项在切图、循环和容器缩窄时保持可见。缩略图使用 `IntersectionObserver` 按可见范围挂载，馆藏经 `/sample-thumb`，来源图片共享查询缓存，关注优先取附件小图；Swiper 14.3.0 留给灯箱。馆藏样张复用 `sample_images.detail_refs` 和 `/sample-image`，`/api/item` 只读本地序号、最多 30 项；查看图片暂停正片，返回保留位置。推荐分标签复用 BoardUI `Chip` 与共享 Tooltip。视频预览的 `mountPreviewPlayer` 复用已固定的 Video.js 10.2.0（Apache-2.0）、官方 HLS 媒体组件、`mountPlayerQualityControl`、`mountPlayerSeekPreview` 和 `mountPlayerSpinner`，安装共用控制条、设置、时间、音量与窄宽度折叠。来源给定的 HLS 直接由播放器请求；片源代理、馆藏 ID 和观看上报沿用各自入口。播放器生命周期由当前详情管理，异步加载完成前离开会取消挂载，切换与关闭会停止并销毁实例。

Card multi-select is controlled uniformly by the `selectMode` of `MediaCard` and `FollowCard`: the selection entry is kept, while single-item save, watched, ignore, watch later, preview jump, and trash actions are not mounted; leaving multi-select restores single-item actions. The home page, entity pages, follow, and the trash use the same set of components.

Source credentials in the settings page reuse `Section`, `Stack`, and `SettingsSectionLabel`; standalone credential pages keep their own panel hierarchy. The custom-source form and the uninstall switch use only one level of body spacing, and list and form action areas reuse `Footer`; the add entry of an empty source list sits in the `Stack` body. PikPak shows the account form directly, with both login actions in one row. Connection states such as online and offline reuse BoardUI `StatusDot`; permission, task, and credential results reuse `Chip`. Work-metadata site marks are read from the local cache via `/site-mark`, and the resource-search help links straight to the [detailed configuration guide](RESOURCE_SEARCH.md). No new runtime dependency, and credential contents and the ledger are not modified.

Account ownership reuses `review_decision`, `creator_collection_base`, `write_owned_fields`, and the whole-library ownership audit. Explicitly rejected asset-account combinations are skipped during automatic ingestion; user writes keep the review entry. Web-celebrity bloggers hit only trusted individual, content-creator, or publishing-account assertions, and unclassified accounts keep all roster entries. `attach_avatar_availability` centrally handles work avatars for profile pages, indexes, search, and statistics: a publishing account lacking evidence of the person or a performing occupation still gets the work frame as a fallback, additionally marked `avatar_stand_in`, and installed images are kept independently. Avatar filling for individual bloggers reuses `avatar_cover_face.face_square`, `readable_cut`, and the two-part mutual confirmation `agreeing` of ADR-0074, with frames taken from the contact-sheet nine grid (ADR-0099). Source audit lists, per-relation receipts, and backups are stored in `peach-data/review/`.

`entity_identity_research` and `apply_entity_identity_research.py` reuse canonical entity writes, field-ownership protection, read-only preview, SQLite backup, and frozen plans. Per-row withdrawal receipts check later modifications, newly added entities with extra references are refused deletion, and watch history and manual judgments are not overwritten by rollback. Site research reuses `performer_alias_followup.WikiSitePages` (wrapping `WikiPages` of `sources/seesaa.py`, plus 404 memory and per-site cooldown), the work-table parsing `rows()` and `person_profile`, and the EUC-JP page-name direct-fetch approach, with no new dependency; the successor shell of `creator_identity_followup` copies the two successors for filling studios and filling aliases.

## 复核队列

复核快照在一次请求内复用人物、厂牌和系列的身份解析，下一份快照重新读取账本，别名合并后不会沿用旧身份。已批准及当前自动否决的候选在加载作品预览、头像和取景前移出显示集合；稍后和人工忽略仍各自保留。封面存在性复用 `/cover` 的文件及来源判定，不加载全库封面取景边车。接口只传结构化候选，不重复携带 CSV 的 `candidates_json`，批准仍从原候选文件核验。缓存按实际读取的资料、身份、复核和标签决定表失效，同一版本的并发请求复用一次计算；未提供版本号的旧库仍按提交通知失效，不保留为无限期快照；前端分组与分页计算随输入变化更新。复用标准库 `functools.cache`、现有聚合缓存和 React `useMemo`，无新增依赖或存量数据转换。

复核状态复用 `review_decision`、候选 CSV、TanStack Query 和 BoardUI `Tabs`。`/api/review` 的根段提供待处理，`queues.later` 与 `queues.ignored` 提供独立分类和计数；页面地址保存状态与分类。旧 `skipped` 直接投影为稍后处理，人工 `rejected` 投影为已忽略，自动判定不混入人工忽略。迁移 `0050` 只新增 `review_decision_history`，不重写旧决定。显式恢复先在同一事务保存原决定，再删除当前拒绝或跳过决定；已批准和不在当前候选中的资料不可恢复。只读镜像校验三个状态完整性，旧缓存缺状态时报告未取得。无新增依赖。

筛选浮窗复用首页的 `FilterGlassRows`、`useViewGlide` 和透明状态键。状态与分组、筛选按控件实际宽度自然排列，足宽同排并以首页同款竖线分组，放不下时换排并隐藏竖线；空分类保留状态入口，只读模式不提供全选或写入。分组下拉与全选保留原 Select、Button 样式。字号、状态键高度、玻璃材质和吸顶沿用同一维护源，不另设页签皮肤。

复核卡片的来源选择复用官方 `RadioCard` 与 `RadioGroup`；当前值与候选共用一个正文滚动区，来源依据通过现有 `CollapseRegion` 独立展开。外框与操作带复用管理页卡面和 Fieldset 布局，字段徽章及忽略操作保持中性，通过使用原主按钮。来源、取值和资料采用规则不变；官方源码与差异见[复核卡片取证](reference-snapshots/boardui-review-cards.md)。

## 发现推荐缓存

Search recommendations reuse `/api/items`, the library-scoped `/api/jav/discoveries`, `/api/follow`, `/api/follow/authors`, and `/api/feeds/discoveries`, and images reuse `coverImage`, `feedNewCoverHtml`, `avatarInner`, and `authorAvatarHtml`. JAV keeps the IDF/MMR taste ordering, follow takes random works of all statuses, and videos and images reuse `groupMediaKinds`, `itemForMedia`, and attachment thumbnails; bylines keep the publisher criterion of `followIdentity`. The search box does not start out-of-library metadata completion; the detail and subscription keep the existing work, author, and follow-management routes respectively. No new dependency, migration, or ledger write.

The browsing evidence for discovery recommendations reuses the private history database of `taste_history`, canonical identities, and atomic JSON writes; per-day evidence and same-address identity resolution have independent caches. The cache stores only entity counts, and history, aliases, follows, tag decisions, and resolution rules take part in invalidation; watch feedback does not re-resolve history, and task heartbeats do not affect the preference version. Page prefetch reuses TanStack Query's `ensureQueryData`, with no new dependency.

## Player quality

The frame geometry of the work detail is managed by `react/stage/stage.css`, and the library and out-of-library JAV share the viewport height, media row, and the reserved space for resident thumbnails. The two SVG layers of fractional stars share the same icon box. Thumbnail wheel scrolling reuses `wireHorizontalScroller` and cleans up on unmount. The public image cache uses the standard-library `Future` to coalesce same-key requests, with the cache lock covering only in-memory reads and writes; image downloads use three slots, and metadata fetching uses a separate lock and the existing source interval, adding no dependency.

Resources still to find reuse the source chain, release-time policy, and serial rate limiting of `MagnetSearch`, and server results are saved atomically as a private JSON sidecar with `atomic_write_text`. Reading the cache and network queries use separate locks; on restart, raw wall-clock time is converted to the current process's monotonic clock. The sidecar holds at most 128 works and 16 MiB, errors are not saved, and a failed manual refresh keeps successful results. The browser keeps TanStack Query and localStorage, adding no cache service or dependency.

清晰度控件复用 Video.js 10.2.0 官方 `@videojs/hlsjs-video` 的 hls.js 引擎。多档播放从实际轨道读取分辨率，自动码率和固定轨道通过引擎选择；单档 HLS 缺少尺寸时读取原生视频元素的实际尺寸，并响应 `loadedmetadata`、`resize`。尺寸未取得时显示「当前画质」，轨道 ID 不作界面名称。来源各自独立的 MP4 档位由 Peach 保留播放位置后切换片源。

画面旋转复用浏览器 CSS `transform` 与 `ResizeObserver`；Peach 仅计算保持比例的显示尺寸、设置菜单及会话释放。采用现有 Video.js 10.2.0，不增加依赖；依赖旧版组件接口的旋转插件不进入运行时。本地播放器复用 Python 标准库 `os.startfile`、`subprocess` 和 macOS `/usr/bin/open`，保留固定播放器名单、媒体根授权、本机直连与 Docker 边界。本机请求判断由 `web_local_access` 供配置与媒体路由共用。媒体路径复用 `media.normalized_path`，Windows 保留可直接读取的挂载盘盘符，不向虚拟文件系统请求最终路径。系统能力随 Python／操作系统发布，无额外许可证或包体积；真实桌面应用唤起与浏览器协议支持分别验收，不能由进程请求成功推断解码成功。用法与上游依据见[外部播放器](EXTERNAL_PLAYERS.md)。

The player is wired in following the [official Video.js 10 migration guide](https://videojs.org/docs/framework/html/guides/migrate-from-video-js-8) and the [10.1.0 source](https://github.com/videojs/video.js/tree/919ad955a9baf2433c68aba8151654dde0e58b5d/packages), covering `video-player`, `media-container`, the official buttons / sliders, and the HLS / DASH media components. `@videojs/cdn` 10.1.0 (Apache-2.0) provides pinned ESM modules; the build script distributes them by the closure of static and literal dynamic imports and registers hashes, and browser support follows the project targets. Peach keeps source authorization, duration semantics, statistics, the subtitle menu, and the stage lifecycle, and the native video carries subtitles and watch reporting through the `media` slot. The legacy entry of the `video.js` 10.1.0 package is only a shim with error codes and is not used as a runtime dependency; v8 components and VHS do not enter the artifact. Real short-clip regression covers MP4, HLS, and DASH decoding, quality-level selection is verified against the HLS engine, and DASH does not claim manual quality-level capability. Production cutover and the ledger are not part of the dependency takeover worktree.

The Peach player skin decorates the light DOM of Video.js 10 directly: the right-hand control group sticks to the right edge of the control bar with an automatic left margin; the official time slider takes the full 18 px hit area, the 6 px track and the single 12 px thumb are drawn separately, and hover and focus enlarge the thumb. The browser regression `frontend/e2e/player-skin.test.ts` uses a synthetic short clip to verify desktop, 390×844 mobile, progress clicks, and entering and leaving fullscreen; no player dependency is added.

字幕文件加载复用 `subtitles.to_webvtt`，接受 SRT、ASS、SSA、VTT，兼容已有的编码探测；4 MiB 以内的文件经 `/api/subtitles/convert` 转换后挂到当前 video 的 TextTrack，关闭播放器释放 Blob URL。字幕列表、CC 开关与外观选项按用户截图和 [YouTube 官方说明](https://support.google.com/youtube/answer/100078?hl=en) 组织；字体、字号、颜色、不透明度、背景、窗口与边缘样式按视频保存到服务端文件，时间轴仍由浏览器驱动。自有字幕层绘制文字与背景，Chromium／WebKit 的原生字幕容器隐藏。字幕文件不写 ledger，不保存到媒体目录。

字幕面板复用播放器的选项行、描边勾选、设置齿轮和悬停表面；多行轨道名按内容增高并保留上下留白。在线入口按当前作品的番号或片名自动检索，结果保留在当前播放会话，失败可重试。本地和在线字幕可同时保留为独立轨道，当前选择互斥；新加载轨道自动选中。轨道、选择和外观由同一份视频记忆恢复。详情原生 `dialog` 只处理自身的 `cancel`，文件输入框冒泡的取消事件保留详情与字幕菜单（[浏览器事件说明](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/API/HTMLInputElement/cancel_event)）。播放器模块加载期间只显示海报，原生控制条仅用于加载失败后的播放回退。桌面与手机回归覆盖首次加载、文件取消、菜单 Escape、结果加载及字幕外观。

Online Chinese subtitles use only [SubtitleCat](https://www.subtitlecat.com), reusing the existing httpx and BeautifulSoup dependencies. Source selection refers to the [PotPlayer JAV Chinese subtitle plugin](https://github.com/736368217/PotplayerJavChineseSubs_subtitlecat/tree/0b569fdc3bd2cb27fca2140858ad77bf6d7ddcb8) (MIT; only the public protocol and language selection are referenced, no code is copied). Code search reuses the normalization, query variants, and identity comparison of `catalog_rules`, combined with `feeds.scan_code` to read result titles; only the same release identity is kept, and details are then read six per page. Titles support compact codes, language and version suffixes; date separators and unconfirmed release prefixes are kept distinct. Title search keeps the user's keywords. Only results that already have a `zh-CN` / `zh-TW` file are listed, at most the first 60 are checked, and paging is triggered by the user; no generated translation is triggered. Downloads are limited to fixed HTTPS hosts, subtitle paths, and 4 MiB, and reuse the existing transcoder. On 2026-10-18 all 20 fixed public codes obtained Chinese files; this small sample does not represent whole-site or library coverage and does not prove translation accuracy. The UI note may say it contains machine translation and does not claim human translation. ASSRT is not integrated; JavSub.ai had 8 Chinese entries in the same sample, 7 requiring purchase and 1 marked free, but downloads returned 403, so it is not used as an online source. No credentials needed, no added dependency, and no write to the ledger or media directory.

## Tiered diagnostics

Startup forensics reuses the Python 3.14 standard library's `sys.excepthook`, `threading.excepthook`, `sys.unraisablehook`, `logging.Handler`, `faulthandler`, and `zipfile` (PSF license, no extra install size). `error_reporting` loads before configuration and third-party dependencies, and `support_bundle` collects only selected logs and system fields; Peach keeps the redaction, fallback directory, local permissions, and report-content policy. The existing diagnostic report is used for page and runtime exports. Temporary directories and standalone subprocesses cover no console, import failure, thread exceptions, fallback directory, corrupted configuration, and redaction.

Optional remote diagnostics reuses the official [sentry-sdk 2.72.0](https://github.com/getsentry/sentry-python/tree/2.72.0) (MIT, maintained, supports Python 3.14); the pinned source was checked with `opensrc path pypi:sentry-sdk`. The SDK handles background HTTP, TLS, the Sentry envelope, and server-side rate limiting; Peach handles local choice, technical-field projection, aggregation of same-kind errors, and a per-machine daily cap. Version statistics and error diagnostics share one default-checked switch, and the SDK is not constructed when it is off; the SDK's global auto-integrations and its tracing, replay, logging, session, and metric collection are all disabled. A narrow override of `HttpTransport._send_envelope` rechecks the local choice version right before sending and strips local transport metadata; it is an internal interface of the pinned version, and upgrades must pass a real SDK envelope regression. An in-memory receiver with no real egress checks the final bytes, queue invalidation after disabling, and that a network outage does not affect local forensics. The only new dependency is `sentry-sdk`, reusing the already declared urllib3 / certifi; size is determined by the release build. A hand-written Sentry protocol or a Workers collection backend was not adopted, to avoid maintaining transport, deduplication, and a viewing UI separately. No real ledger writes.

故障频率复用已固定的 `filelock`、`fsutil.atomic_write_text` 和 SDK 事件标签，保留 Peach 的同意版本、技术投影、跨进程计数与每日批次策略。`diagnostic_frequency` 保存有界技术样本及待提交增量，首次、每小时和正常退出通过同一 SDK 提交；关闭会清除聚合。Sentry 默认事件数统计批次，不会将自定义次数标签自动求和；汇总按事件的 `occurrence_day` 与 `occurrence_count` 计算。`reporting_transport` 复用官方 `HttpTransport` 的 `_handle_response`、`_handle_request_error` 与队列丢弃回调，取得 HTTP 状态且不重复实现请求、TLS 或重试；固定内部接口以真实 SDK 和模拟 HTTP 回归。`reporting_delivery` 只保存本机有限回执；SDK 入队、HTTP 接收与接收端留存分别解释。用途、环境与公开构建号复用 Sentry 原生字段，测试在同一传输边界拦截外发。未开启 SDK metrics 或新增收集服务：独立指标通道会增加同意边界、额度与交付口径。计数只覆盖已筛选技术故障，发送及保留限制见 [诊断核验](DIAGNOSTICS.md)。没有新增 Python 依赖或真实 ledger 写入。

Windows connection cleanup reuses the [Proactor object protocol in CPython 3.14.7](https://github.com/python/cpython/blob/v3.14.7/Lib/asyncio/proactor_events.py) (PSF license, no new dependency). It precisely identifies `WinError 10054` in the pure-standard-library cleanup callback and only completes the socket close, server deregistration, and closed state, without notifying the protocol again and without globally modifying standard-library classes or replacing the event loop. It uses the private interfaces `_sock`, `_server._detach`, and `_called_connection_lost`, which must be re-verified when Python is upgraded.
