Real sockets and `asyncio.Server` with synthetic faults verify resource release; a failure in the cleanup itself keeps the original exception chain and is reported. 33 loopback experiments did not reproduce the original race timing, so the client is not concluded to be the source. The protocol category is written only to the local log; remote filtering for sources, tasks, media, and browsers reuses existing domain state, see [diagnostics verification](DIAGNOSTICS.md#remote-error-filtering-review).

Library and taste cohort research reuses `LedgerDatabase.read_connection`, canonical codes, `entities.male_performer_ids`, `taste_evidence.era/length_band`, the entity favorites snapshot, `error_reporting.redact`, and the pinned Sentry in-memory attachment API. `library_research` handles local deduplication, user signals, manual tags, and grouped rating histograms, which is Peach's domain projection; it reads no browsing history and writes no preference cache. The JSON uses the Python 3.14 standard-library `gzip` (PSF, no new dependency) with a fixed filename, and the gzip header carries neither a source filename nor a generation time. A 10,000-work projection restores byte for byte, and the real SDK's in-memory transport verifies the final attachment, the allowlist, and the consent gate. Sentry attachment reception reuses the official SDK (MIT); no anonymous relay or separate aggregation service is introduced, and this contract keeps per-submission projections and discloses the linkage risk. For fields, size limits, and acceptance see [Library and taste cohort research](DIAGNOSTICS.md#library-and-taste-cohort-research).

Code display uniformly reuses `frontend/src/react/components/code-block.tsx`: BoardUI's already pinned Button, semantic colors, typography, and corner radius, plus the existing overlay scrollbar; it includes a language label, full-text copy, a focusable scroll area, clipboard-failure feedback, and optional on-screen truncation. It is used for library upload JSON, CloudDrive2 TOML, and custom-source JSON. On 2026-10-19 the [official BoardUI catalog](https://www.boardui.com/components) and `boardui list` were checked, and the public registry has no standalone Code Block, so Peach's wrapper is composed from existing primitives; no syntax-parsing dependency is introduced just for read-only text, and no Pro template is copied. Component tests cover full-text copy and HTML text safety, and layout is verified in desktop and narrow-screen browsers. No new dependency, and vendored upstream source files stay unchanged.

Entity favorites reuse `profiles.read_settings` and `library_favorites.favorite_entities`, which centrally handle canonical identity, redirects, tag selection, count limits, and deduplication, shared by the page and cohort research. Named filters and HTTP presentation are handled by the web layer; the data layer does not depend on web modules.

`src/peach/diagnostics.py` uniformly produces the CLI and local API reports; it reuses `settings_file`, the read-only migration check of `health`, `FFmpegResolver`, the access policy, and the mount snapshot. The database uses SQLite `mode=ro`; write and port checks use brief standard-library probes, adding no runtime dependency.

The application lifecycle reuses the read-only ledger and migration check of `health.inspect_database`; only when the ledger is readable and its structure is `current` are tasks restored and follow catch-up, subscriptions, push discovery, download reconciliation, and library aggregate warm-up started. When the ledger is missing, blank, corrupted, or migrations are not ready, the health and configuration entries are kept and only a status hint without paths or SQL is recorded; the ledger is neither created nor migrated, and the service is restarted once the ledger is ready. `checks.background` of `/healthz?ready=1` reflects whether this lifecycle started the ledger background tasks; an instance whose start was deferred stays not-ready until restarted. Configuration and tunnel tests reuse the standard-library `unittest.assertNoLogs` to check for unexpected background errors, fault injection uses `assertLogs` to check expected reasons, and lifecycle regression uses the real migration template of `support.ledger.fresh_ledger`. No new dependency or real ledger write.

Aggregate warm-up receives the shutdown signal through `asyncio.Event`, service exit waits for in-flight `asyncio.to_thread` queries to release their connections, and the remaining aggregates are skipped. When shutdown begins, `checks.background` is `false`; a temporary SQLite connection and a thread-event regression verify the ordering between service end and ledger release.

Browser network-failure forensics reuses Chromium's (BSD) NetLog and the existing Playwright launch entry point; `PEACH_E2E_NET_LOG_DIR` enables `--log-net-log`, with the capture mode fixed to [`HeavilyRedacted`](https://chromium.googlesource.com/chromium/src/net/+/master/log/net_log_capture_mode.h), keeping the network event fields allowed to be public and not collecting URLs, hosts, IPs, or request headers. The log is finished when the browser closes normally and is saved by CI together with TAP; a failed request still fails the test, and `ERR_NO_BUFFER_SPACE` is neither retried nor filtered. The code-sample fixture installs its response before the first navigation, opens the target page directly, and closes the context if waiting for the main body fails. No new dependency or machine resource quota.

The diagnostic fields and hint organization of sakuramedia `a10fcef8` (GPL-3.0) were checked and used only as product reference, with no source copied. OpenAver `8cc17e50` (MIT) has an HTTP health probe that is insufficient to express per-item status. [fastapi-health 0.4.0](https://pypi.org/project/fastapi-health/) (MIT) treated the status dict as truthy in an offline trial; the CLI, tiering rules, and redaction would still need to be handled by this project, so it was not introduced.

The diagnostics page reuses the same report, TanStack Query, and the BoardUI settings composites. Library health follows the count→list entry of javm `dd0e6b62` (MIT), using Peach's assets, cover keys, and processing log; the work list reuses the existing TanStack Table, BoardUI Table, sorting and pagination, cross-page selection, and copy actions, showing title, filename, code, media library, full path, duration, and size. A single group report loads at most 500 items, and the full count and the number loaded are shown separately. Identity fields are for the local diagnostics page only, and a shared diagnostic bundle continues to exclude the library list. Source evidence reuses the successfully landed sources of `performer_profile_followup` and `scraping_access.cooldown_state`, and does not use HTTP 200 as content health. No new dependency; a local read-only trial checked the existing logs and success records.

System diagnostics, as a configuration section, reuses the configuration tabs and deep links. Checks and source status reuse BoardUI `Chip`; each source session reuses BoardUI `SettingsCard`, with the shared `SiteMark` reading the verified local favicon and providing a failure fallback, and icons keep a square canvas; recent parsing, past success, and no record are shown separately. The session list shows metadata platforms and connection services; studio pages and the routing that picks a site per studio are not listed separately. Optional sources outside the default chain are shown when they have an actual check or cooldown record. Icon provenance is in `resources/site-marks/ORIGIN.md`. No new dependency or ledger write.

## Resource indexer

`resource_search.py` uses `parse_capabilities` and `parse_torznab` of Torznab 0.2.1 (MIT, Python ≥3.10, supports the project's Python 3.14). The source is pinned to tag v0.2.1, and the project is still labeled Alpha; an offline XML POC covers caps, GUID magnets, size, seeders, peers, and duplicate tags. The PyPI package adds one direct dependency, with requests already a transitive dependency; acceptance against a real indexer needs a user-configured endpoint.

Requests reuse HTTPX 0.28.1. The SDK's own requests layer has no response-size limit and its exceptions may carry the full URL, so only its parsers are used. Peach handles the two requests per source (caps and search), at most six torrents, a 2 MiB response cap, at most four sources per round, a 45-second budget, disabled redirects, redacted errors, rejection of XML entity declarations, and size, seeder, blacklist, quality ranking, and at most five candidates. Title codes reuse `feeds.scan_code`, and identity and Chinese-subtitle and uncensored edition detection reuse `catalog_rules`. Credentials reuse `CredentialStore` with no cross-machine sync field registered; changing the endpoint does not carry over a saved API key.

It was checked against the [Torznab 1.3 specification](https://torznab.github.io/spec-1.3-draft/torznab/Specification-v1.3.html) and the FC2 query, magnet-field fallback, and partial-failure boundaries of SakuraMedia `torznab.py` (GPL-3.0, revision `9c6a31915c9a364c9d6717798575daf9445916bd`). SakuraMedia is coupled to its ORM and is not a runtime dependency. Candidates are filled into the magnet box of the cloud-download popover, and after user confirmation go through `submit_offline_download`.

OneJAV and Free JAV Torrent give only torrent links. Such entries fetch the torrent through the download proxy of the same origin as the indexer, decoded by bencode2 0.3.35 (MIT, Python ≥3.10, with a cp314 Windows wheel and a pure-Python wheel), and then the SHA-1 of the info dictionary is computed. Evidence: the hash computed from a real sukebei torrent matches the Torznab `infohash`; the Prowlarr proxy returns `application/x-bittorrent` for OneJAV and Free JAV Torrent. Both bencode2 and fastbencode 0.3.11 (Apache-2.0) reject dictionaries with out-of-order keys, so re-encoding gives back the original. bencode2 was chosen because it has a pure-Python fallback. torf (GPL-3.0) does far more than needed, and bencode.py (BitTorrent license) and bencodepy (no updates since 2015) are not adopted.

This is an implementation lookup table: which ready-made implementation carries each capability, and which part Peach itself owns. Before adding, restoring, or rewriting code, follow `.claude/skills/peach-reuse-first/SKILL.md` and check this file, the current tree, Git history, and mature external implementations first; an old filename not existing does not mean the capability is missing, and for successor relationships see "Removed legacy implementations and current successors".

The exact versions of installed dependencies are maintained by the [Python manifest](../pyproject.toml), the [Python lock file](../uv.lock), the [frontend manifest](../frontend/package.json), and the [static dependency manifest](../package.json). This page records purpose, license, and trade-offs; evidence versions and commit numbers represent only the corresponding evidence.

## Mount reachability

`mount_reachability.py` follows OpenAver's `core/source_reachability.py` (MIT, Copyright 2026 peace; pinned revision `8cc17e50453d9f69a81f5fee1a072df80f7aab73`, 2026-10-01) in adopting a normal 600-second and abnormal 60-second cadence, two-failure confirmation, and in-flight deduplication of timeouts. Peach implements this algorithm independently without copying upstream source; it uses Python 3.14 standard-library threads, events, and Futures, adding no dependency. Peach's path mapping and first directory read belong to `platform`, and the source interface, health summary, and tray share a pure in-memory snapshot; at most four directory probes are in flight, and the background thread does not block service exit.

The reference implementation's UNC TCP 445 probe was not adopted: port connectivity cannot prove that a shared directory is readable. A regular file also cannot serve as a media root, and `NotADirectoryError` is listed separately; permission denied, directory not found, and other read failures are reported separately. APScheduler is already used for scheduled tasks, but its thread executor cannot cancel a blocking filesystem call; this feature drives scheduling with a stop event and keeps directory probes that have not yet returned. The task center writes to the ledger and is not used for this read-only probe.

## Board UI and numeric settings

Site-wide `title` tooltips share the native Popover, Range, and existing scroll-anchor criteria through `board-tooltip.ts`; fully visible text is not repeated, while truncated original text, icon names, and supplementary notes can be viewed. Elements with no layout box of their own are positioned by their visible children, and text links are positioned by the actual text; popover hints stay inside their dialog. The player continues to share the Video.js tooltip with shortcut keys, with no new dependency.

The follow image masonry reuses the browser's [CSS Grid](https://www.w3.org/TR/css-grid-1/#auto-placement-algo), `ResizeObserver`, and the existing React, with no new dependency. `use-masonry-grid.ts` fixes column ownership by list order and generates grid rows by card boundaries; the number of tracks grows with the card count, and image dimensions arriving and continued paging only affect the later positions of their own columns. Cards keep DOM order, keyboard entry, and the selection range. After an image loads its intrinsic width and height are used immediately; fixed-ratio card faces use `cover`, and the detail shows the full image.

The existing implementation and history `57c42c05` and `91f2c5f0` provide size placeholders and the learning interface. The [column balancing](https://www.w3.org/TR/css-multicol-1/#filling-columns) of CSS multi-column does not satisfy fixed column ownership. The external candidate [Masonry 4.2.2](https://github.com/desandro/masonry/blob/v4.2.2/package.json) (MIT, browser DOM library, depends on `get-size` and `outlayer`) would require maintaining another set of positioning and [layout calls](https://masonry.desandro.com/layout) after images load, and is not adopted. Peach maintains only column ownership and measurement, leaving positioning to native Grid; desktop and 390×844 cases cover no dimensions, rejection of read-only write-back, delayed tall images, and continued paging.

Which shared pieces carry page controls and interactions: for the component mapping, Board upstream evidence, and licenses see [BoardUI adaptation](BOARD_UI.md).

- Page sections reuse `BoardTabs` and the neutral-fill selected state of the legacy navigation; the display-mode switch uses `Segmented`. The title sits at the top left of the content, before navigation and filters. The JAV library reuses the directory, and the skeletons for recommended and wanted come from `jav-skeleton.ts` and share the container, column count, and cover ratio with the page. Cloud-drive marks reuse `SourceMark`, `MEDIA_SOURCE_ICONS`, and `SourceLabel`, adding no icon dependency.
- JAV recommendations match the entity identity and canonical names in the taste through the `performer_refs` of `/api/feeds/discoveries`; references come only from the existing `entities.resolve_entity`, and on a name collision no identity is selected, the source original text is not rewritten, and the frontend maintains no name mapping table.

- External-link buttons reuse BoardUI `ButtonLink`, call sites add `data-button-link`, and the site-wide text-link rule excludes it with `:where`, keeping the style priority of entity-page controls. Browser acceptance uses one renderer process, an in-process audio service, and synthetic audio output, leaving a resource budget for the demo server and the media process; the entry points remain `scripts/test.ps1` / `scripts/test.sh`.

- 功能性动效由 `frontend/src/react/components/use-moving-surface.ts`、`modal-frame.tsx`、`selection-dock.tsx` 承担，用现有 Motion 14.0.0（MIT）与 React Aria Components 1.22.0（Apache-2.0）。Fluid Functionalism 只作行为与短程参数参考（[取证](reference-snapshots/fluid-functionalism.md)），不装其 Registry（额外的 Radix、字体与上下文不合 BoardUI 组合）；Peach 保留单一选中底板、键盘即时反馈与原有主题。
- 首页新作与实体资料页共用 `/api/entity/shapes` 和 `feedNewSkeletonHtml`；`home.feed` 按新作列表同一套未入库、未忽略与合集条件判定，有内容才留位；同步设置先于最终横条和网格，同形骨架复用节点。
- 首页、人物页和 JAV「发现」的新作头像共用 `feedIdentity`、`web_catalog.entity_ref` 与 `avatarInner`，携带实体图片版本和人脸取景，多人最多叠三枚，图片缺失时显示首字。已建档人物的头像通往规范资料页，不新增实体、头像采集或运行依赖。
- 作者别名管理复用 `@tanstack/react-table` 9.3.1（MIT）和 BoardUI `Table`、`DataTableFrame`、`DataTableFooter`、`AuthorAvatar` 与既有别名 API。两张表均支持全集排序、分页和跨页选择；待合并表以平台别名为行身份，已保存表以规范创作者键为行身份。批量合并与移除读当前清单的所选行，串行执行并报告部分失败；勾写 `slot={null}`。`frontend/test/react/follow-alias.test.tsx` 验证清单刷新、勾选、合并、保存与移除，不新增依赖。扫描与采集方式收在 BoardUI `Button` 与 React Aria `Popover` 下拉里，行外观复用 `menu-styles.ts`（见 `frontend/src/react/boardui/ORIGIN.md`）。
- 表格自适应列宽复用已固定的 `react-aria-components` 1.22.1（Apache-2.0）`ResizableTableContainer` 与 `Column` 的 `1fr`、像素宽度及调整回调，沿用库内 `TableColumnLayout` 的尺寸分配和键盘调整，不添加布局依赖。Peach 保留各表的最小宽度、操作列默认值、偏好持久化、重置和列显示规则；文件名、路径及地址的中间省略继续复用 `middle-truncate.ts` 的 `ResizeObserver`。
- 数据管理的孤儿记录与链接结果复用 `react/components/data-table.tsx`，由同版 TanStack Table 处理全集排序、行选择与最后一步分页，外观复用 BoardUI `Table` 与 `DataTableFrame`。七张表共用 `table-controls.tsx` 的分页底栏；关注列表保留卡片与表格共享的排序状态，其余表的排序控件复用 `DataTableSort`。工具栏共享 `styles.css` 的 `data-responsive-toolbar`、`data-toolbar-heading` 与 `data-toolbar-actions` 分组，使用 CSS Flexbox 按可用宽度整体换行；列设置与排序方向各有独立原子组，不新增布局依赖。默认每页 20 条，批量动作在选中后使用 `SelectionDock`。`use-table-selection.ts` 在同页表格间互斥选择，保持一份批量栏。孤儿记录批量删除复用 `/api/batch`；链接清理的可选 `ids` 必须属于 `check_id` 对应的失效清单，只重验和处理所选链接，未选行保留。组件回归与临时数据库验证跨页选择、清单刷新、确认和删除范围；不新增依赖。
- 关注列表分页用 `pagination.ts` 的页码范围与边界裁剪（React 与遗留层共用这份纯函数）；默认视图按创作者组，表格按来源。关注列表与 JAV 订阅源表格复用 `@tanstack/react-table` 9.3.1（MIT，https://github.com/TanStack/table ，按 ADR-0031「前端基础库」引入），兼容 React 18+ 与 Node 20+；传递依赖为 `table-core`、`react-store`、`store` 和 `use-sync-external-store`，精确版本由前端锁文件记录。按官方 [React 迁移指南](https://tanstack.com/table/latest/docs/framework/react/guide/migrating) 使用 `useTable`、`tableFeatures` 和带 features 类型的 `createColumnHelper().columns()`，注册分页、行选择、排序与列显隐状态，单元格使用 `getVisibleCells`。Peach 保留共用排序比较器、来源 ID（`getRowId`）、跨视图勾选集合、页码裁剪和批量写入策略；排序作用在全集，分页只切最后一步，频率、日期与数量按原始值排序。`frontend/test/react/follow-manage.test.tsx` 验证排序、取消选择、跨页跨视图勾选、批量写与清单变化。
- 馆藏表格列宽复用 React Aria Components 1.22.1（Apache-2.0）的 `ResizableTableContainer` 与 `ColumnResizer`，按官方 [Table 列宽调整](https://react-aria.adobe.com/Table#column-resizing) 组合现有 BoardUI Table；鼠标、触摸与键盘调整由该组件处理。列隐藏复用 TanStack Table 9.3.1 的 `columnVisibilityFeature` 与现有 Popover、Checkbox；隐藏列仍可参与服务端排序。`RatingStars` 共用馆藏表格、馆藏详情和 JAV 来源评分的图形与颜色，支持交互星级与只读小数填充；评分取值共用 `rating-values.ts`，表格写入复用 `/api/feedback`、`useAction`、查询缓存与操作回执。Peach 维护各列默认值和上下限、浏览器偏好保存与重置；右侧弹性空白列承接剩余宽度，不参与偏好保存。使用现有依赖，无新增包；回归覆盖长名称、缺图、宽屏两种布局、列宽与显示偏好、隐藏列排序、评分保存与取消、失败重试及撤销。
- 所有数据表格通过 `DataTableFrame` 复用 React Aria 的列宽容器、`wireTableScroller` 和覆盖式滚动条，`TableColumnControls` 负责列显示菜单和重置，`useTablePreferences` 按表隔离浏览器列宽与显示偏好。各表只声明列名、默认宽度、上下限和必留身份列，TanStack 的 `columnVisibilityFeature` 负责实际显隐，隐藏字段保留排序入口。拖动沿用 `wireHorizontalScroller` 的阈值、点击拦截与卸载清理，表格关闭滚轮转向、渐隐和回弹。按钮、链接、勾选框及列宽手柄自行处理指针；拖动不触发行选择。馆藏的勾选框由显式多选状态控制，取消动作共用 `setSelectMode(false, true)`。
- 表头选择复用 `TableSelectionHeader`，只统计对应范围内仍存在且可选的行；关注与 JAV 订阅选择当前页，别名和通用管理表选择筛选全集。链接命中范围由共用表格样式和 `SourceLink` 限定为内容宽度；站点图标复用 `SourceIcon`、`SiteLabel` 与缓存端点。
- 别名改名复用现有别名端点和 `FollowStore.rename_author_group`；实体名称更新从既有端点提取为 `entities.prefer_entity_name`，共用名称碰撞检查、原名别名和兼容字段同步。别名组、关联实体与作品字段使用同一 SQLite 事务，冲突整体回滚；测试仅操作临时数据库，无迁移和新依赖。
- 折叠正文复用 `setCollapseRegionOpen`、`setCollapseOpen` 和 `growCollapse`；React 外置按钮通过 `CollapseRegion` 保留挂载内容，原生详情使用 `Disclosure`。`CollapseChevron` 与侧栏、教程的稳定箭头元素使用现有 `--motion-swap`；高度维持共享 Collapse 的 200ms 过渡，快速反向点击由同一代次保护处理，减少动态效果沿用全站样式。
- 排序方向的 React `IconSwap` 与 HTML `iconSwapHtml` 共用 `.iconswap` 状态、模糊与缩放参数；两枚图标保持挂载，BoardUI 按钮通过直属 `data-icon-state` 驱动稳定图标节点，不修改 vendor。换批忙态复用 `#i-shuffle` 的两条描线及既定 2.2s 周期，`FilterIconButton` 仅为此保留图标，其余动作继续使用 Spinner。JAV 标题复用 Stage 的 `revealTexts`，不新增触发计时器或动效依赖。
- 窄屏筛选框共用 `filterScrollState()`、现有滚动帧调度和原生 sticky；同一方向累计 8px 再切换吸顶，保留文档占位与键盘可达性。
- 搜索玻璃用 `glideEase()` 的采样弹簧，`frontend/src/application/search-morph.js` 只管视口边界与轮廓关键帧。
- 横排滚到头的回弹是 `wireHorizontalScroller` 内的 `edgeBounce`，头像排、厂牌排、新作排、筛选条共用；transitions.dev 的 43 条配方里没有（2026-09-23 核对）。越界位移借 UIScrollView 的橡皮筋公式 `(1 - 1/(x·c/d + 1))·d`，c = 0.55，与 use-gesture（MIT）的 `rubberband` 同一条，回弹走 `--spring-pane`。只抄公式不引依赖：拖动与滚轮归属已由 `wireHorizontalScroller` 判定。新作排自动滚动是同文件的 `wireAutoScroll`。
- React 横向导航通过 `useHorizontalScroller` 绑定上述共享行为，适用于页签、分段控件、筛选栏和首字跳转；`data-horizontal-scroll` 隐藏原生滚动条，左右渐隐由实际溢出方向决定。容器和子项尺寸、文本及增删变化都会重算，竖向分类列关闭绑定；表格和代码块继续使用覆盖式滑块，不新增依赖。
- 详情影院模式通过 `data-theater` 改变已有容器的 overflow，共享滚动区观察器同步接管新滚动轴。`data-skeleton` 与 `data-skeleton-tier` 期间隐藏原生滚动条并移除旧轨道，内容恢复后由同一观察器重新接管；首页与资料页共用的新作占位在整条横向容器上标记 `data-skeleton="feed/new"`。保留原占位尺寸与排版，不为占位另建滚动组件。
- 管理页头及 `manageHeaderSkeletonHtml` 共用一级导航、面包屑、页面标题的顺序，页内分类放在标题之后；管理条保持跨模块导航，面包屑保留上级链接与当前页语义。
- 浮层筛选首页是页面内组件 `catalog-filter`，资料页是 `entity-filter`，共用 `FilterGlassRows` 两排、交集条与排序键，滑动玻璃走 `use-view-glide.ts`。外框管玻璃与吸顶，壳管查询状态和取数，岛只画、动作回壳，按下态在发请求前由壳经 `updateManagedRoute` 推到；身份与观看状态的组合沿用 `/api/items`。
- `web/board.css` 共用正式页面结构。首启、登录与错误三页是独立页面包 `/dist/peach-pages.js` 里的 React 页，服务端只吐 `web_entry.page_shell()` 那一张薄壳，外框是 `frontend/src/react/pages/auth-card.tsx`，SPA 外壳之外的新页面挂进同一个包。
- `frontend/src/number-setting.ts` 共用带单位输入、可选 Switch、整数边界和锚定错误提示；关闭保留上次合法值，异步读取后切换也恢复实际值，保存归调用方。
- 筛选内层的标签胶囊是 `FilterPill`，换一批、排序键与交集条是 `entity-filter-page.tsx` 导出的同一组组件，页面各自提供查询键与读数；`collectionHeaderHtml` 只剩资料页骨架那一排读数。横向行的拖动、滚轮、渐隐与卸载清理归 `wireHorizontalScroller` 同一个生命周期。
- 选择范围与工具条用 `frontend/src/selection.ts`；馆藏、关注与复核保持各自身份、可见顺序、默认选择与写入权限，批量失败项的保留归业务。
- 灰色页签与管理导航使用 `frontend/src/react/components/pill-tabs.tsx`，共用从 BoardUI `PillTabList` 适配的测量与过渡；React Aria 集合通过 Portal 承载装饰层。路由、键盘、纵横布局和选中项仍由调用方维护；减少动态效果时关闭位移过渡。来源与适配边界见 `reference-snapshots/boardui-settings-tabs.md`。
- React 设置分区用 `frontend/src/react/settings/section.tsx`：`Section` 给标题、卡片与表单外壳，`Footer`、`Note`、`ErrorText`、`FactList`、`Progress`、`Disclosure` 补齐 BoardUI 注册表没有的部分。`Rows` 与 `FactList` 标记共享行组，分隔线由行与相邻组边界共同绘制，左侧内缩、右侧通到卡片边缘；设置弹层遵循同一规则，底部操作栏使用通栏线。`service-preferences.tsx` 组合缩略图与订阅采集参数，继续使用已有设置、关注计划和缩略图 API。`use-action.ts` 的 `useAction` 管提交互斥、卸载取消与原位错误，`busy-props.ts` 的 `busyProps` 写忙态；媒体目录行（`folder-rows.tsx`）与密码加确认两格（`password-pair.tsx`）由配置页与首启页共用；密码校验与服务端回执归各分区，不自动重试写入。口味读取、封面采集、amane 桥重建、关注检查与查找共用 `frontend/src/react/background-job.ts` 的 `useBackgroundJob`。
- 增量列表用 `catalog-grid/catalog-grid-page.tsx` 的 `LoadMore`：请求互斥、失败留原位重试、卸载即停；目录、资料页照片墙与关注页注入读取和可用条件。首页页码在读取成功后推进，照片沿用随机种子，关注合并分组；显式页码用 `pagination.ts`。
- 在图上框一块由 `frontend/src/crop-geometry.ts`（纯算术）加 `react/crop/crop-frame.tsx`（四块压暗、一个可拖可缩的框）承担，换头像与裁封面共用；坐标一律是源图像素、右下开区间。不引 `react-image-crop`：换到的只有把手样式，代价是一条依赖和它自己的坐标约定。裁封面通过 Application 的媒体域动作连接 React 裁剪组件（ADR-0031）。
- 后台任务用 `watchJob`、`followJobProgress` 默认面板与 `jobActivityHtml` 的真实计数／未知总量显示，关注、来源扫描、链接检查和扫描采集共用；业务保留启动、终态回执和结果面板，不新增轮询循环。任务本体是 `jobs.BackgroundJob`，不加队列或调度依赖；浏览器由 `frontend/src/jobs.ts` 跟进，刷新后经读接口恢复。可恢复的采集任务由本机恢复记录重放，其他任务只保留终态；`BackgroundJob.update(job_id)` 报阶段与计数，查询不重新执行任务。
- 遗留层反馈控件 `noteHtml`、`progressHtml`、`gaugeHtml`、`projectBannerHtml` 与 `wireContextCard` 集中在共享 UI 模块；信息卡片用原生 Popover 与现有锚定菜单定位，不加浮层依赖。证据见 `reference-snapshots/vercel-geist-note-progress-switch-analytics.md`。
- 资源核对复用 `web_resource_sync` 的目录枚举、离线跳过、写前逐条复验与 BackgroundJob，覆盖 local、115、PikPak；失效记录的永久删除复用 `web_batch.purge_assets`，文件仍在盘上的行进 `blocked`。确认弹层复用 Fieldset、Note、Toast 与 confirmModal：失败留在弹层，危险动作初始聚焦取消，忙态阻止重入与关闭；不引入另一套对话框库。
- 新界面走 BoardUI／React（ADR-0031），遗留层共用的 Geist 控件随页面迁走，不引入 Geist React 运行时。覆盖式滚动条 `attachOverlayScrollbar`（滑块不占宽度）、Collapse 的 `wireCollapse`／`setCollapseOpen`、Geist Select 的 `selectFieldHtml`／`wireSelectField`、来源站标 `MEDIA_SOURCE_ICONS`，以及模板、骨架、确认框等其余共用控件的唯一实现在 `frontend/src/ui-kit/`，Application 与 React 页面直接引用共享源码，随 `/dist/peach-app.js` 发出一份；独立页面包只打入用到的控件；覆盖式滚动条与 `.ui-geist-scroller`（只给两端渐隐）可叠加；整页异步重绘复用导航代际隔离。
- 局部等待复用 `skeleton-sheen`：绝对定位的微光由占位元素自己的 `relative` 或 `absolute` 定位，并在该元素内裁切。JAV 评论占位在折叠正文内预留结构，首次展开按占位高度测量；请求仍按展开触发，收起时不标记等待态。
- 图标按钮统一清除浏览器内边距并居中 SVG，不覆盖业务显隐。Remix Icon 由 `vendor_web_dependencies.mjs` 生成设置导航 symbol；随机按钮保留原有双路径动画。
- 宽字形的比例统一取自 `core/icon-geometry.ts`；遗留模板、React 控件、设置和实体页共用。控件按位置决定高度，Aa 等字形按比例定宽，同级图标以实际轮廓核验。
- 统计页与口味页的柱状、径向、雷达图复用 EvilCharts Recharts 分支（MIT，源码逐字复制进 `frontend/src/react/evilcharts/`，依赖 `recharts`、`motion`、`clsx`），单系列柱状图统一走 `frontend/src/react/charts/bar-card.tsx` 的 `BarCard`，悬停浮层统一走 `charts/chart-tip.tsx` 的 `ChartTip`。星期 × 小时与每日热力图是 `charts/heat-card.tsx` 的 `ActivityHeat`，统计页播放时间与口味页浏览活跃共用；EvilCharts 没有这类图（ADR-0076）。
- `frontend/src/react/taste/taste.ts` 使用 d3-sankey（BSD-3-Clause）及其类型包计算来源网站到创作者线索的流向。布局依赖不读取浏览历史；Peach 提供去重聚合值并负责隐私边界。分发许可随 `web/vendor/d3-LICENSE.txt` 保留。

复核用 Checkbox、Button、Badge、Select 与 `/api/review/decision`，默认勾选、沿用馆藏页 Shift 连选；支持跨组通过／拒绝与无歧义的共同来源选择。按候选数量、来源组合或字段分组，每项只出现一次，切组保留选择；成功移出、失败保留，离开页面即停。整页在 `frontend/src/react/review/`，一条队列一个 `queryKey`，判定后改缓存不重取。React Aria Components 1.22.1 的 `Radio`／`Checkbox` 继续承载原生焦点与方向键；共用 `base.css` 将隐藏输入框的定位归于所属标签，来源切换只滚动卡片正文，分段单选、媒体库图标选择和复选框使用同一定位基线，不新增依赖。批准回执在同一事务中读取实际资产版本，缓存只推进仍持有该次写入前版本的候选；逐项与批量提交共用缓存中的并发凭据。身份候选用创作者页、作品详情与 revealSource 核对样本；来源图与缺图占位共用 220px 预览区，样本支持无缩略图作品，回收站不参与。

Local NFO pairing reuses `library_nfo`, the code and multi-part recognition of `catalog_rules`, the standard-library ElementTree, and the existing metadata fill-blank flow. The same-name file and `movie.nfo` conventions of the [Kodi NFO docs](https://kodi.wiki/view/NFO_files/Movies) were checked on 2026-10-17; only the public format is referenced, with no external source copied. Peach keeps multi-part sharing for the same code, same-name file priority, rejection of multiple matches, content-code conflict checks, and input safety limits. The whole Kodi media center was not brought in as a dependency; the standard library ships with Python 3.14, adding no size or license. After reading the NFO, source caches and missing-caches are reused to check online fields; `requires_review` follows the candidate to keep the override-review requirement, and manual decisions save the displayed candidate identity, see ADR-0108. Isolated media and ledger regression covers shared NFOs, different works mixed in one folder, a wrong NFO continuing with online completion, fill-blank versus override routing, and version receipts for consecutive approvals; no real-feedback NFO samples have been obtained yet, and no new dependency.

## Local settings and uninstall

- The Peach proxy reuses HTTPX's `trust_env`, `proxy`, and the local CredentialStore; a source chooses only the public policy or a direct connection, and the address is not echoed back. A single legacy proxy can be inherited, while several addresses require an explicit choice.
- Autostart uses a Windows WScript.Shell shortcut ([Microsoft docs](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/troubleshoot/windows-client/admin-development/create-desktop-shortcut-with-wsh), TargetPath, Arguments, and Save, with COM built in, no pylnk3) and a macOS LaunchAgent; reading, writing, and removing a real shortcut at a temporary Chinese path passes.
- Standalone-package uninstall uses a normal tray exit and a Windows PowerShell helper; the plan is limited to the program marker, the direct children of the data directory, and non-overlapping media, and the helper refuses directory links, exits processes still running from the program directory, and retries deletion. A full uninstall treats only `config.toml.<description>-<date>-<time>` as a settings backup written by Peach, and keeps a hand-made `config.toml.bak`; real input mixing in-use files, data, media, and unrelated files verifies that only the planned content and the whole extraction directory are deleted. The source tree only gives manual uninstall instructions.
- Scan and collection are uniformly hosted in data management; the resident task card jumps to the same entry. The default sort and direction use browsing preferences, with an explicit URL taking precedence.

The directory page's progress banner, missing-preview prompt, and data-management card jointly read `LIBRARY_PROCESSING_KEY` ([shared state](FRONTEND.md#how-to-write-shared-state)); startup submits only once, and the status query continues the tray's first processing. The home-page progress collapses after completion and jumps to data management on failure; data management keeps reading the phase and real counts. The Geist Banner evidence and Peach differences are in [docs/reference-snapshots/vercel-geist-library-banner.md](reference-snapshots/vercel-geist-library-banner.md).

The missing-preview prompt reuses the availability criterion of `cardArtwork`, and the directory, the entity works area, and the detail share `LibraryCompletionPrompt` and `ProcessingActions`. The `collect` phase of `/api/library-processing` fills metadata and local previews, `previews` generates only the local nine grid, and `metadata` collects only metadata; the optional `asset_ids` limits to existing movies, an empty array is rejected, and whole-library scans are not used for targeted processing. The nine grid reuses `frame_capture`, FFmpegResolver, and `/poster`, local phases run before external queries, and task logs and failure retry are kept; no dependency is added. Browser regression uses a temporary demo library and intercepted responses to verify the default action, the dropdown, focus return, and card-face updates after completion.

## Online update for the standalone test package

- Task start and install reservation reuse the pinned filelock 4.2.0 (MIT, Windows/macOS/Linux, no extra dependencies) and atomic JSON. `update_tasks` serially registers tasks and reserves installation within the same data directory, covering each service process, the task center, successor claims, and `job_main` batch processing. An install request makes collection tasks save at a checkpoint and pause, the task center keeps a `pending` row, and new collection requests wait. The install entry waits for in-flight steps to save progress and then stops its own service, without making the user wait for the whole round of tasks to finish. Source batch processing keeps the PID lock, waits for installation after writing the in-flight batch, and the original process continues afterwards.
- The installer expands the bundled runtime in a temporary directory, takes over library-processing state with `--installer-prepare`, and pauses tasks; the reservation is held by the installer and released on completion or cancellation. Online update hands the reservation to the update helper, which releases it after directory switch and migration complete, and also on failure rollback. Recovery polling reuses APScheduler, and when installation is cancelled the existing service also picks tasks back up. No new task queue or update framework is adopted: task semantics, the PyInstaller directory switch, and the Inno Setup lifecycle continue to be carried by existing implementations.
- `job_recovery` stores requests for library-processing scope, phase, specified movies, timeline density, single-title cover, follow, subscription, and want metadata; after service start or install completion only unfinished records are claimed. A paused task reuses the same row of the task center, and completed, failed, and deliberately stopped tasks are not retried in a loop. Library previews save a movie checkpoint after the artifact is registered, metadata checkpoints follow the candidate CSV being written to disk successfully, and items with no result are not recorded as complete. The timeline reuses the existing index decision. Recovery claims and new task starts share the install gate, and data stays in the local state directory, with no ledger migration introduced.

- 自动更新设置用 APScheduler（见「必须复用」表的定时轮询）、共享 HTTPX 发行查询、filelock 与原子 JSON 写入，安装走 `standalone_update`。按 [interval trigger](https://apscheduler.readthedocs.io/en/3.x/modules/triggers/interval.html) 每分钟查是否到期，本机持久时间与文件锁协调多个服务；关闭、6/24/168 小时间隔与源码下载限制归 Peach。默认关闭，下载只准备安装，重启仍需确认；设置存 state 目录的 `automatic-updates.json`。
- 版本与资产信息用 [GitHub Releases REST API](https://docs.github.com/en/rest/releases/assets)（测试通道含预发布），查询与流式下载用项目 HTTPX，ZIP 解压用标准库，互斥用 FileLock。只有已上传完整独立包的发布才进更新，优先选择 `*-update.zip`，历史完整 ZIP 保留原名供旧客户端更新。`package_windows_release.py` 从同一构建目录生成完整包、应用更新包与 FFmpeg、untrunc、cloudflared、人脸模型（`face-models`）工具包；更新包内清单固定工具文件哈希、工具包大小与 SHA-256，运行时只复用校验相符的文件，按需下载不符的工具。清单可以多出本版不认得的工具，读取时只留本版认得的那些，认得的一个都不能少；新增工具包由该工具自己的运行时兜底补齐，不让旧版更新器拒收整个更新包。工具包路径限于对应工具目录，补齐后再次校验并沿用同卷暂存与目录回滚。复用已有 HTTPX、hashlib、zipfile、copy2，无新增依赖；不引入 Velopack 的包格式或二进制差分机制。隔离回归用实际生成的 ZIP 验证工具复用、独立补下载与失败保留现有安装，不使用真实 ledger。
- 安装策略与进度归 Peach：下载校验后在程序同卷暂存，用户确认重启，复制出来的包内助手等原托盘退出再切换完整目录；失败保留或恢复旧目录。配置、数据库与媒体不进更新包。
- 轻量安装包复用应用更新包的工具清单、`standalone_update.prepare_tools` 与既有 HTTPX、hashlib、zipfile。Inno Setup 6.7.3 用 [external 文件](https://jrsoftware.org/ishelp/topic_filessection.htm)安装临时目录中已验证的工具，用[滚动进度页](https://jrsoftware.org/ishelp/topic_isxfunc_createoutputmarqueeprogresspage.htm)显示准备状态；官方 API 已于 2026-10-17 核对。工具先在预检目录准备，成功后才暂停任务并覆盖安装；安装包旁的工具 ZIP 同样按清单校验，不读取馆藏配置或增加依赖。不引入另一套下载器或更新框架，校验及解压路径与在线更新共用。
- 不用 [Velopack](https://docs.velopack.io/packaging/operating-systems/windows)（要求其安装目录与包格式）与 [WinSparkle](https://winsparkle.org/)（要求 appcast 与原生更新界面）：产物是 PyInstaller ZIP、进度在 Web 显示，沿用托盘进程与目录替换协议。
- Web 复用 Fieldset、Progress、confirmModal；状态由 `standalone-update.json` 保存。下载按字节计量，解压按文件数计量，替换使用阶段进度；服务重启期间保留等待状态，恢复连接后核对版本。
- 新版有待应用迁移时，重启安装复用 `migrate upgrade --yes`，先保存 SQLite 备份；迁移或启动失败时恢复数据库与程序。数据库备份位于用户 Peach 数据目录的 `state/update-backups/`。
- 更新事务按当前配置读取 state 与账本路径，助手显式继承所选 Peach 数据目录。已有数据目录恢复复用严格配置解析与位置记录，只接受含配置和账本的目录。`scripts/smoke_desktop.py` 验证恢复及再次启动；`scripts/smoke_online_update.py` 使用真实 Release 包和临时馆藏验证在线更新，可注入无法启动的暂存 EXE 验证回滚。
- 覆盖升级验收复用 `smoke_desktop` 的首启配置、临时 Peach 数据目录、登录和进程退出；`scripts/smoke_overlay_upgrade.py` 使用标准库下载并校验已发布制品，分别走 ZIP 原位解压和 Inno Setup 原位安装。安装包只在没有 Peach 安装登记的 Actions runner 上验收，覆盖安装器停止旧进程、目录记忆、配置与密码保留、账本哨兵、普通启动及卸载保留数据；不新增依赖。

The JAV default cover (official cover / preview image) and the default video size (large image / small image) are saved independently, using localStorage, the shared Switch, and the existing `/cover` and `/poster`. The settings dialog syncs the layouts of the home page and JAV, and the filter bar can adjust the current view alone. `frontend/src/jav-artwork.ts` manages work identity, preference restoration, and missing-image fallback, and the home page, continue watching, entity works, detail recommendations, Mix stillness and image flipping, and the playback queue share the cover choice. Small images show the full jacket, and a portrait front cover is placed completely in a large-image card; new-release covers are shown only after framing completes, and framing is recomputed when a cached image is inserted.

关注视频与馆藏详情的续播复用 Video.js 10.2.0（Apache-2.0）的元数据事件和 `currentTime`、浏览器 localStorage 与既有活动接口。`player/progress` 按本地资产、关注条目和媒体序号登记秒数，暂停、定位、关闭、小窗销毁、页面退出均保存；原生 video 回退也恢复，明确的时间深链优先，放完从头开始。本地既有记录可按 `leave_ratio × duration` 恢复；最远观看位置与累计观看时长不作为续播秒数。活动请求用 keepalive，销毁同步冲掉末段并清理定时器与监听。此状态属于当前浏览器，未引入账本迁移或跨设备同步，无新增依赖。

The detail person avatar reuses `card-art.entityFaceImg` and `avatarFrame`, with the image mounted directly in the sized `[data-id-face]` circle. Framing reads the geometry of the image's parent box, and a `display:contents` wrapper has no usable size. The first character and the image are carried by the same piece of HTML, the image shares the fallback chain when loading fails, and browser regression covers desktop and mobile details with face boxes.

图片尺寸档复用 `previews.DerivedImageService`：实体图默认取长边 640 的 `/entity-image?thumb=1`，只有资料页大位取原件；卡片封面按屏幕像素密度从 `/cover?thumb=2`（长边 800）或 `thumb=1`（长边 1600）起步，`framing.upgradeCover` 量过显示尺寸再逐档换到原件。两档封面同在 `generated/cover-thumbs/`，按长边分文件名、按需生成。侧栏与登录卡的站标用 `scripts/generate_brand_assets.py` 缩出的 64 与 128 px 小图，以 `srcset` 挑选。

新作封面复用 `best_cover` 的官方候选与 `cover_artwork.install_cover`，保存本地封面、正封框、人脸坐标与来源证据。JavDB 图片仅作来源复核证据，不进入安装、封面索引、详情封面投影或缺图兜底；`scripts/quarantine_javdb_covers.py` 将有来源证据的历史图片及边车移入可恢复归档。未取得合格来源时显示未取得状态，详见 ADR-0107。

For censored (with-mosaic, 有码) works without a snapshot, the cover entry reuses the existing `DmmSource` GraphQL query and exact identity verification; a successful snapshot directly expands the DMM image candidates, and r18.dev and DMM use the same catalog evidence. FC2 source snapshots extract the publisher's original storage via `storage_original`, excluding community image hosts. Catalog queries keep the source cooldown, and HTTP, network, and parsing failures each keep a not-obtained state.

官方目录的连接与超时异常按 [HTTPX 异常层次](https://www.python-httpx.org/exceptions/)中的 `TransportError` 隔离，复用已固定的 `httpx 0.28.1`（BSD-3-Clause）与既有候选比较流程，不增加依赖或重试层。目录未作答时仍比较其他候选；没有可用图片时保留可重试状态，只有来源均有确定答复才记录无图。动作预算与编程错误继续向调用方传播。馆藏、库外新作与手动重探共用 `best_cover`，已安装图片和来源边车继续由原子安装入口管理。

官方综合目录的聚合无图记忆按 `upgrade_rules.OFFICIAL_COVER_CATALOG_MISSES` 复用。规则版本不匹配的非 FC2 聚合记录在后续查询时重新核验；FC2 商品图记录、逐站无图记忆、有效图片、资料快照及个人记录保留。查询结果照常写回现有记忆文件，不启动额外全库采集。

批量封面 CSV 用 `outcome` 区分 `not_found` 与 `unavailable`，`catalog_rules` 保存无图判据版本。续跑只跳过当前规则确认的目录无图记录；旧综合目录记录允许重探，FC2 的确定答复与成功来源记录继续复用。读取不改写原日志，实际执行时沿用原子日志写入。

MGS 专属发行先查自营商品目录，Prestige 番号复用 `metadata_routes.maker_sites` 路由后汇总集团官网与 MGS 图片；手上已有候选仍一起比较实际尺寸。专属目录未取得候选时才查综合目录，避免无关目录故障消耗整项预算。

Official covers and product images are chosen by pixel area; at equal size the product image is preferred, and still-frame previews keep the last tier; the image role does not let a low-resolution product image override a larger official cover.

Snapshots with only studio images continue to query the DMM catalog; only a successful DMM source snapshot skips the catalog query, and neither a CDN address built from the studio number nor the missing-memory of r18.dev replaces a DMM query.

The face cache uses `cover-face-v4` with source image size validation. `detect_cover_faces.py` automatically recomputes stale records, and per-image checks with `--refresh-faces` can decide the layout after recomputing the main face of the whole image; large covers on 4:3 or √2:1 canvases without a credible spine, or where the main face on the left clearly dominates, are framed as the whole picture. Installation, batch recompute, and restoring default framing read the same layout marker.

Traditional front covers are centered in full, proportional padding reuses the blurred backfill of the derived tier of the same image, and new releases and video-feed cards use the same framing rules. Full-spread large covers use the card's solid background, portrait ones fill by main face, and landscape ones fit completely into the container; leftover background properties are cleared on image switch.

取景由 `poster-crop-v13` 管理。Blu-ray 来源标题、明确的版次标签与图片比例共同决定 0.85 正封比例，标题随正封框保留，重算人脸与正封框均读取同一版次；有效宽正封框在没有版次标题的卡片上也完整显示。VR 标记、IENEE、IENFE、SDHS 数字发行拼图，以及可信主脸跨过 DVD 正封先验边界的大封保留整幅画面，由 `cover_frame.layout=still` 传给卡片。两条满高接缝夹出居中正封且两侧纹理均低于正封四分之一时，只显示中间正封。大封在竖版大图按主脸居中填满，横版小图完整显示，均不使用模糊补边；JPEG 软边的书脊识别结合突出度、左忙右静的方向与正封侧基线。手工框保持用户取景。重试队列按最早尝试时间轮转，收起的合集沿用设置跳过；逐番号结果保存在 `feed-cover-attempts/`。资料已齐、只缺封面的新作另派 `feed-cover`（补新作封面）批次，只取图、不写账本，走独立后继通道；排在队里或正在跑的番号不再进下一批。复用现有 HTTP、Pillow、OpenCV 与文件锁，无新增依赖。

Library and new-release image fetching share `cover_artwork.metadata_snapshots` and `best_cover`, and official candidates from studio sites, publishers, R18, and DMM all take part in size comparison. Library collection treats a long edge of 1600px as the high-definition re-probe threshold, so community-reposted images can be replaced by official covers; official images of the same kind are upgraded by pixel area, and FC2 keeps the source protection of product images. `scripts/audit_cover_artwork.py` checks size, source, edition, and framing box image by image and outputs CSV; `scripts/upgrade_audited_covers.py` re-probes code by code against existing official snapshots, and `--live` supplements online through the same official chain, saving the original backup and result checkpoints. Both handle only rebuildable images and sidecars, and do not write to the ledger.

## Reuse decision gate

Every decision of "use something ready-made or write it ourselves" follows these four rules; the remaining entries in this section are concrete decisions made by them:

- First run a no-write POC on real input, then choose one of "direct dependency, pinned-source implementation, or keep in-house".
- An adopted item must record the pinned version, license, first consumer, and the domain boundary Peach keeps; a candidate dependency must not sit idle.
- Keeping in-house must record the rejected candidates and the irreplaceable constraint, not just "special requirements".
- When an external project is unsuitable as a runtime dependency but its public data model or algorithm is clearly more mature, pin a revision and use it as a reference
  implementation; when the license does not allow derivation or the source is unstable, use it only as behavior evidence and do not copy code.

- Python install and build use [uv](https://github.com/astral-sh/uv) (MIT/Apache-2.0; `pyproject.toml` sets only the lower bound `>=0.12.13` and it does not enter the dependency graph) and the official setup-uv (MIT; version in the [test workflow](../.github/workflows/test.yml)): the uv project interface, `uv.lock`, `uv sync --locked`, and Dependabot using the official `uv` ecosystem. Development and builds create the environment in an isolated worktree, and the production venv does not take part in exact sync; direct dependencies are pinned exactly and transitive dependencies are reproduced by the lock file.
  The test database reuses `tests/support/ledger.py`: migrations generate a template and then copy independent temporary databases ([timing](SOURCING.md)), real migration tests still run migrations; retry tests use the existing sleeper injection point. CI uses independent GitHub Actions runner shards and the existing unittest entry point, Peach maintains only the impact-domain policy, and no concurrent test framework is introduced (see `TESTING.md`).
- Access passwords reuse Python 3.14's [hashlib.scrypt](https://docs.python.org/3.14/library/hashlib.html) and OpenSSL, browser sessions reuse [ItsDangerous](https://itsdangerous.palletsprojects.com/en/stable/) (Pallets, BSD-3-Clause, pure Python, 16 KB wheel, no transitive dependencies), and local atomic configuration writes use tempfile/os.replace and filelock; the access passphrase of the authentication entry, the three rejection responses, and the local configuration guard continue to be reused as before. Starlette SessionMiddleware has a uniform duration and renews with responses, which cannot satisfy each device choosing its own deadline, so the same-origin signing library is used directly, and Peach manages the optional password, deadlines, and revocation, without an account system or migration.
- Cloudflare Quick Tunnel uses the official `cloudflared` (Apache-2.0), without writing our own tunnel protocol. The source environment only manages the process in PATH / environment variables; the Windows standalone package bundles a pinned `2026.9.0` alongside, with the asset, address, and SHA-256 in `scripts/cloudflared-windows.json`, double-checked by `fetch_cloudflared.ps1` and the build script. The readiness criterion is the official `--pidfile` (written only after first connecting to the edge), and availability is not claimed before connecting. The source HTTPS origin uses the project CA, the standalone package uses loopback HTTP, and write-request origin validation carries a random URL held by the current process.

- The README delivery check uses the system Git's `diff --no-renames -z` and [`interpret-trailers --parse`](https://git-scm.com/docs/git-interpret-trailers), hooked into `agent_worktree.py ready/integrate`; Peach defines only the affected files and the bilingual declaration policy. Native hooks are not installed with a clone and cannot judge copy semantics, so it is hooked on the shared delivery entry points ([README maintenance](README_MAINTENANCE.md)).
- Configuration access decisions use the `client` / `server` addresses of the ASGI connection and the standard-library `ipaddress`: a local connection matches a loopback address or the server IP, and Host only validates the tray-configured domain or bound address and does not prove the caller is on this machine. Configuration saving uses revision-number validation, atomic replacement, and the `SetupGate` reload marker, and the tray child service declares reload capability with `PEACH_TRAY_MANAGED`. Install detection uses `winreg`, `shutil.which`, and the install directory, media tools use `FFmpegResolver`, and a detached drive is not judged as not installed. Download entries verified 2026-09-06: [CloudDrive](https://www.clouddrive2.com/download.html), [WinFsp](https://winfsp.dev/rel/), [macFUSE](https://macfuse.github.io/), [FFmpeg](https://ffmpeg.org/download.html), [OpenSSL](https://openssl-library.org/source/).
- Release identity uses each source's parsers and Seesaa's raw DTOs, and numeric-prefix equivalence is Peach's domain policy. Query fallback cannot carry identity confirmation ([the `390JAC-040` case](SOURCING.md)), responses are checked against the original query, and the MGStage official detail path can corroborate display-number aliases; external adapters only take values, and the first search hit is not a ledger identity assertion.
- The Seesaa work table uses HTTPX, Beautiful Soup 4.15.0 (MIT), and the existing `HostLimiter`, code normalization, field candidates, and snapshot protocol. Neither Javinizer-Go `d9724f239d7e127afcb747fa8ce4358685912f50` (MIT) nor MetaTube `6a5e6128c725187aeaf921d48ed7d9cd9f30671b` (Apache-2.0) has a Seesaa adapter (checked 2026-09-06), so `peach.sources.seesaa` carries EUC-JP, column mapping, exact row identity, and unknown-roster protection, with `scrape_codes --profile seesaa` as the consumer ([site details](SOURCING.md#seesaa-wiki-work-evidence)).

- The performer-alias successor (ADR-0055, 0061, 0064) reuses the `minnano_av` search and profile-table parsing, `sources.seesaa.WikiPages`,
  the `sources.fc2cmadb` performer column, `HostLimiter`, the `scraping_access` cooldown, `metadata_alias_resolve.is_planning_alias`, and
  the four do-not-write criteria of `apply_alias_candidates.py`, with withdrawal via `revert_auto_landing.py`. minnano-av does not use `page_cache.Site`
  (it loses the final address after redirects, starts a rate limiter per instance, and caches a 200 verification page as body text); `MinnanoPages` records the final address, shares one
  `HostLimiter`, and recognizes the verification page without caching it and records a cooldown; the transport is `SourceTransport`, and when blocked it goes through
  `browser_transport.shared` per `browser_fallback` (see [source collection](SOURCING.md#intermittent-blocking-on-minnano-av)).
- The performer-profile successor (`performer_profile_followup`, ADR-0067) reuses `minnano_profile_pages`, name checking, and `MinnanoPages`
  (plus the `source` cooldown key and `max_age`, also used by avwikidb and javdb), and scheduling uses ADR-0053's `Attempts` (written marks are kept for 30 days).
  Parsing is in `minnano_av.profile` (profile table) and `avwikidb` (JSON-LD), and reads and writes are in `performer_profiles`. The Minnano-AV rules of `kanojo-db/scrapers`
  and `stashapp/CommunityScrapers` are not installable libraries (the latter is Stash XPath configuration, ADR-0021).
  The performer page header (ADR-0069) outputs five items and alias groups from `performer_header`, the alias-column notes are split by `minnano_av.name_entries`,
  and names are split with `sources.seesaa.split_names`.

Documentation checking uses seiso (MIT, dev dependency), and the [copy gate](../tests/test_copy_final_state.py) calls its stable rules; file classification and the third-party original-text boundary are in the [seiso configuration](../seiso.toml), and wording and manual review are in [documentation and UI copy](WRITING.md). No self-built Markdown document-responsibility parser.

Testing and integration use `test_runner.py` and `agent_worktree.py`, and process mutual exclusion uses the dev dependency `filelock`'s [`FileLock`](https://py-filelock.readthedocs.io/en/stable/tutorials.html). Full verification and integration share one lock with a wait of at most 30 minutes; verification holds the lock until the record is written. Temporary Git repository regression covers mutual exclusion, waiting, and release; code, environment, and scope records are Peach's integration constraints. An invalidated record returns `4`, on which the Windows tray retries at most three rounds, and a test failure returns `1`. Both test entry points reuse the existing `find_uv` and the main checkout's `Dependencies.check` through `test_environment.py`; isolated worktrees sync with uv's official lock file, adding no package-management implementation or runtime dependency. A sync failure stops the test, and the main checkout is checked read-only; policy and behavior regression are in `test_test_environment.py`.

Targeted debugging reuses the standard-library unittest's load-by-name and the existing browser fixtures, and record reuse follows the file manifest, impact domains, and FileLock, adding no test framework or cache service. A partial success record keeps the original verification time of unaffected domains, a failed domain cannot be restored from a historical baseline, and a browser failure stops immediately. Regression covers expiry, environment differences, unknown and shared files, re-verification after failure, path escape, and not signing a debug proof; see [Testing](TESTING.md#targeted-debugging-and-partial-records) for details.

Commit messages reuse Git's [`interpret-trailers --parse`](https://git-scm.com/docs/git-interpret-trailers), and the attribution and README-declaration criteria are shared by `co_author.py` and `check_readme_impact.py`; `check_commit_message.py` is wired to the repository `commit-msg` hook. Git itself handles comments and multi-line trailers, and Peach only checks field shape and segmentation. Temporary-repository regression on Windows Git 2.55.0.windows.3 covers rejecting a commit, keeping the staging area, joint human and agent attribution, amend, and merge messages; no new parsing dependency. For the specific trigger scope see [README maintenance](README_MAINTENANCE.md).

### Playback, collection, release, and packaging

For the measurements and evidence behind each entry see the [reuse evidence record](SOURCING.md).

- 页面、框体、操作条与选中项共用一套灰阶，操作条用 `--overlay-5`。
- javdb 属有码与素人链的社区档，候选保留 community 来源性质，不自动写真相字段。
- [JavDB Web UI 实证](reference-snapshots/javdb-web-ui-poc.md)：库外作品详情复用舞台、人物身份、想要、`WantMagnets` 与云下载组件；`javdb_public.PublicAPI` 复用 `HttpxTransport`（httpx 0.28.1，BSD-3-Clause）、`HostLimiter`、Pillow 12.3.0 与标准库 MD5/XOR，独立映射公开协议。匿名详情、两页评论、6 条样本资源、封面与预览图像素已取得；全站完整性和不限流未证明。请求串行、3 秒间隔、6 小时有界缓存，评论最多 8 页，失败保留资料并冷却；发现复用持久资料边车和固定集合补齐，传输层预算由 `ContextVar` 隔离。未新增依赖或写入 ledger。
- 公开签名材料按请求头调用与签名计算式定位，不依赖压缩器生成的变量名，不执行来源脚本；常量长度、唯一赋值与组合结构均核对，格式不匹配时保留来源故障。FC2 搜索使用来源接受的商品号，返回资料仍按原始规范番号及作品 ID 严格核验；其他番号沿用原查询写法。
- 馆藏和库外样图共用 `SourcePicture` 与浏览器图片缓存。组件按作品保持身份，缓存命中在布局阶段揭示，网络图片经原生 `HTMLImageElement.decode()` 就绪后显示；等待时保留已显示的图片，晚到结果按当前地址核对。失败和重试仍按图片地址隔离，无新增依赖。
- 后台诊断导出复用 `BackgroundJob`、本机访问与同源检查、`BufferedFileResponse`，使用 Python 标准库 `zipfile`、`TemporaryFile` 和 `JSONEncoder.iterencode` 分块生成。取消使用线程事件，任务身份核对、同一管理器互斥和未完成文件回收由导出模块负责；不登记 ledger 任务，不新增依赖。普通诊断按压缩字节数决定是否合并刮削详情。
- 慢项跳过复用 `ProcessingControl` 的持久控制文件、`checkpoint` 和 `ActiveDeadline`，按任务及当前资产消费跳过指令；资料来源的逐项发布和失败项重试继续共用现行采集路径。前端共用 `Note`、`useAction` 与现有任务查询，常驻任务浮窗禁止滑动关闭，显式隐藏和恢复入口独立于任务状态。
- 库外 JAV 身份引用复用 `entity_ref` 的本地头像与取景、厂牌 `has_logo` / `logo_version`；来源演员头像按作品演员 ID 取回并由同一 Pillow 路径解码，未知演员不绑定其他实体。性别只投影来源明确字段或规范资料，未知值保留 `unknown`；`MIDA-361` 的一位女优和四位男优与原站演员标记一致。标签投影复用 `genre_decision`、`genre_taxonomy.resolve_genre` 与 `tag_cat`，`tag_refs` 保留来源原词。样本 `MIDA-849` 的两张来源演员头像完整解码成功；其 HLS 清单 GET 200，密钥与媒体 HEAD 200，均允许匿名跨域访问。`MIDA-361` 的 HLS 在隔离实例中实际解码为 1024×576，播放按钮推进超过 2 秒，暂停、设置与卸载通过；未保存媒体帧或视频文件，整段播放未验证。预览仅下发已验证 HTTPS 主机与路径，播放复用既有 Video.js；无需新增代理或依赖。外部候选与版本依据沿用上条只读实证，Peach 保留身份、来源和请求预算策略。
- 候选 `ANDonekey/javapi` revision `9a2a245a659a4008ee75bc148b0af9c7126e7a71` 未归档、最后推送为 2026-05-11，使用 Go 和部署服务，GitHub 未标明许可证，不能作为 Python 依赖或复制其源码。仅以部署公开前端与匿名实测确定协议事实；原始取证脚本及脱敏概要保留在工作树 `build/javdb-public-api/`。`phoenixthrush/javdb-python`（MIT、Python ≥3.11、无运行依赖）解析的是 `javdatabase.com` HTML，站点与协议不匹配；现有 `sources.javdb` 同样只适用于 HTML。Peach 保留番号核对、权限失败、字段投影和缓存策略。
- 编码边界依据 [MDN 视频编码说明](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/Media/Guides/Formats/Video_codecs) 与 [ffprobe 文档](https://ffmpeg.org/ffprobe.html)，用当前 FFmpeg；Peach 只持有兼容格式判定与缓存策略。
- CloudDrive 引导用现有 `settings_file`、`platform.root_online`、`scan_location` 与 React 配置页，来源为 `local`、`115`、`pikpak`。挂载由用户安装的 CloudDrive 负责（[官方帮助](https://www.clouddrive2.com/help.html)：Windows 用盘符、macOS 用目录挂载点），Peach 不捆绑其二进制，挂载引导不依赖管理 API，路径处理用 `pathlib`、`os.scandir`、`tomllib`；表单、来源归属与扫描选择归 Peach。云下载和账号代理配置经官方 proto 子集（`downloads_clouddrive.proto`，version 1.1.2）走 gRPC，令牌存 CredentialStore，不用非官方 SDK（ADR-0089）。
- 媒体目录校验沿用 `media.normalized_path` 的平台策略：Windows 用标准库 `os.path.abspath` 保留盘符，macOS 用 `Path.resolve` 解析文件系统链接。实现只依赖项目 Python 标准库（PSF 许可），不新增依赖；校验器独立于账本路径翻译与媒体仓储。映射盘转 UNC 的边界由注入文件系统解析结果的回归覆盖，首次设置的本地来源也验证配置保留盘符。直接输入 UNC 仍按盘符路径契约拒绝；真实 NAS 挂载需在目标机器验收。
- 非 Windows 表单的内部路径由 `media_configuration.prepare_sources` 补齐，复用既有来源校验、配置写入和 `platform` 路径转换；不改数据库路径模型或执行迁移。标准库 [PureWindowsPath / PurePosixPath](https://docs.python.org/3.14/library/pathlib.html#pure-paths) 用于比较两种路径，[hashlib.sha256](https://docs.python.org/3.14/library/hashlib.html) 按来源与实际目录生成稳定候选前缀，已有配置和显式输入优先，冲突时换盘符。媒体库名称使用实际目录名，前端复用现有 `Disclosure` 和 `Input`，无新增依赖。
- JAV 入库资源查询复用 `sources.javdb` 的身份核对、`SourceTransport` 的 Cookie 与冷却、`HostLimitedTransport` 的 3 秒间隔、`beautifulsoup4==4.15.0` 和 `downloads.parse_magnet`。JavPack 的 `JavDB.magnet.user.js` 0.0.2（GPL-3.0，仓库 revision `b546e1881147e15796c697e8e086db9d3ac0c5be`）只作页面字段参考；它依赖浏览器用户脚本 API，不能直接作为 Python 服务端依赖。Peach 负责评论链接、协议分流、去重、60 秒缓存和最多三页评论的预算，不新增依赖。2026-10-02 单作品只读实测取得 4 条磁链和含 ed2k 的评论页；评论入口从 `.review-tab[data-url]` 读取，受限评论保留来源限制提示。磁链可交现有云下载服务，ed2k 与其他网页链接提供复制。
- 磁力优先链复用同一套 `SourceTransport`、`HostLimitedTransport`、`downloads.parse_magnet` 与 Torznab 后备，不引入新的抓取运行时。manko.fun 的页面请求实际落到 `healertanker.com/swx`，响应 `data` 是 basE91 包装的 JSON；实现只移植该公开响应形态与编号精确匹配，不复用会话脚本。Sukebei 直接读搜索表格；JavBus、16mag、OneJAV、Free JAV Torrent 由现有自配 Prowlarr／Jackett 索引器续接。Akiba Online 与 Reddit 只作线索，不自动取磁力。候选按分辨率、做种、推广附件数量与正片体积排序；总文件数不再是一票否决，manko.fun 候选绕过水印门槛，其他来源只能标为待到盘检查。
- 云下载后处理复用 `task_run` 后继串行、`process_library(retry_ids=...)`、`junk.isolate`、`organize.build_plan(asset_ids=...)`、SQLite backup 与整理批次日志。没有另造第二套刮削、垃圾词库或移动器。
- `junk.scored_candidates` 统一复核与自动隔离的证据；`junk.isolate` 要求显式资产或来源目录，保护人工决定、个人记录、字幕与正片身份，写前重查证据，仅写可撤销的回收站状态。扫描、推送、媒体探测、资料处理、云下载与维护脚本共用该入口；页面只补展示成本与缩略图字段。复用当前树的词库、目录关系、`record_holders`、`LedgerDatabase` 与历史评分回归，使用 Python 标准库和现有 SQLite JSON 支持，无新增依赖、协议或媒体读取。流程顺序见[垃圾文件](OPERATIONS.md#垃圾文件)。
- 抓取入口的复用缺口见 [来源采集](SOURCING.md) ；跨用户安装、来源网络、Cookie GUI、最高可得画质与图像清单按 [ADR-0024](adr/0024-mark-manifest-not-bundled-bytes.md)。私有后缀判断用 tldextract 5.4.0（BSD-3-Clause），随包 PSL、`suffix_list_urls=()`、`cache_dir=None`、`include_psl_private_domains=True`；Instaloader 4.15.3 与 browser_cookie3 不进正式依赖。HTTPX（BSD-3-Clause）、curl_cffi（MIT）、Pillow、amane 桥与现有候选缓存是正式基础，请求节拍用 `scripting.RateLimiter`／`HostLimiter`。
- 采集 GUI 用 React island、CredentialStore、HTTPX、Pillow 与 BackgroundJob，`jav_cover_fetch` 同时服务界面与 CLI；Peach 保留域内凭据、来源路由、预算、冷却、番号身份与高清替换策略。Cookie 文本用标准库 SimpleCookie／MozillaCookieJar 解析，不导入 pickle。
- 删除失效链接与资源同步的执行阶段用 `BackgroundJob.start_result` 存终态回执；刷新只查状态，写入不自动重放，确认与检查结果过期门槛照旧有效。
- 关注进度用 Fieldset 与 Progress；完成时保留内容取数，按作者检查用 `sources` 范围参数，报错作者用现有身份解析。
- GET 重试归现有连接器：[HTTPX 原生重试](https://www.python-httpx.org/advanced/transports/)只覆盖连接失败，统一不了 HTTPX 与 curl_cffi 的读超时、临时 HTTP 状态与页面进度。POST 不重放，403 单次终止。
- 标签发布用系统 Git、GitHub CLI（MIT）与 [Actions runs REST API](https://docs.github.com/en/rest/actions/workflow-runs)；[release_tag.py](../scripts/release_tag.py) 管版本、主线归属、同提交成功 CI 与不可覆盖策略，工作流用 Release 制品验收。
- 独立 Windows 测试包用 PyInstaller（GPL-2.0-or-later，带 bootloader 分发例外）的 [onedir 与自启动子进程](https://pyinstaller.org/en/stable/common-issues-and-pitfalls.html)：完整目录共享资源、避免重复解包，onefile 托盘入口只用于源码部署；Peach 保留进程所有权、数据目录、配置和扫描策略。
- 首启数据目录定位复用标准库 `pathlib` 和现有 `fsutil.atomic_write_text`，在默认数据目录记录本机绝对路径，读取限定跳转次数并检查循环。目录选择、单实例锁交接及服务环境属于 Peach 生命周期策略，无新增依赖。临时目录回归覆盖保存配置、真实子服务启动、正常首页、再次发现所选位置和锁冲突。
- Windows 安装包用 Inno Setup 6.7.3（Inno Setup License，允许免费分发）与官方 `Files/Languages/Unofficial/ChineseSimplified.isl` 译文（在 `scripts/installer/`）。目录选择与升级目录记忆复用官方 [`DisableDirPage`](https://jrsoftware.org/ishelp/topic_setup_disabledirpage.htm)、[`UsePreviousAppDir`](https://jrsoftware.org/ishelp/topic_setup_usepreviousappdir.htm)；Peach 在目录页和安装前校验独立目录、数据重叠、目录链接、写入权限及已有安装位置，使用现有 Inno Setup，无新增依赖。馆藏与口味上传说明复用 [`CreateOutputMsgMemoPage`](https://jrsoftware.org/ishelp/topic_isxfunc_createoutputmsgmemopage.htm) 的只读滚动页与 `ShouldSkipPage`；Unicode 正文赋给 `RichEditViewer.Text`，仅主动选择上传时显示。`test_desktop_installer.py` 编译正式脚本并运行共用判据的隔离 EXE，覆盖中文和空格路径、原位升级及目录保护，不安装到真实位置、不写 ledger。`.iss` 只管文件、开始菜单项和卸载登记；停托盘、补迁移、撤快捷方式经 `desktop_installer` 调已有入口，自更新与应用内卸载是唯一实现。
- 运行一致性：用 `LedgerDatabase.write_transaction` 的提交边界与 `OrderedDict`；HTTP 导航用 FastAPI/Starlette，图片复验用 `StaticFiles.is_not_modified` 与 `FileResponse` 的 ETag；列表用 SQLite 的 IN/UNION 保留隐藏标签与多标签组合，不引查询框架。
- 馆藏侧栏用 `catalog_filter` 的列表条件，已保存在线卡片用关注来源的标签与封面投影，详情用 `openFollowDetail`、Video.js 与媒体队列；导航范围与标签计数在 `frontend/src/sidebar.ts`。
- wheel 资源用 setuptools 的 `build_py.copy_tree`（[官方包内数据建议](https://setuptools.pypa.io/en/stable/userguide/datafiles.html)），钩子只复制三个既有资源目录，因为源码、桌面构建和前端产物共用其维护位置。

## Domain logic Peach must build itself

The following is product behavior and continues to be implemented by Peach:

- The canonical identity, aliases, and sources of performers, studios, creators, and tags; the old id of a merged entity finds the new entity via the `entity_redirect` tombstone and `entities.resolve_entity_id` (ADR-0088);
- Profile behavior, watch later, playlists, taste, and recommendation ranking;
- Binding and fallback policy for local, 115, PikPak, and online sources;
- Billing-source authorization, privacy classification, and candidate review import;
- Cross-type evidence, manual review, and trash semantics for physical-resource junk candidates; empty-directory cleanup reuses the Python standard library's bottom-up `os.walk` and `Path.rmdir`, which removes only empty directories, and Peach is responsible only for online sources, root-directory protection, and the boundary where CloudDrive entries vanish concurrently;
- Permanent junk deletion reuses the quarantine, database rollback, and post-commit retirement of `purge_assets`; the per-file review list checks path, size, and source root, adding no dependency; quarantine names carry a UUID, move and restore use `os.rename` and the target must not exist, and when the mount refuses a move the file and the ledger row are kept;
- Private acquisition sources, provenance references, and discovery keywords;
- Creator-level visual-sampling semantics;
- The capability contracts of the inference Provider and Agent Provider;
- Task ownership, progress, cancellation, cost, and evidence rules.

The following are infrastructure rather than product behavior, so the rejected candidates and the irreplaceable constraints are recorded separately, to avoid starting again from "this looks like it should have a ready-made library".

| In-house implementation | Rejected candidates | Irreplaceable constraint |
|---|---|---|
| `mp4index.py` bounded MP4 keyframe index | PyAV, `pymp4`, Bento4 | PyAV needs demuxing, `pymp4` depends on the old Construct, and Bento4 is an extra binary; none can show that they keep the constraint of "read only moov/stss/stts and avoid whole-file traffic" on cloud-drive files. |
| `mp4repair.py` rebuilds the missing `ctts`, storing the new header as a sidecar | `ffmpeg -c copy` remux, `untrunc`, Bento4 `mp4edit` | ffmpeg's h264 demuxer cannot recover display order (measured: after remuxing, 986/2015 frames still run backwards), `untrunc` repairs truncation rather than a missing table, and `mp4edit` only rewrites the whole file in place, so changing one byte of a cloud-drive file means re-uploading several GB. The constraint is "not a single byte of the original file moves, and a compliant MP4 is assembled at playback time". |
| `certs.py` pinned project CA and short-lived leaf certificates (encoding still calls OpenSSL) | mkcert, cryptography | mkcert takes over the local CA install and private key and cannot keep a project CA pinned across devices; cryptography only replaces certificate encoding and adds a native dependency, and cannot remove Peach's Apple 398-day and CA lifecycle policy. |
| `migrations.py` SQLite migrations | Alembic | Alembic would bring in SQLAlchemy/Mako/greenlet; the current scope needs only sequential SQL, checksums, backup, and PyInstaller resource location, and has no ORM consumer. |
| Gofile API direct HTTP | Community wrappers | The official side has no maintained Python SDK; community wrappers are thin shells that cannot bypass the Premium `contents` permission and cannot reduce Peach's Bearer isolation and media normalization. |
| `netwatch.py`, streaming/segments, sync, versioning/Windows update | Generic alternatives | Respectively: system notifications without PyObjC, session policy on FFmpeg/Starlette, single-writer ledger rules, and the Git/PyInstaller update contract; a generic alternative would keep the same amount of policy or enlarge the dependencies. |
| `downloads_pikpak.py` PikPak cloud download | PikPakAPI; `p115client` used for cloud download direct to 115 | PikPakAPI is GPL-3.0-only, async-only, and swallows the verification page address, so only protocol constants are borrowed; `p115client` needs a separate 115 authorization and competes with CloudDrive2 mounting for authorization slots (ADR-0089). Browser login (`downloads_pikpak_browser.py`) follows the PikPak Assistant userscript (MIT) in reading `credentials_*` from localStorage, with the pop-up window reusing the `attended` tier of `browser_transport`; the userscript renews through the web page, and Peach renews itself after taking the credentials (ADR-0093). |
| `organize.py` / `organize_templates.py` directory organizing | amane's organize templates | Only the placeholder and optional-grouping semantics are borrowed; amane treats files as deletable derivatives, while Peach's media stays in place and organizing must be user-initiated (ADR-0039). |
| `wants.py` want list | SakuraMedia's wants and subscriptions | SakuraMedia is only behavior evidence; library entry reconciles automatically by code and lands directly in the database per ADR-0052 (ADR-0090). |
| `jobs.PidFileLock` batch process lock | `portalocker==4.3.0` | The candidate covers PID writing, holder, atomic replacement, and stale-file cleanup, but no consumer ever landed; per "a candidate dependency must not sit idle" it is not introduced. |
| `record_rehome.py` reattaches personal records to new files | Reattach by file fingerprint; change six tables to `ON DELETE SET NULL` | A vanished file cannot be read or fingerprinted; a record detached from `asset` loses its code and title. The code is the only key stable across versions, and those without a code are reattached by creator, filename stem, and duration (ADR-0087). |

Release-directory flattening and directory-ad cleanup reuse `scripts/flatten_release_dirs.py`, `catalog_rules`, and the SQLite backup.
The path plan is indexed by directory component and preserves operation order, covering assets and registered subtitles; physical moves carry sidecar files such as unregistered covers.
The directory graph excludes the file's last level; multiple subdirectories with the same name and directory wrappers with a media extension are judged as redundant levels, and the extension reuses the scanner's classification.
When a redundant directory is the sole content of its parent, two whole-directory renames and empty staging-directory cleanup are used; when the parent contains attached files or other versions, entries are merged only if the target entries do not conflict. Execution is refused when the staging location or filename conflicts.
When a wrapper directory occupies the content's target name, that directory is staged first and the content moved out, while other versions stay in place.
Each move first rechecks the real directory, a partial failure immediately restores that entry, and a ledger transaction failure restores the batch.
No new dependency; the Python standard library's path operations and SQLite already cover the underlying capability, and Peach keeps the criteria for redundant names, ads, and ledger consistency.

Full directory categorization and placement uses `scripts/organize_library_dirs.py`, reusing `organize._rename`, the directory flattening entry point, the code parser, entity categories, and the process lock. The plan is frozen by default, and only `--apply --backup` executes it; `--batch` saves the plan and receipt separately and failures are recoverable. `--stage flatten` generates its own flatten review CSV. Confirmed works in the same directory and cover images missing a studio are handled as a whole package; mixed directories move only known file groups and their paired subtitles, physical files are deduplicated, and unknown videos, conflicting identities, and overwrite targets are all kept; sidecar files in the same directory are determined by `scan.is_sidecar`, titled ones follow their video, bare `poster`, `movie.nfo`, and `extrafanart` follow only when all videos in the directory are moved, and registered and quarantined files are left alone. Source directories that cannot be deleted are recorded with their remaining names in the receipt's `cleanup_errors`. A creator target keeps only the structure below the canonical-name level, container directories above the canonical name are discarded, and when the path has no canonical name only the source directory name is carried; container directory names that are not renamed as a whole are in `resources/naming/container_directory_words.txt`. `--rollback` undoes committed entries in reverse order of the receipt, reusing `restore_entry`, and entries whose ledger has since been modified are reported separately and left untouched; without `--apply --backup` it only lists the plan. Release sites, quality, and part markers in directory names reuse `release_code_from_filename`; the date-style separator matching of 1Pondo reuses its official work-number parsing and does not merge date-style numbers across studios; the target code directory is first aligned with an existing equivalent directory, and when several coexist the official-site work-number spelling is used. When a video filename carries its own number that is not the same work as the ledger code, no directory is built and it is recorded as pending confirmation. FC2 release directories may use the `FC-` shorthand with a title appended; `@fc编号_分片` (the `@fc<number>_<part>` pattern) lacking a code checks both the parent directory number and the studio, and the directory number is not written back to the ledger. Korean MIB is placed by studio. When a unique creator relation exists but the sub-category is unconfirmed, it goes into the generic `创作者` ("creator") directory without promoting the identity candidate. Path sync covers assets and registered subtitles and does not backfill ground-truth fields.

PikPak WebDAV same-source moves are provided by `organize_clouddrive.py`, and the directory entry enables it only with an explicit `--pikpak-webdav --location pikpak --batch`. It reuses the token already stored by CloudDrive and the authentication header of the official `GetDownloadUrlPath`; the origin is fixed at `https://dav.mypikpak.com`, and authentication is not written into addresses, logs, or the ledger. The protocol follows the [official PikPak WebDAV notes](https://mypikpak.com/zh-TW/help-center/connected_apps/webdav/how_to) and [RFC 4918](https://www.rfc-editor.org/rfc/rfc4918), the HTTP client reuses the declared `httpx`, and message encoding reuses the declared `protobuf`, adding no dependency. The official `GetSubFiles(forceRefresh=True)` reads the full directory level by level from the source root, checking each level's member source, parent directory, duplicate names, and size. Same-parent renames use the official `RenameFile`; cross-directory moves within the same source that keep the original name use the official `MoveFile` with the conflict policy `Skip`, explicitly disabling cross-cloud moves and recursive merges. WebDAV only creates the required parent directories with HTTPS `MKCOL`, and both renames and moves go through the official interface. When the official side returns false or a rejection code (`INVALID_ARGUMENT`, `PERMISSION_DENIED`, etc.) and the directory confirms the original is still present and the target has not appeared, that entry is recorded in the receipt's `failures` and rolled back, and a resumed run skips it; the batch is aborted only when the original and the target both exist or both do not. `organize._rename` refuses the A drive by default and allows it only when the officially verified rename is injected through `organize.verified_renames`; when not injected, every execution entry refuses the whole batch. A single HTTP response is capped at 2 MiB, and traversal at 10,000 entries. After a move is submitted, only the caches of the directories involved are refreshed, path and size change evidence is obtained along the full ancestor chain, and then it is handed back to the existing ledger transaction; there are at most four read-only confirmations, and the move is not submitted again. When the state is uncertain the intent receipt is kept and execution stops, and even a success response must obtain complete member-change evidence. Flattened directories on the A drive use staging names that do not start with a dot and keep the same recovery receipt; the R drive does not enter this transport path.

Same-parent directory renames on the A drive reuse CloudDrive's official `RenameFile`; operations that change parent and name are split into a same-parent `RenameFile` and a name-preserving official `MoveFile`, with the intermediate path entering the recovery receipt and overwriting forbidden. Each step checks the full members and sizes; a success response does not replace path verification. `@` in paths is kept as a URI path character, and other characters use UTF-8 percent-encoding. When the authentication sample has already been moved, at most two other in-library image samples are queried, extracting only the existing authentication and not reading media content.

推广网页资源目录中的横幅、标志和导航图片由 `junk._attachment_junk_reason` 进入复核队列。判据同时要求 `_files`、网址推广套话、域名、导航元素文件名和体积上限；继承的作品番号不豁免网页导航图片，作品图片、字幕、NFO 与普通 `_files` 目录保留。固定命名的推广附件（安装包、宣传视频、二维码卡片、下载站横幅）按扩展名、体积上限与整名收在词库 `resources/junk/promo_attachment_names.json`，由 `junk._promo_name_reason` 读取；命中只计复核档 `JUNK_REVIEW_SCORE`，未探测时长视频的推广目录、推广创作者位与体积证据同样只到复核档，这类分数单独凑不到 `trash_junk.py` 默认的自动移入回收站门槛 `AUTO_TRASH_SCORE`，不凭名称移入回收站或删除。永久删除仍走 `trash_junk.py --purge --review-csv` 和 `purge_assets`。

极短视频附件复用同一垃圾复核评分、`promo_free_key` 的名称比较和 `release_code_from_filename` 的自身番号识别。发行目录须有与目录同名、至少 20 分钟且至少 120 MiB 的活动物理正片；附件须有有效测量值、不超过 30 秒与 32 MiB，正片至少为附件时长的 40 倍、体积的 10 倍。同目录至少 3 条此类附件，或相同体积、毫秒时长与有效分辨率的附件分布在至少 3 个发行目录，才计结构证据。真实创作者归属、自身番号、分卷、独立短动画与普通混合目录受保护；目录继承的番号不能证明附件身份。结构证据只够复核，短、小或同测量值都不能单独证明是广告，默认自动隔离线不接收仅由这些证据凑分的候选。查询只读账本，不读取云盘媒体，不新增依赖。

带站点水印的文件名先核对自身番号与分卷标记，明确分卷排除在垃圾队列外；「方括号来源站名 + 序号」视频只计复核证据，避免把短片的打包水印当作广告。画面取证可复用已有 FFmpeg 抽帧与 `scripts/windows_ocr.ps1`；本机复核先使用缓存缩略图。现有 `opencv-python-headless==5.0.0.93`（Apache-2.0，vision 可选组）提供 [QRCodeDetector](https://docs.opencv.org/4.7.0/de/dc3/classcv_1_1QRCodeDetector.html)，只能定位或解码二维码，不能证明整片是广告；[Windows OcrEngine](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/uwp/api/windows.media.ocr.ocrengine.trycreatefromlanguage) 依赖 Windows 与已安装的识别语言。两者均不放进列表请求的同步评分路径，避免平台依赖、图片读取及文字识别失败影响复核队列；内容结论写入仓库外的逐文件复核产物。

Ordinary work images and `images.rar` are kept. Deleting small files over CloudDrive WebDAV reuses the native `Path.unlink`: when a same-directory quarantine rename reports WinError 50, files up to 1 MiB are first fully backed up to `generated/purge-staging/` with the standard-library `shutil.copyfile`, recording the original path, size, and SHA256, and then deleted. If the database fails the backup is restored, and retired after commit; large files continue to report a block. This path adds no dependency, and backup and restore are managed only by `purge_assets`.

English media filename tokenization reuses `wordninja-enhanced==3.2.0` (MIT, released 2026-09-04, Python ≥3.9, pure Python, 11.4 MB wheel, no transitive dependencies). The source was read through `opensrc path pypi:wordninja-enhanced@3.2.0`; the original wordninja is older in release, and the enhanced version offers punctuation preservation and a supplementable dictionary. `space_media_names.py` first produces a per-file review CSV, inserts only spaces, keeps ordinals, characters, and extensions, and infers no person or provenance; real files in the specified directory are verified and then reuse `organize.apply_plan`, the SQLite backup, and the rollback log. Domain vocabulary is in `english_filename_words.txt` (pinned with extra weight, entries should be few: each extra entry lowers high-frequency words such as `a` and `of`), and concatenated strings often mistaken for words are moved out of the word-frequency table in `english_filename_nonwords.txt`. Apostrophe contractions, all-caps abbreviations, domains and site numbers, and hexadecimal viewkeys are not split; Cyrillic look-alike letters count as Latin letters for split points while the original characters are kept. Executed batches use `--recompute-batch <log>` to recompute the review CSV by original names and then go through `--apply --review-csv`.

Junk quarantine on the A drive uses the short name `peach-purge-<32-digit UUID>.peach-quarantine`, and the scan entry skips only this exact format; ordinary media and other `.tmp` files still need review. Quarantine restore and the full local backup keep the transaction boundary of `purge_assets`.

Original-file forensics for A-drive promotional attachments reuses CloudDrive's official `GetDownloadUrlPath` local original-file proxy, without going through Peach transcoding and without passing authentication to other sources; the actual QR-code copies and video forensic frames are in the library review output.

Keeping in-house is not a permanent exemption: when constraints change or candidate implementations are updated, rerun the POC, and this table's conclusions are not a reason to skip an external search.

## Settled product behavior

Each entry is a criterion left behind by one acceptance, one sentence each. Changing these behaviors is a product decision, and reimplementing them as written is
duplicated effort, so confirm here before starting. What the README, the [development conventions](../AGENTS.md), [docs/OPERATIONS.md](OPERATIONS.md),
and the ADRs already state is not repeated here; find provenance with `git log -S`.

- 本地浏览器直放 MP4/WebM/Ogg，其余容器由 `TranscodeService` 按六秒片段缓存成 H.264/AAC MP4，不改写原媒体；ffprobe 判定可复制的流不重编码，其余在 Windows 走 CUDA/NVDEC。
- 远端 MP4 默认走标准 Range，显式开启的 HLS 使用关键帧对齐片段并在失败时回退 Range。
- 页面共用 SPA、JSON 与 gzip/ETag；侧栏随当前视频集合，已保存在线作品复用关注详情。
- Logo、侧栏「首页」和沉浸模式关闭统一清除分类、搜索与 JAV 筛选，首页默认稳定随机、换批才换种子，再点当前排序回到随机。
- 高亮、竖屏密度、索引骨架已验桌面/390×844，HTTPS 生效；手机命中区 44 px。
- 主题三选一（跟随系统／浅色／深色），只存本机，首帧前由内联脚本写进 `<html>` 的 `data-theme`。
- 同番号的分卷派生（A/B、1/2、CD/Disc/DVD/Part/Vol、「首卷裸名 + 后续卷 `-2`/`-3`」）折叠成一张卡并按时长排除完整版，首页、搜索、资料页网格、版次队列与角标计数共用这套判定。卷号后挂着尾缀时先剥掉组内每个文件名都带、且从分隔符起头的那一段，不按版次词表拆（`1080p` 会被拆成 `10` 加 `80p`）。
- 番号提取复用 `catalog_rules` 的视频容器后缀、日期式番号和 KIRARI 形态：扫描支持的媒体与图片后缀均可剥除，明确卷标后的两位序号保留，日期式序号接受 2～4 位并保留原分隔符。`MKBD-S`／`MKD-S` 共用文本、文件名、自定义规则、无码来源路由和标题与版次投影判据；目录预检、配套资料和资料采集均调用同一解析器。运行时只用标准库 `re`，不引入外部解析器；[NeoAVDC](https://github.com/haoo999/NeoAVDC) 0.0.4（MIT）的 TypeScript 解析器只作边界对照，其全串宽松提取不适用 Peach 的创作者账号与相机编号保护。
- 分卷卡与版次卡（有码／中字／无码）不翻卡（各卷共用同一封套，翻了像卡住），悬浮走分段视频预览；封面只有一个计数（「N 卷」或「N 个版本」），叠层封面格垫 `--page` 挡住模糊衬底的半透明边。分卷详情标题带卷号，因为各卷标题、女优、厂牌逐字相同。
- 本地播放版本复用 `catalog_rules` 的番号、版次、卷号与重复文件判断。`catalog_versions` 先区分版次、探测规格与修复标记，再识别各版分卷；完整文件与分卷同时存在时核对总时长，重复副本保留文件并共用一个版本身份。计数、首页、资料页、详情队列和相关推荐读取同一查询投影，ledger 不变。`scripts/audit_catalog_versions.py` 只读输出全库复核 CSV；没有时长、规格或明确番号证据的关系保留待复核。
  - 文件身份复用 `metadata.identifies_code` 核对 DMM 数字前缀与补零写法；通用文件名须同时有带归属的刮削标题和匹配的番号目录，显式文件号冲突优先拒绝。同名副本的已知时长相差超过 10% 时保持独立，缺测量项不附到有时长冲突的同名组。来源目录时长保留为证据，不代填媒体实测时长；特典、广告与缺内容的取舍记录在逐文件复核产物里。
- [Jellyfin 的多版本命名](https://jellyfin.org/docs/general/server/media/movies/#multiple-versions)以共同作品名和版本后缀区分文件（2026-10-10 核对）；其整套服务器与严格目录前缀不能直接承接 Peach 的 FC2 短番号、分卷合集和跨盘资产。这里只参照作品、版本与文件的分层，不复制 GPL 源码、不引入依赖。最小实证使用 `FC2-PPV-1110408` 的完整文件和两段 4K 修复文件：两个播放版本、三个文件、同版独立分卷队列。
- 合集翻图复用 `use-stack-flip`（`frontend/src/react/components/`）：逐张解码后才显示，失败帧不入队，悬停代际隔离异步结果，退出清理计时器。馆藏与关注详情共用原生 `dialog#stage`，浮窗不参与列表排版，关闭保留关注列表岛；图片灯箱沿用现有实现。
- 关注卡翻卡去重：`follow_faces` 复用 `community_catalog.fingerprint` 的 dHash，另加 8×8 RGB 色块（dHash 分不开同姿势的穿衣版与 nude 版）。阈值的实测依据见 [复用取证记录](SOURCING.md#媒体与关注实测)。
  - 地址相同即同一张；时长都已知且差 ≤1 秒时 dHash ≤6、最大格差 ≤4；时长未知时 dHash ≤2、格差 ≤3。
  - 翻卡按画面去重、不分站，封面计数把跨站同一画面并成一个媒体，并完只剩一个时写「N 个来源」；同站不按画面合并，4K／8K 两个文件和局部差分会混进来。
  - 文件内容哈希相同即同一个媒体，不论同站跨站、不看 alt／WIP 标签：连接器的 `content_hash` 从已存原始地址解析、不发请求（kemono／coomer／pawchive `/data/<h0h1>/<h2h3>/<sha256>`，rule34.xxx `/images/<目录>/<md5>`，paheal `r34i.paheal-cdn.net/<h0h1>/<h2h3>/<md5>`）；fanbox、rule34video、f95zone 的文件名是随机 id 或签名令牌，不参与。
  - 同站相同 `external_id` 与媒体序号标识一份媒体，归档站同时核对服务与作者范围；多个关注来源重复收录只增加收录份数。已知文件哈希不同、不同帖子或不同媒体序号各自计数。
  - 签名由后台线程补进 `generated/posters/follow-faces/`，不进 ledger。
- 关注视频规格复用 `follow_variants`：`4K60fps`、`1080p60fps` 与带空格的规格拆成清晰度、帧率两个版本标记；存量作品键在读取时投影，无需重抓或改写 ledger。
- Rule34Video 封面复用 `FollowCoverService`、HTTPX 与 Pillow，经 `/follow-cover` 缓存静态 poster。自身图片失效时只在当前作品组内寻找其他版本的封面，沿用来源白名单、请求字节上限、并发与缓存锁。该路径不解析正片、不抽帧；首页、作者筛选、详情队列与已保存在线卡片共用缩略图投影。复核记录见 `<私有验证记录>`（仓库外）。
- F95 讨论图片依据已确认的附件身份在采集与读取投影中排除。网盘资源保留，未取得作品预览时显示资源服务图标；不能仅凭 GIF 扩展名过滤作品。
- 普通多女优卡片叠放前 3 个头像、只显示第一位姓名和真实总人数；JAV 小图是整页版式，混入的非番号作品统一为标题、身份、标签三行固定高度。
- JAV 详情持有 `asset.catalog_title`／`original_title` 与官方 Tag 身份，官方标记用常规字重，身份区收齐到同一内容起点。
- JAV 官方封面重探只在面积更大时替换，失败保留原图。
- 播放器按 YouTube 锁定源码对齐控件、状态、图标形变、设置面板动画与悬停提示，倍速五格到 3.0，窄屏按播放器宽度折叠留黑边，沉浸模式按 Shorts 版式，竖屏用居中 9:16 舞台，播放统计两页共用、按传输方式分口径。参考快照在 `docs/reference-snapshots/youtube-*.md`，索引见 [开发约定](../AGENTS.md)；只复用可测量的层级、尺寸和状态语义。
- 图片灯箱在本地照片、番号样张和关注在线图片间复用同一套 Swiper，样张与在线图片只显示来源、合集序号和浏览器实际解析结果；照片标签进入分页图片墙，点开才按原图比例呈现。番号集卡复用播放列表卡的叠层纸边、`mixbadge` 张数与 `mixcopy` 两行字，封面走视频卡同一份 `coverImage`（ADR-0068）。
- 资料页照片与关注在线图片两处图片墙共用大小（顶部按钮）与固定比例／瀑布流（筛选浮层）设置；关注图片的「仅显示图片」独立保存，隐藏卡片文字与角标。瀑布流用 CSS 多栏、`break-inside:avoid` 与图片 `aspect-ratio:auto 1`，懒加载前留非零高度（[MDN](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/CSS/Reference/Properties/aspect-ratio)）；`alt` 留给读屏，字色透明。
- 缓存型资产路由先问缓存，不先解析源文件：`media_engine.file_for()` 的存在性检查落在 CloudDrive 网盘上一次 137–402 毫秒。`/photo-thumb` 先问 `photo_service.cached()`，`/sample-thumb` 先问 `SampleCache.cached()`，缓存根从 `photo_root` 推出。
- 远程媒体持久缓存复用 HTTPX 0.28.1 的流式响应、Starlette 1.7.0 的文件 Range、filelock 4.0.12 和 `fsutil.atomic_path`；缓存身份、失败保留与按需取得由 Peach 管理。HLS 清单解析用 [m3u8 6.0.0](https://pypi.org/project/m3u8/)（MIT，Python ≥3.7，纯 Python、24.1 KB wheel、无传递依赖），已读取固定版 `model.py` 并验证嵌套清单、密钥、初始化段和字节范围。Hishel 1.4.0（BSD-3-Clause）已读取 `_sync_cache.py` 与 Range 判据，通用 HTTP 缓存按来源 URL、缓存头和重新验证工作；此处需按稳定条目身份避开签名 URL 更新、永久保留成功媒体，因此复用现有持久文件路径。规则及验收见 [媒体缓存](MEDIA_CACHE.md)。
- 账本路径在 Windows 只做 `abspath`，不 `resolve()`：PikPak 的 A: 是 WinFsp 网络驱动器，`resolve()` 得到 UNC 形态，之后每个经 `file_for()` 的播放请求都慢几个数量级；115 的 B: 不受影响。
